	"""Remove a tag from a document."""
	frappe.has_permission(doctype, "write", throw=True)
	from frappe.desk.doctype.tag.tag import remove_tag
	return remove_tag(tag, doctype, docname)


# ── CT Modal — child table row fetch ──────────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_child_table_rows(doctype: str, docname: str, table_fieldname: str) -> list:
	"""Return all rows of a child table from a parent document.

	Used by the Excel View CT cell modal to display the full child table
	without loading the entire parent form.

	Security:
	  - Caller must have READ permission on the parent DocType.
	  - ``table_fieldname`` must be a Table-type field on that DocType.

	Returns a list of dicts (one per child row), excluding internal Frappe
	meta-fields (_assign, _comments, etc.).
	"""
	frappe.has_permission(doctype, "read", throw=True)

	# Validate that table_fieldname is actually a Table field on this DocType
	parent_meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
	table_df = next(
		(f for f in parent_meta.fields if f.fieldname == table_fieldname and f.fieldtype == "Table"),
		None,
	)
	if not table_df:
		frappe.throw(_("'{0}' is not a Table field on {1}").format(table_fieldname, doctype))

	child_doctype = table_df.options
	frappe.has_permission(child_doctype, "read", throw=True)

	# Fetch child rows — only fields that have a backing DB column.
	# frappe.model.no_value_fields covers all UI-only types (Image, HTML,
	# Section Break, Table, etc.) that Frappe never writes to the database.
	from frappe.model import no_value_fields as _no_value_types

	child_meta = frappe.get_meta(child_doctype)
	data_fields = [
		f.fieldname
		for f in child_meta.fields
		if f.fieldtype not in _no_value_types and not getattr(f, "is_virtual", 0)
	]
	# Always include idx and name for row identification
	fields = list({"name", "idx", *data_fields})

	# Cross-check against actual DB columns to guard against any edge cases
	# (renamed fields, migration gaps, etc.) that could cause SQL errors.
	try:
		actual_cols = set(frappe.db.get_table_columns(f"tab{child_doctype}"))
		fields = [f for f in fields if f in actual_cols]
	except Exception:
		pass  # If column introspection fails, proceed with meta-derived list

	rows = frappe.get_all(
		child_doctype,
		filters={"parent": docname, "parenttype": doctype, "parentfield": table_fieldname},
		fields=fields,
		order_by="idx asc",
		limit=500,
	)
	return rows


@frappe.whitelist()
def save_child_table_rows(doctype: str, docname: str, table_fieldname: str, rows: str) -> bool:
	"""Replace a child table on a document with the supplied rows and save.

	Rows that carry their original ``name`` are updated in place; rows without
	a ``name`` (or with a HOT-generated local name) are treated as new inserts.
	Rows previously in the table but absent from ``rows`` are deleted.
	"""
	frappe.has_permission(doctype, "write", throw=True)

	parent_meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
	table_df = next(
		(f for f in parent_meta.fields if f.fieldname == table_fieldname and f.fieldtype == "Table"),
		None,
	)
	if not table_df:
		frappe.throw(_("'{0}' is not a Table field on {1}").format(table_fieldname, doctype))

	parsed = frappe.parse_json(rows) if isinstance(rows, str) else rows

	# Strip HOT/client-side internals; remove local names so Frappe auto-assigns them
	clean_rows = []
	for row in parsed:
		clean = {k: v for k, v in row.items() if not k.startswith("__")}
		name = clean.get("name", "")
		if not name or (isinstance(name, str) and name.startswith("new-")):
			clean.pop("name", None)
		clean_rows.append(clean)

	doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
	doc.check_permission("write")
	doc.set(table_fieldname, clean_rows)
	doc.save(ignore_permissions=False)
	return True



@frappe.whitelist()
def bulk_set_value(doctype: str, updates: str) -> dict:
    """
    Update multiple existing records via the full Frappe ORM (doc.save()).

    Every write goes through validate, before_save, after_save, on_change and
    all controller methods — identical to saving from the Frappe form UI.
    doc.save() calls notify_update() internally, so realtime list_update events
    are queued automatically and fired on the final commit.

    Submitted documents are rejected with a clear error; use the amend workflow.

    updates: JSON array of {name: str, fields: {fieldname: value, ...}}
    Returns {"errors": [{"name": ..., "error": ...}, ...]} — empty list = all OK.
    """
    frappe.has_permission(doctype, "write", throw=True)

    updates_data: list[dict] = frappe.parse_json(updates)
    errors: list[dict] = []
    any_saved = False

    for item in updates_data:
        name   = item.get("name")
        fields = item.get("fields") or {}
        if not name or not fields:
            continue
        try:
            doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, name)
            for fieldname, value in fields.items():
                doc.set(fieldname, value)
            # doc.save() enforces all Frappe rules natively:
            # - submitted docs: only allow_on_submit fields may be changed
            # - cancelled docs: no edits allowed
            # - validate, before_save, after_save hooks all run
            doc.save()
            any_saved = True
        except Exception as exc:
            errors.append({"name": name, "error": str(exc)})

    if any_saved:
        # Explicit commit flushes all after_commit=True realtime events
        # queued by doc.save() → notify_update() in the loop above.
        frappe.db.commit()

    return {"errors": errors}


@frappe.whitelist()
def bulk_create_records(doctype: str, rows: str) -> dict:
    """
    Create multiple new Frappe documents from a JSON array of field-value dicts.

    Args:
        doctype: Target DocType, e.g. "Customer"
        rows:    JSON string — [{fieldname: value, ...}, ...]
                 Empty-string values are skipped (treated as unset).

    Returns:
        {
            "created": [{"idx": 0, "name": "CUST-001"}, ...],
            "errors":  [{"idx": 1, "message": "Customer Name is required"}, ...]
        }

    Security: caller must have Create permission on the DocType.
    Each document is saved with the caller's permissions (no ignore_permissions).
    """
    frappe.has_permission(doctype, "create", throw=True)

    rows_data: list[dict] = frappe.parse_json(rows)
    created: list[dict]   = []
    errors:  list[dict]   = []

    for idx, row in enumerate(rows_data):
        try:
            doc = frappe.new_doc(doctype)
            for field, value in row.items():
                # Skip empty values — let Frappe apply its own defaults
                if value is not None and value != "":
                    doc.set(field, value)
            doc.insert()
            created.append({"idx": idx, "name": doc.name})
        except Exception as exc:
            errors.append({"idx": idx, "message": str(exc)})

    if created:
        frappe.db.commit()

    return {"created": created, "errors": errors}


# ── Permission Panel API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_PERM_BLOCKED_DOCTYPES = frozenset({
	"DocType", "DocField", "DocPerm", "Custom DocPerm",
	"Property Setter", "Role", "User", "Session",
	"DefaultValue", "Error Log", "Access Log",
})


def _perm_guard(doctype):
	"""Block meta-system doctypes from being managed via the permission panel."""
	if doctype in _PERM_BLOCKED_DOCTYPES:
		frappe.throw(
			f"Permissions for system DocType '{frappe.bold(doctype)}' cannot be "
			"managed from Excel View.",
			frappe.PermissionError,
		)
	frappe.only_for("System Manager")


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doctype_permissions(doctype):
	"""Return active permission rows for the DocType."""
	_perm_guard(doctype)
	from frappe.core.page.permission_manager import permission_manager as pm
	return pm.get_permissions(doctype=doctype)


@frappe.whitelist(methods=["POST"])
def update_role_permission(doctype, role, permlevel, ptype, value=None, if_owner=0):
	"""Toggle a single permission bit."""
	_perm_guard(doctype)
	from frappe.core.page.permission_manager import permission_manager as pm
	return pm.update(
		doctype=doctype,
		role=role,
		permlevel=frappe.utils.cint(permlevel),
		ptype=ptype,
		value=value,
		if_owner=if_owner,
	)


@frappe.whitelist(methods=["POST"])
def add_role_permission(doctype, role, permlevel=0):
	"""Add a new (role, permlevel) row."""
	_perm_guard(doctype)
	from frappe.permissions import add_permission
	add_permission(doctype, role, frappe.utils.cint(permlevel))


@frappe.whitelist(methods=["POST"])
def remove_role_permission(doctype, role, permlevel, if_owner=0):
	"""Remove a (role, permlevel) row."""
	_perm_guard(doctype)
	from frappe.core.page.permission_manager import permission_manager as pm
	pm.remove(
		doctype=doctype,
		role=role,
		permlevel=frappe.utils.cint(permlevel),
		if_owner=if_owner,
	)


@frappe.whitelist(methods=["POST"])
def reset_doctype_permissions(doctype):
	"""Delete all Custom DocPerm and revert to Standard DocPerm."""
	_perm_guard(doctype)
	from frappe.core.page.permission_manager import permission_manager as pm
	pm.reset(doctype=doctype)


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_field_permlevels(doctype):
	"""Return every data field with its current permlevel."""
	_perm_guard(doctype)
	SKIP_TYPES = frozenset({
		"Section Break", "Column Break", "Tab Break",
		"Fold", "Heading", "HTML", "Custom HTML", "Password",
		"Table", "Table MultiSelect",
	})
	meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
	return [
		{
			"fieldname": df.fieldname,
			"label":     df.label or df.fieldname,
			"fieldtype": df.fieldtype,
			"permlevel": frappe.utils.cint(df.permlevel),
		}
		for df in meta.fields
		if df.fieldtype not in SKIP_TYPES and df.fieldname and not df.is_virtual
	]


@frappe.whitelist()
def set_field_permlevel(doctype, fieldname, permlevel):
	"""
	Change a field's permlevel via Property Setter (upgrade-safe).
	frappe.make_property_setter creates/updates a Property Setter record and
	calls frappe.clear_cache(doctype) inside its validate() hook automatically.
	"""
	_perm_guard(doctype)
	permlevel = frappe.utils.cint(permlevel)
	if not 0 <= permlevel <= 9:
		frappe.throw("permlevel must be between 0 and 9.")

	# Validate field exists in this doctype
	meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
	if not meta.get_field(fieldname):
		frappe.throw(f"Field '{frappe.bold(fieldname)}' not found in {frappe.bold(doctype)}.")

	frappe.make_property_setter({
		"doctype":         doctype,
		"doctype_or_field": "DocField",
		"fieldname":       fieldname,
		"property":        "permlevel",
		"value":           permlevel,
		"property_type":   "Int",
	}, is_system_generated=False)

	# Belt-and-suspenders: schedule one more clear after commit
	frappe.db.after_commit.add(lambda: frappe.clear_cache(doctype=doctype))


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_role_user_counts():
	"""
	Return {role_name: user_count} for every role that has at least one user.
	Single aggregating query — O(1) round-trips regardless of role count.
	"""
	rows = frappe.db.sql(
		"SELECT role, COUNT(*) AS cnt"
		" FROM `tabHas Role`"
		" WHERE parenttype = 'User'"
		" GROUP BY role",
		as_dict=True,
	)
	return {r.role: r.cnt for r in rows}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_all_roles():
	"""
	Return sorted list of non-disabled, non-automatic role names.
	Mirrors permission_manager.get_roles_and_doctypes() role list.
	"""
	try:
		from frappe.permissions import AUTOMATIC_ROLES
		restricted = set(AUTOMATIC_ROLES)
	except ImportError:
		restricted = {"All", "Guest"}

	if frappe.session.user != "Administrator":
		custom_user_type_roles = frappe.get_all(
			"User Type", filters={"is_standard": 0}, fields=["role"], pluck="role"
		)
		restricted.update(custom_user_type_roles)

	restricted.add("Administrator")

	return frappe.get_all(
		"Role",
		filters={"disabled": 0, "name": ["not in", list(restricted)]},
		pluck="name",
		order_by="name asc",
	)


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_access_profiles():
	"""
	Single round-trip for the Access Profiles tab.
	4 lightweight queries merged in Python — O(profiles + assignments + modules).
	Returns all role profiles, module profiles, and installed module names.
	"""
	# ── Role Profiles ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	rp_rows = frappe.get_all("Role Profile", fields=["name"], order_by="name asc")

	if rp_rows:
		rp_counts = {
			r.role_profile_name: r.cnt
			for r in frappe.db.sql(
				"SELECT role_profile_name, COUNT(*) AS cnt"
				" FROM `tabUser`"
				" WHERE role_profile_name IS NOT NULL AND role_profile_name != ''"
				"   AND enabled = 1"
				" GROUP BY role_profile_name",
				as_dict=True,
			)
		}
		rp_role_map = {}
		for r in frappe.get_all(
			"Has Role",
			filters={"parenttype": "Role Profile"},
			fields=["parent", "role"],
		):
			rp_role_map.setdefault(r.parent, []).append(r.role)
	else:
		rp_counts, rp_role_map = {}, {}

	role_profiles = [
		{
			"name":       rp.name,
			"user_count": rp_counts.get(rp.name, 0),
			"roles":      rp_role_map.get(rp.name, []),
		}
		for rp in rp_rows
	]

	# ── Module Profiles ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	mp_rows = frappe.get_all("Module Profile", fields=["name"], order_by="name asc")

	if mp_rows:
		mp_counts = {
			r.module_profile: r.cnt
			for r in frappe.db.sql(
				"SELECT module_profile, COUNT(*) AS cnt"
				" FROM `tabUser`"
				" WHERE module_profile IS NOT NULL AND module_profile != ''"
				"   AND enabled = 1"
				" GROUP BY module_profile",
				as_dict=True,
			)
		}
		mp_mod_map = {}
		for r in frappe.get_all(
			"Block Module",
			filters={"parenttype": "Module Profile"},
			fields=["parent", "module"],
		):
			mp_mod_map.setdefault(r.parent, []).append(r.module)
	else:
		mp_counts, mp_mod_map = {}, {}

	module_profiles = [
		{
			"name":            mp.name,
			"user_count":      mp_counts.get(mp.name, 0),
			"blocked_modules": mp_mod_map.get(mp.name, []),
		}
		for mp in mp_rows
	]

	# ── All installed modules ──────────────────────────────────────────────
	from frappe.utils.modules import get_modules_from_all_apps
	all_modules = sorted(
		m["module_name"]
		for m in get_modules_from_all_apps()
		if m.get("module_name")
	)

	return {
		"role_profiles":   role_profiles,
		"module_profiles": module_profiles,
		"all_modules":     all_modules,
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def save_role_profile(profile_name, roles):
	"""
	Replace the full role list for a Role Profile in one ORM save.
	Frappe's on_update() queues user-sync automatically after commit.
	roles: JSON-encoded list of role name strings.
	"""
	import json as _json
	role_list = _json.loads(roles) if isinstance(roles, str) else list(roles)

	doc = frappe.get_doc("Role Profile", profile_name)
	doc.roles = []
	for role in role_list:
		doc.append("roles", {"role": role})
	doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)


@frappe.whitelist()
def save_module_profile(profile_name, blocked_modules):
	"""
	Replace the full blocked_modules list for a Module Profile in one ORM save.
	Frappe's on_update() queues user-sync automatically after commit.
	blocked_modules: JSON-encoded list of module name strings.
	"""
	import json as _json
	mod_list = _json.loads(blocked_modules) if isinstance(blocked_modules, str) else list(blocked_modules)

	doc = frappe.get_doc("Module Profile", profile_name)
	doc.block_modules = []
	for module in mod_list:
		doc.append("block_modules", {"module": module})
	doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_access_profile(profile_type, name):
	"""
	Create a new Role Profile or Module Profile.
	profile_type: "role" | "module"
	"""
	name = (name or "").strip()
	if not name:
		frappe.throw(_("Profile name cannot be empty."))

	if profile_type == "role":
		if frappe.db.exists("Role Profile", name):
			frappe.throw(_("Role Profile {0} already exists.").format(frappe.bold(name)))
		doc = frappe.new_doc("Role Profile")
		doc.role_profile = name
		doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
	elif profile_type == "module":
		if frappe.db.exists("Module Profile", name):
			frappe.throw(_("Module Profile {0} already exists.").format(frappe.bold(name)))
		doc = frappe.new_doc("Module Profile")
		doc.module_profile_name = name
		doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
	else:
		frappe.throw(_("Invalid profile_type."))

	return {"name": doc.name}


@frappe.whitelist()
def delete_access_profile(profile_type, name):
	"""Delete a Role Profile or Module Profile."""
	if profile_type == "role":
		frappe.delete_doc("Role Profile", name, ignore_permissions=True)
	elif profile_type == "module":
		frappe.delete_doc("Module Profile", name, ignore_permissions=True)
	else:
		frappe.throw(_("Invalid profile_type."))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/excel_view_tasks.py
"""
Scheduled background tasks for Excel View.
"""
import frappe


def hourly():
	"""Refresh cached schema graph, link-edge cache, and GenBI child table map."""
	try:
		# Bust child table map so it picks up any newly installed apps / DocTypes
		from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import _CHILD_TABLE_MAP_CACHE_KEY, _get_child_table_map
		frappe.cache().delete_value(_CHILD_TABLE_MAP_CACHE_KEY)
		_get_child_table_map()  # rebuild immediately
	except Exception:
		frappe.log_error(frappe.get_traceback(), "GenBI: hourly child table map refresh failed")

	try:
		# Bust link-edge cache so next call rebuilds it fresh
		frappe.cache().delete_value("ev_link_graph_edges_v3")
		# Rebuild immediately (warms the cache for this worker)
		from f_genbi.excel_view_api import _get_all_link_edges
		_get_all_link_edges()
	except Exception:
		frappe.log_error(frappe.get_traceback(), "Excel View: hourly edge cache refresh failed")

	try:
		# Refresh schema graph (used by Smart Lookup, find_related_doctypes)
		from f_genbi.excel_view_api import build_schema_graph
		build_schema_graph(force_refresh=True)
	except Exception:
		frappe.log_error(frappe.get_traceback(), "Excel View: hourly graph refresh failed")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/api/query_engine.py
import hashlib
import json
import re
import time

import frappe
from frappe import _

from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.schema_builder import get_relevant_schema, get_schema_for_doctypes


# ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# Public API
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def process_question(question, chat_history=None, session_id=None):
	"""Main endpoint: natural language question -> data + visualization config."""
	settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")

	if not settings.enabled:
		return {"success": False, "error": _("GenBI is not enabled. Please enable it in GenBI Settings.")}

	# Derive a stable session_id so the same user picks up the same session today.
	# The frontend may pass an explicit session_id (e.g. for the history sidebar).
	if not session_id:
		session_id = f"{frappe.session.user}:{frappe.utils.today()}"

	# Task 4.1: Route forecast/prediction questions before LLM SQL generation
	_FORECAST_PAT = re.compile(
		r"\b(forecast|predict|projection|project|what\s+will|future|next\s+\d+\s*(month|week|quarter|year))\b",
		re.I,
	)
	if _FORECAST_PAT.search(question):
		try:
			from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.forecaster import forecast as _forecast
			result = _forecast(question)
			if result.get("success"):
				result["session_id"] = session_id
				result["engine"] = "forecast"
				result["sql"] = None
				result["result_count"] = len(result.get("data") or [])
				result["from_cache"] = False
				_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, result)
				return result
		except Exception:
			pass  # fall through to normal pipeline

	# Task 4.6: Route explain-mode questions before any SQL generation
	_EXPLAIN_PAT = re.compile(
		r"^\s*(explain|what\s+(is|does|means?|are)|why\s+(is|are|does)|"
		r"how\s+(is|are|does|do)|what.*mean|define|describe|tell\s+me\s+about\s+the\s+(term|field|concept)|"
		r"normal\s+range|typical\s+value|what.*relationship|how.*related)",
		re.I,
	)
	if _EXPLAIN_PAT.match(question):
		try:
			from f_genbi.genbi.explain_mode import explain as _explain
			result = _explain(question)
			if result.get("success"):
				result["session_id"] = session_id
				result["engine"] = "explain"
				result["sql"] = None
				result["data"] = []
				result["chart_config"] = None
				result["result_count"] = 0
				_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, result)
				return result
		except Exception:
			pass  # fall through to normal pipeline

	# Task 1.1: Route to No-LLM Local Engine when no LLM provider is configured
	if settings.llm_provider == "None (Local NLP Engine)":
		from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.local_engine import LocalNLPEngine
		history_list = []
		if chat_history:
			try:
				history_list = json.loads(chat_history) if isinstance(chat_history, str) else chat_history
			except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
				history_list = []
		result = LocalNLPEngine().process(question, history_list)
		if result.get("success"):
			_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, result)
			result["session_id"] = session_id
		return result

	# Phase 5.0: Route to Neural Engine (offline, no LLM API)
	if settings.llm_provider in ("Neural (Local)", "Neural + RPG Boost", "Neural + Lightning"):
		from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_engine import NeuralEngine
		history_list = []
		if chat_history:
			try:
				history_list = json.loads(chat_history) if isinstance(chat_history, str) else chat_history
			except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
				history_list = []
		result = NeuralEngine().process(question, history_list)
		result["session_id"] = session_id
		if result.get("success"):
			_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, result)
		return result

	# Parse chat history from JSON string
	history = []
	if chat_history:
		try:
			history = json.loads(chat_history) if isinstance(chat_history, str) else chat_history
		except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
			history = []

	start_time = time.time()
	query_plan = None

	try:
		# Step 1: resolve entities then get relevant schema
		full_context = question
		if history:
			recent_questions = [h.get("question", "") for h in history[-3:]]
			full_context = " ".join(recent_questions) + " " + question
		schema = _get_schema_with_entity_resolution(question, full_context)

		# Step 1b: pre-query feasibility check (Task 1.4)
		feasibility = _check_query_feasibility(schema)
		empty = feasibility["empty_tables"]
		detailed_keys = [d for d in (schema or {}).get("detailed_schema", {}).keys()
		                 if d not in ("DocType", "DocField")]
		if empty and detailed_keys and set(empty) >= set(detailed_keys):
			# ALL matched tables are empty — short-circuit before LLM call
			names = ", ".join(empty)
			return {
				"success": True,
				"question": question,
				"explanation": f"The table(s) {names} have no records yet. Nothing to show.",
				"sql": None,
				"data": [],
				"chart_config": None,
				"result_count": 0,
				"execution_time": round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
				"from_cache": False,
			}

		# Task 4.7: resolve user's company once for this request
		user_company = _get_user_company_filter()

		# Step 2: convert question to SQL via LLM (with conversation history)
		query_plan = _convert_to_sql(question, schema, settings, history,
		                             data_warnings=feasibility["warning"],
		                             company_filter=user_company)

		# Step 2b: Handle chat-only responses (no SQL needed)
		if query_plan.get("type") == "chat":
			elapsed = round(time.time() - start_time, 3)
			return {
				"success": True,
				"question": question,
				"explanation": query_plan.get("answer", ""),
				"sql": None,
				"data": [],
				"chart_config": None,
				"result_count": 0,
				"execution_time": elapsed,
			}

		# Step 3: sanitize, validate, and execute
		sql = _sanitize_sql(query_plan.get("sql", ""))
		# Task 4.7: ensure company filter is present (safety net in case LLM omitted it)
		sql = _inject_company_filter(sql, user_company)
		# 10.1/10.2: permission injection and PII masking
		_primary_dt = _primary_doctype_from_sql(sql)
		sql = _inject_permission_filters(sql, _primary_dt)
		sql = _apply_pii_masking(sql)
		_validate_sql(sql)

		max_rows = settings.max_query_rows or 500
		results, from_cache = _execute_query(sql, max_rows)

		# Step 3b: auto-fetch child items if user asked about items but LLM only queried parent
		child_items = _auto_fetch_child_items(sql, results, question)

		# Step 4: build visualization config
		# If child items were auto-fetched, show them as the table (more relevant than parent-only data)
		if child_items:
			viz_config = _prepare_visualization(child_items, {
				"chart_type": "table",
				"explanation": query_plan.get("explanation", ""),
			})
		else:
			viz_config = _prepare_visualization(results, query_plan)

		elapsed = round(time.time() - start_time, 3)

		# Step 5: generate conversational summary of actual results (including child items)
		answer = _summarize_results(question, results, query_plan, settings, child_items=child_items)

		# Log the query
		if settings.enable_query_logging:
			_log_query(
				question=question,
				sql=sql,
				chart_type=query_plan.get("chart_type", "table"),
				explanation=answer,
				status="Success",
				result_count=len(results),
				execution_time=elapsed,
			)

		response = {
			"success": True,
			"question": question,
			"explanation": answer,
			"sql": sql,
			"data": results,
			"chart_config": viz_config,
			"result_count": len(results),
			"execution_time": elapsed,
			"from_cache": from_cache,
			"session_id": session_id,
		}
		_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, response)
		return response

	except Exception as e:
		elapsed = round(time.time() - start_time, 3)
		error_msg = str(e)

		if settings.enable_query_logging:
			_log_query(
				question=question,
				sql=query_plan.get("sql", "") if query_plan else "",
				status="Failed",
				error_message=error_msg,
				execution_time=elapsed,
			)

		return {
			"success": False,
			"error": error_msg,
			"suggestion": _get_error_suggestion(error_msg),
		}


@frappe.whitelist()
def stream_question(question, chat_history=None, session_id=None, stream_id=None):
	"""Task 2.1: Streaming variant of process_question.

	Emits `frappe.publish_realtime` events at each pipeline stage so the
	frontend can show progressive status updates instead of a blank spinner.

	Event envelope: {"event": "<stage>", ...stage-specific fields...}
	Stages (in order):
	  schema_ready  — entity resolution done; relevant DocTypes identified
	  sql_ready     — LLM produced SQL; client can show a preview
	  data_ready    — SQL executed; row count known
	  done          — full result payload (same shape as process_question response)
	  error         — something failed

	Falls back gracefully: if realtime is unavailable the frontend uses
	process_question instead, so this endpoint never needs to be critical path.
	"""
	user = frappe.session.user

	def _emit(stage, data=None):
		payload = {"event": stage}
		if data:
			payload.update(data)
		frappe.publish_realtime(
			f"genbi_stream_{stream_id}",
			payload,
			user=user,
			after_commit=False,
		)

	settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")

	if not settings.enabled:
		_emit("error", {"error": _("GenBI is not enabled. Please enable it in GenBI Settings.")})
		return {"success": False}

	if not session_id:
		session_id = f"{user}:{frappe.utils.today()}"

	# ── Local engine path (no LLM) ────────────────────────────────────────────
	if settings.llm_provider == "None (Local NLP Engine)":
		from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.local_engine import LocalNLPEngine
		history_list = []
		if chat_history:
			try:
				history_list = json.loads(chat_history) if isinstance(chat_history, str) else chat_history
			except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
				history_list = []
		result = LocalNLPEngine().process(question, history_list)
		if result.get("success"):
			_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, result)
			result["session_id"] = session_id
			_emit("done", result)
		else:
			_emit("error", {"error": result.get("error", "Local engine error")})
		return {"success": True}

	# ── Neural engine path (offline, no LLM API) ──────────────────────────────
	if settings.llm_provider in ("Neural (Local)", "Neural + RPG Boost", "Neural + Lightning"):
		from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_engine import NeuralEngine
		history_list = []
		if chat_history:
			try:
				history_list = json.loads(chat_history) if isinstance(chat_history, str) else chat_history
			except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
				history_list = []
		for event in NeuralEngine().stream(question, history_list):
			_emit(event.pop("event"), event)
			if event.get("success") is not None:
				# "done" or "error" — persist and finish
				if event.get("success"):
					event["session_id"] = session_id
					_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, event)
		return {"success": True}

	# Task 4.1: forecast shortcut (before LLM)
	_FORECAST_PAT_S = re.compile(
		r"\b(forecast|predict|projection|project|what\s+will|future|next\s+\d+\s*(month|week|quarter|year))\b",
		re.I,
	)
	if _FORECAST_PAT_S.search(question):
		try:
			from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.forecaster import forecast as _forecast_s
			fcast = _forecast_s(question)
			if fcast.get("success"):
				fcast["session_id"] = session_id
				fcast["engine"] = "forecast"
				fcast["sql"] = None
				fcast["result_count"] = len(fcast.get("data") or [])
				fcast["from_cache"] = False
				_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, fcast)
				_emit("done", fcast)
				return {"success": True}
		except Exception:
			pass  # fall through to normal LLM path

	# ── LLM path ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	history = []
	if chat_history:
		try:
			history = json.loads(chat_history) if isinstance(chat_history, str) else chat_history
		except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
			history = []

	start_time = time.time()
	query_plan = None

	try:
		# Step 1: entity resolution + schema
		full_context = question
		if history:
			recent_questions = [h.get("question", "") for h in history[-3:]]
			full_context = " ".join(recent_questions) + " " + question
		schema = _get_schema_with_entity_resolution(question, full_context)

		doctypes = list((schema or {}).get("detailed_schema", {}).keys())
		doctypes = [d for d in doctypes if d not in ("DocType", "DocField")]
		_emit("schema_ready", {"doctypes": doctypes[:6]})

		# Step 1b: feasibility check
		feasibility = _check_query_feasibility(schema)
		empty = feasibility["empty_tables"]
		detailed_keys = [d for d in (schema or {}).get("detailed_schema", {}).keys()
		                 if d not in ("DocType", "DocField")]
		if empty and detailed_keys and set(empty) >= set(detailed_keys):
			names = ", ".join(empty)
			result = {
				"success": True,
				"question": question,
				"explanation": f"The table(s) {names} have no records yet. Nothing to show.",
				"sql": None, "data": [], "chart_config": None,
				"result_count": 0,
				"execution_time": round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
				"from_cache": False, "session_id": session_id,
			}
			_emit("done", result)
			return {"success": True}

		# Task 4.7: resolve user's company once for this request
		user_company = _get_user_company_filter()

		# Step 2: LLM SQL generation
		query_plan = _convert_to_sql(question, schema, settings, history,
		                             data_warnings=feasibility["warning"],
		                             company_filter=user_company)

		if query_plan.get("type") == "chat":
			elapsed = round(time.time() - start_time, 3)
			result = {
				"success": True,
				"question": question,
				"explanation": query_plan.get("answer", ""),
				"sql": None, "data": [], "chart_config": None,
				"result_count": 0, "execution_time": elapsed,
				"session_id": session_id,
			}
			_emit("done", result)
			return {"success": True}

		sql = _sanitize_sql(query_plan.get("sql", ""))
		# Task 4.7: ensure company filter is present (safety net in case LLM omitted it)
		sql = _inject_company_filter(sql, user_company)
		# 10.1/10.2: permission injection and PII masking
		_primary_dt = _primary_doctype_from_sql(sql)
		sql = _inject_permission_filters(sql, _primary_dt)
		sql = _apply_pii_masking(sql)
		_validate_sql(sql)
		_emit("sql_ready", {"sql": sql})

		# Step 3: execute query
		max_rows = settings.max_query_rows or 500
		results, from_cache = _execute_query(sql, max_rows)
		_emit("data_ready", {"result_count": len(results), "from_cache": from_cache})

		# Step 3b: auto-fetch child items
		child_items = _auto_fetch_child_items(sql, results, question)

		# Step 4: visualization
		if child_items:
			viz_config = _prepare_visualization(child_items, {
				"chart_type": "table",
				"explanation": query_plan.get("explanation", ""),
			})
		else:
			viz_config = _prepare_visualization(results, query_plan)

		elapsed = round(time.time() - start_time, 3)

		# Step 5: conversational summary
		answer = _summarize_results(question, results, query_plan, settings, child_items=child_items)

		if settings.enable_query_logging:
			_log_query(
				question=question,
				sql=sql,
				chart_type=query_plan.get("chart_type", "table"),
				explanation=answer,
				status="Success",
				result_count=len(results),
				execution_time=elapsed,
			)

		response = {
			"success": True,
			"question": question,
			"explanation": answer,
			"sql": sql,
			"data": results,
			"chart_config": viz_config,
			"result_count": len(results),
			"execution_time": elapsed,
			"from_cache": from_cache,
			"session_id": session_id,
		}
		_persist_conversation_turn(session_id, question, response)
		_emit("done", response)
		return {"success": True}

	except Exception as e:
		elapsed = round(time.time() - start_time, 3)
		error_msg = str(e)
		if settings.enable_query_logging:
			_log_query(
				question=question,
				sql=query_plan.get("sql", "") if query_plan else "",
				status="Failed",
				error_message=error_msg,
				execution_time=elapsed,
			)
		_emit("error", {"error": error_msg, "suggestion": _get_error_suggestion(error_msg)})
		return {"success": False}


@frappe.whitelist()
def parse_nl_filters(doctype, text):
	"""Task 3.4: Parse a plain-English filter phrase into Frappe filter tuples.

	Returns a list of [field, operator, value] triples that frappe.ui.filter_area
	can consume directly.

	Examples:
	  "status is Submitted"               → [["status", "=", "Submitted"]]
	  "grand_total > 10000"               → [["grand_total", ">", "10000"]]
	  "created last 7 days"               → [["creation", ">=", "<7 days ago>"]]
	  "customer_name contains Acme"       → [["customer_name", "like", "%Acme%"]]
	  "outstanding_amount > 0 and status is Overdue" → two triples
	"""
	import datetime as _dt

	text = (text or "").strip()
	if not text:
		return []

	filters = []

	# Validate that fields are real DocType fields (prevent injection)
	try:
		meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
		valid_fields = {f.fieldname for f in meta.fields} | {"name", "creation", "modified", "owner", "docstatus"}
	except Exception:
		return []

	def _resolve_date(days_ago):
		d = _dt.date.today() - _dt.timedelta(days=int(days_ago))
		return str(d)

	# Split on "and" / "," into individual clauses
	clauses = re.split(r"\s+and\s+|,\s*", text, flags=re.I)

	_OP_MAP = {
		"is not": "!=",
		"is": "=",
		"equals": "=",
		"equal": "=",
		"not equal": "!=",
		"greater than": ">",
		"less than": "<",
		">=": ">=",
		"<=": "<=",
		">": ">",
		"<": "<",
		"!=": "!=",
		"=": "=",
		"contains": "like",
		"starts with": "like",
		"does not contain": "not like",
		"like": "like",
	}

	# Relative date patterns: "last N days/weeks/months"
	_DATE_PAT = re.compile(
		r"(creation|created|modified|posting_date|transaction_date)\s+(?:in\s+)?last\s+(\d+)\s*(day|week|month)s?",
		re.I,
	)

	for clause in clauses:
		clause = clause.strip()
		if not clause:
			continue

		# Relative date shorthand
		dm = _DATE_PAT.search(clause)
		if dm:
			field = dm.group(1)
			if field == "created":
				field = "creation"
			n = int(dm.group(2))
			unit = dm.group(3).lower()
			days = n if unit == "day" else (n * 7 if unit == "week" else n * 30)
			if field in valid_fields:
				filters.append([field, ">=", _resolve_date(days)])
			continue

		# General: field operator value
		matched = False
		for op_text, op_sym in sorted(_OP_MAP.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):
			pat = re.compile(
				r"^([a-z_][a-z0-9_]*)\s+" + re.escape(op_text) + r"\s+(.+)$",
				re.I,
			)
			m = pat.match(clause)
			if m:
				field = m.group(1).lower()
				value = m.group(2).strip().strip("'\"")
				if field not in valid_fields:
					break
				if op_sym == "like":
					value = f"%{value}%"
				elif op_sym == "not like":
					value = f"%{value}%"
				filters.append([field, op_sym, value])
				matched = True
				break

		if matched:
			continue

		# Simple "field value" (no operator) — assume equality
		parts = clause.split(None, 1)
		if len(parts) == 2:
			field, value = parts[0].lower(), parts[1].strip().strip("'\"")
			if field in valid_fields:
				filters.append([field, "=", value])

	return filters


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_widget_data(widget_name):
	"""Task 3.1: Return fresh data for a GenBI Widget (used by dashboard embedding).

	Runs the widget's saved query SQL directly (fast) or falls back to
	full process_question() if no SQL is cached yet.
	"""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("GenBI Widget", widget_name)
	if not doc.is_public and doc.owner_user != frappe.session.user:
		frappe.throw(_("Not permitted"))

	import time as _time
	start = _time.time()

	# Try saved query SQL first (fastest path)
	sql = None
	if doc.saved_query:
		try:
			sq = frappe.get_doc("GenBI Saved Query", doc.saved_query)
			sql = sq.generated_sql
		except Exception:
			pass

	if sql:
		try:
			sql = _apply_pii_masking(sql)
			_validate_sql(sql)
			settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
			results, from_cache = _execute_query(sql, settings.max_query_rows or 500)
			elapsed = round(_time.time() - start, 3)
			viz_config = _prepare_visualization(results, {"chart_type": doc.chart_type or "table"})
			return {
				"success": True,
				"title": doc.title,
				"explanation": doc.title,
				"sql": sql if doc.show_sql_toggle else None,
				"data": results,
				"chart_config": viz_config,
				"result_count": len(results),
				"execution_time": elapsed,
				"from_cache": from_cache,
				"chart_type": doc.chart_type or "table",
				"refresh_interval": int(doc.refresh_interval or 0),
				"show_last_updated": bool(doc.show_last_updated),
			}
		except Exception:
			pass

	# Fall back to full NLP pipeline
	result = process_question(doc.question)
	if result.get("success") and not doc.saved_query:
		# Auto-cache the generated SQL back into the widget
		generated_sql = result.get("sql") or ""
		if generated_sql:
			frappe.db.set_value("GenBI Widget", widget_name, "saved_query", None)
			# Just cache inline — create a saved query silently
			try:
				sq = frappe.get_doc({
					"doctype": "GenBI Saved Query",
					"title": doc.title,
					"question": doc.question,
					"generated_sql": generated_sql,
					"chart_type": doc.chart_type or "table",
					"is_public": 1,
					"owner_user": frappe.session.user,
				})
				sq.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
				frappe.db.set_value("GenBI Widget", widget_name, "saved_query", sq.name)
				frappe.db.commit()
			except Exception:
				pass
	return result


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_widget(title, question, chart_type="table", refresh_interval=0,
                  show_sql_toggle=0, show_last_updated=1, saved_query=None):
	"""Task 3.1: Create a GenBI Widget and optionally a companion Custom HTML Block."""
	doc = frappe.get_doc({
		"doctype": "GenBI Widget",
		"title": title,
		"question": question,
		"chart_type": chart_type,
		"refresh_interval": int(refresh_interval),
		"show_sql_toggle": int(show_sql_toggle),
		"show_last_updated": int(show_last_updated),
		"saved_query": saved_query or None,
		"is_public": 1,
		"owner_user": frappe.session.user,
	})
	doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
	frappe.db.commit()

	# Create a matching Custom HTML Block so it can be dropped into any Workspace
	block_name = f"GenBI: {title}"[:140]
	embed_script = f"""
<div class="genbi-workspace-widget" data-widget="{doc.name}" style="min-height:120px">
  <div class="text-muted text-center" style="padding:20px">Loading GenBI widget\u2026</div>
</div>
<script>
(function() {{
  var el = document.querySelector('[data-widget="{doc.name}"]');
  if (!el || el._genbi_loaded) return;
  el._genbi_loaded = true;
  frappe.call({{
    method: 'f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_widget_data',
    args: {{ widget_name: '{doc.name}' }},
    callback: function(r) {{
      var d = r.message;
      if (!d || !d.success) {{
        el.innerHTML = '<p class="text-danger">' + (d && d.error ? d.error : 'Error') + '</p>';
        return;
      }}
      frappe.genbi && frappe.genbi.render_workspace_widget
        ? frappe.genbi.render_workspace_widget(el, d)
        : el.innerHTML = '<p class="text-muted">' + frappe.utils.escape_html(d.explanation || d.title || '') + '</p>';
    }}
  }});
}})();
</script>""".strip()

	try:
		if frappe.db.exists("Custom HTML Block", block_name):
			block = frappe.get_doc("Custom HTML Block", block_name)
			block.script = embed_script
			block.save(ignore_permissions=True)
		else:
			block = frappe.get_doc({
				"doctype": "Custom HTML Block",
				"name": block_name,
				"script": embed_script,
				"private": 0,
			})
			block.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
		frappe.db.commit()
	except Exception:
		block_name = None

	return {
		"success": True,
		"widget_name": doc.name,
		"title": doc.title,
		"custom_block_name": block_name,
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_widgets(limit=50):
	"""Task 3.1: Return GenBI Widgets visible to the current user."""
	user = frappe.session.user
	try:
		rows = frappe.get_all(
			"GenBI Widget",
			or_filters=[["owner_user", "=", user], ["is_public", "=", 1]],
			fields=["name", "title", "question", "chart_type", "refresh_interval",
			        "owner_user", "is_public", "saved_query"],
			order_by="modified desc",
			limit=int(limit),
		)
		return rows
	except Exception:
		return []


@frappe.whitelist()
def save_query(question, sql="", chart_type="table", title="", description="", is_public=0, tags=""):
	"""Task 3.5: Save a query to the GenBI Saved Query library."""
	auto_title = title.strip() or question[:60].strip()
	doc = frappe.get_doc({
		"doctype": "GenBI Saved Query",
		"title": auto_title,
		"question": question,
		"generated_sql": sql,
		"chart_type": chart_type or "table",
		"description": description,
		"is_public": int(is_public),
		"tags": tags,
		"owner_user": frappe.session.user,
	})
	doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
	frappe.db.commit()
	return {"success": True, "name": doc.name, "title": doc.title}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_saved_queries(search="", only_mine=0, limit=50):
	"""Task 3.5: Return saved queries visible to the current user."""
	user = frappe.session.user
	filters = []
	if int(only_mine):
		filters.append(["owner_user", "=", user])
	else:
		filters.append(["or", [["owner_user", "=", user], ["is_public", "=", 1]]])

	if search:
		filters.append(["title", "like", f"%{search}%"])

	try:
		rows = frappe.get_all(
			"GenBI Saved Query",
			filters=filters,
			fields=["name", "title", "question", "chart_type", "is_public", "is_pinned",
			        "owner_user", "tags", "last_run", "last_result_count"],
			order_by="is_pinned desc, modified desc",
			limit=int(limit),
		)
		return rows
	except Exception:
		return []


@frappe.whitelist()
def run_saved_query(name):
	"""Task 3.5: Execute a saved query and update its last_run stats."""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("GenBI Saved Query", name)
	# Permission: owner or public
	if doc.owner_user != frappe.session.user and not doc.is_public:
		frappe.throw(_("Not permitted"))

	import time as _time
	start = _time.time()
	sql = doc.generated_sql
	if not sql:
		# Re-run via NLP if no SQL stored
		result = process_question(doc.question)
		return result

	sql = _apply_pii_masking(sql)
	_validate_sql(sql)
	settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
	max_rows = settings.max_query_rows or 500
	results, from_cache = _execute_query(sql, max_rows)
	elapsed = round(_time.time() - start, 3)
	viz_config = _prepare_visualization(results, {"chart_type": doc.chart_type or "table"})

	# Update last run stats
	frappe.db.set_value("GenBI Saved Query", name, {
		"last_run": frappe.utils.now(),
		"last_result_count": len(results),
		"last_execution_time": elapsed,
	})
	frappe.db.commit()

	return {
		"success": True,
		"explanation": f"Saved query: {doc.title}",
		"sql": sql,
		"data": results,
		"chart_config": viz_config,
		"result_count": len(results),
		"execution_time": elapsed,
		"from_cache": from_cache,
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def delete_saved_query(name):
	"""Task 3.5: Delete a saved query (owner or System Manager only)."""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("GenBI Saved Query", name)
	if doc.owner_user != frappe.session.user and "System Manager" not in frappe.get_roles():
		frappe.throw(_("Not permitted"))
	frappe.delete_doc("GenBI Saved Query", name, ignore_permissions=True)
	frappe.db.commit()
	return {"success": True}

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_quick_insight(template_key):
	"""Execute a pre-built insight template."""
	templates = _get_templates()
	if template_key not in templates:
		return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown template: {template_key}"}

	template = templates[template_key]
	try:
		results = frappe.db.sql(template["query"], as_dict=True)
		viz_config = _prepare_visualization(results, {
			"chart_type": template.get("chart", "table"),
			"explanation": template_key,
			"x_field": template.get("x_field"),
			"y_field": template.get("y_field"),
		})
		return {
			"success": True,
			"explanation": template_key,
			"data": results,
			"chart_config": viz_config,
		}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}

# ── Task 4.7: Multi-Company Query Routing ────────────────────────────────────

# ERPNext DocTypes that carry a `company` field at the document (header) level
_COMPANY_AWARE_TABLES = frozenset({
	"tabSales Invoice", "tabPurchase Invoice",
	"tabSales Order", "tabPurchase Order",
	"tabDelivery Note", "tabPurchase Receipt",
	"tabPayment Entry", "tabJournal Entry",
	"tabStock Entry", "tabMaterial Request",
	"tabQuotation", "tabSupplier Quotation",
	"tabExpense Claim", "tabLeave Application",
	"tabSalary Slip", "tabAsset",
	"tabWork Order", "tabProduction Order",
	"tabSales Invoice Item", "tabPurchase Invoice Item",
	"tabSales Order Item", "tabPurchase Order Item",
})

_COMPANY_WHERE_PAT = re.compile(r"`?company`?\s*=\s*['\"]", re.I)


def _get_user_company_filter():
	"""Task 4.7: Resolve a single company for the current user on a multi-company site.

	Returns the company name string, or None when:
	- The site has only one company (no filtering needed)
	- The user has access to multiple companies (cannot auto-filter)
	- The company cannot be determined
	"""
	try:
		companies = frappe.get_all("Company", pluck="name")
		if len(companies) <= 1:
			return None  # Single-company site — filtering adds no value

		# 1. User's default company (set via Defaults)
		user_company = frappe.defaults.get_user_default("Company")
		if user_company:
			return user_company

		# 2. User Permission — only if exactly one company is allowed
		allowed = frappe.get_all(
			"User Permission",
			filters={"user": frappe.session.user, "allow": "Company"},
			pluck="for_value",
		)
		if len(allowed) == 1:
			return allowed[0]

		return None  # Multiple or unresolvable — do not auto-filter
	except Exception:
		return None


def _inject_company_filter(sql, company):
	"""Task 4.7: Inject `company = 'X'` into a SQL WHERE clause when safe.

	Skips injection if:
	- A company filter is already present
	- No company-aware table is referenced in the SQL

	Handles both "WHERE already exists" and "no WHERE" cases.
	"""
	if not company or not sql:
		return sql

	if _COMPANY_WHERE_PAT.search(sql):
		return sql  # Already filtered

	sql_upper = sql.upper()
	if not any(t.upper() in sql_upper for t in _COMPANY_AWARE_TABLES):
		return sql  # No company-aware table referenced

	safe_company = company.replace("'", "''")

	trailer_match = re.search(r"\b(ORDER\s+BY|GROUP\s+BY|HAVING|LIMIT)\b", sql, re.I)
	where_match = re.search(r"\bWHERE\b", sql, re.I)

	if where_match:
		company_clause = f" AND `company` = '{safe_company}'"
		if trailer_match:
			pos = trailer_match.start()
			return sql[:pos] + company_clause + " " + sql[pos:]
		return sql.rstrip() + company_clause
	else:
		company_clause = f" WHERE `company` = '{safe_company}'"
		if trailer_match:
			pos = trailer_match.start()
			return sql[:pos] + company_clause + " " + sql[pos:]
		return sql.rstrip() + company_clause


def _primary_doctype_from_sql(sql):
	"""Extract the primary DocType name from a Frappe SQL query (tab-prefixed table)."""
	m = re.search(r"FROM\s+`?tab([^`\s,]+)`?", sql, re.I)
	return m.group(1) if m else None


def _inject_permission_filters(sql, doctype):
	"""10.1: Inject User Permission WHERE clauses when enforce_permissions is ON.

	Checks frappe.has_permission for the doctype and injects user-permission
	link filters so users only see rows they are allowed to see.
	"""
	try:
		settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
		if not getattr(settings, "enforce_permissions", 0):
			return sql
	except Exception:
		return sql

	if not doctype or not sql:
		return sql

	# Gate: user must have at least read permission on the DocType
	if not frappe.has_permission(doctype, "read"):
		frappe.throw(_("You do not have permission to query {0}").format(doctype))

	# Inject user permission link filters (e.g. territory, department, company)
	try:
		user_permissions = frappe.defaults.get_user_permissions(frappe.session.user)
	except Exception:
		user_permissions = {}

	if not user_permissions:
		return sql

	meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
	clauses = []
	for perm_doctype, values in user_permissions.items():
		# Find link fields on this DocType that point to the permission DocType
		for df in meta.get_link_fields():
			if df.options == perm_doctype and not df.ignore_user_permissions:
				allowed = [v.get("doc") for v in values if v.get("doc")]
				if allowed:
					safe_vals = ", ".join(f"'{v.replace(chr(39), chr(39)*2)}'" for v in allowed)
					clauses.append(f"`{df.fieldname}` IN ({safe_vals})")

	if not clauses:
		return sql

	combined = " AND ".join(clauses)
	where_match = re.search(r"\bWHERE\b", sql, re.I)
	trailer_match = re.search(r"\b(ORDER\s+BY|GROUP\s+BY|HAVING|LIMIT)\b", sql, re.I)

	if where_match:
		insert_clause = f" AND ({combined})"
		if trailer_match:
			pos = trailer_match.start()
			return sql[:pos] + insert_clause + " " + sql[pos:]
		return sql.rstrip() + insert_clause
	else:
		insert_clause = f" WHERE ({combined})"
		if trailer_match:
			pos = trailer_match.start()
			return sql[:pos] + insert_clause + " " + sql[pos:]
		return sql.rstrip() + insert_clause


def _apply_pii_masking(sql):
	"""10.2: Replace configured PII fields with '***' AS fieldname in SELECT.

	Only acts when enable_pii_masking is ON and pii_masked_fields lists fields.
	Handles simple `SELECT col1, col2, ...` patterns; skips SELECT *.
	"""
	try:
		settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
		if not getattr(settings, "enable_pii_masking", 0):
			return sql
		masked_raw = getattr(settings, "pii_masked_fields", "") or ""
	except Exception:
		return sql

	masked_fields = {f.strip().lower() for f in masked_raw.split("\n") if f.strip()}
	if not masked_fields:
		return sql

	# Find the SELECT ... FROM span
	sel_match = re.match(r"(SELECT\s+)(.*?)(\s+FROM\b)", sql, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
	if not sel_match:
		return sql

	select_kw, col_list, from_kw = sel_match.group(1), sel_match.group(2), sel_match.group(3)
	if col_list.strip() == "*":
		return sql  # Can't mask individual fields in SELECT *

	# Split columns preserving AS aliases; replace matching ones
	new_cols = []
	for col in col_list.split(","):
		col = col.strip()
		# Determine the bare field name (strip backticks, alias, table prefix)
		bare = re.sub(r"`", "", col)
		bare = re.split(r"\s+AS\s+", bare, flags=re.I)[-1]  # use alias if present as the key
		bare_base = re.split(r"\s+AS\s+", re.sub(r"`", "", col), flags=re.I)[0].strip()
		# Extract field name without table prefix
		field_name = bare_base.split(".")[-1].strip("`").lower()
		if field_name in masked_fields:
			alias = field_name
			new_cols.append(f"'***' AS `{alias}`")
		else:
			new_cols.append(col)

	return select_kw + ", ".join(new_cols) + from_kw + sql[sel_match.end():]


def _check_query_feasibility(schema):
	"""Task 1.4: Check row counts for the schema's DocTypes before running LLM.

	Returns a dict with:
	  - empty_tables  : DocTypes with 0 rows
	  - large_tables  : DocTypes with > 50 000 rows  [{doctype, count}]
	  - warning       : human-readable string to inject into LLM prompt (may be "")
	"""
	doctypes = list((schema or {}).get("detailed_schema", {}).keys())
	# Strip metadata-only entries (DocType, DocField) — always non-empty
	doctypes = [d for d in doctypes if d not in ("DocType", "DocField")]

	empty_tables = []
	large_tables = []

	for dt in doctypes:
		try:
			count = frappe.db.count(dt)
			if count == 0:
				empty_tables.append(dt)
			elif count > 50_000:
				large_tables.append({"doctype": dt, "count": count})
		except Exception:
			pass

	parts = []
	if empty_tables:
		parts.append(f"These tables have 0 rows: {', '.join(empty_tables)}. Warn the user if their question targets only these tables.")
	if large_tables:
		for lt in large_tables:
			parts.append(f"{lt['doctype']} has {lt['count']:,} rows — always include a WHERE clause or LIMIT.")

	return {
		"empty_tables": empty_tables,
		"large_tables": large_tables,
		"warning": " ".join(parts),
	}


_INTENT_HINTS = {
	"ANALYZE_DATA": (
		"The user wants an aggregation or analysis. "
		"Prefer GROUP BY, SUM, COUNT, AVG patterns. "
		"Choose bar or line chart_type."
	),
	"REFINE": (
		"The user is refining a previous query. "
		"Build on the prior SQL — change only what they specify. "
		"Keep the same table(s) and structure where possible."
	),
	"FIND_PATH": (
		"The user wants to explore a relationship or connection between tables. "
		"Include appropriate JOINs to show linked data."
	),
	"EXPLAIN": (
		"The user wants an explanation, not raw data. "
		"It is acceptable to use type='chat' with a natural language answer "
		"if no SQL query would meaningfully answer the question."
	),
	"BUILD_CANVAS": (
		"The user wants a structured tabular view. "
		"Return a table chart_type with the most relevant columns."
	),
	"SUGGEST": (
		"The user is asking for recommendations. "
		"Return a concise list as a chat response if no specific data is requested."
	),
}


def _classify_query_intent(question):
	"""Task 1.2: Classify the query intent and return an LLM hint string.

	Returns an empty string if the classifier is unavailable or confidence is low.
	"""
	try:
		from f_genbi.genbi.intent_classifier import IntentClassifier
		intent, confidence, _ = IntentClassifier().classify(question)
		if confidence >= 0.55 and intent in _INTENT_HINTS:
			return _INTENT_HINTS[intent]
	except Exception:
		pass
	return ""


def _get_schema_with_entity_resolution(question, full_context):
	"""Resolve entities from the question, then fetch schema for those DocTypes.

	Task 1.3: Uses EntityResolver for accurate DocType identification before
	building schema, instead of relying solely on keyword regex matching.

	Falls back to keyword-based schema if:
	- entity_resolver dependencies are not installed (spaCy, sentence-transformers)
	- No entities resolved with sufficient confidence (>= 0.85)
	- Any exception occurs
	"""
	try:
		from f_genbi.genbi.entity_resolver import EntityResolver

		resolver = EntityResolver()
		entities = resolver.extract_entities(question)
		high_conf = [e["doctype"] for e in entities if e.get("confidence", 0) >= 0.85]

		if high_conf:
			return get_schema_for_doctypes(high_conf, question=full_context)
	except Exception:
		pass  # dependencies not installed or resolution failed — fall through

	return get_relevant_schema(full_context)


# ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# LLM Integration
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────


def _convert_to_sql(question, schema, settings, history=None, data_warnings=None, company_filter=None):
	"""Use LLM to convert natural language to SQL."""
	system_prompt = """You are GenBI, a business intelligence assistant for ERPNext. You query the database to answer user questions.

CRITICAL RULE: ALWAYS use type="query" with SQL unless the question is IMPOSSIBLE to answer with data.

ONLY use type="chat" for these EXACT cases:
- Greetings: "hi", "hello", "thanks", "bye"
- Meta questions: "what can you do", "help me", "how do you work"
- Pure process explanations with NO data aspect: "what is the sales order workflow steps"

EVERYTHING ELSE must be type="query" with SQL. Examples that MUST use SQL:
- "contact details of X" → query Contact/Customer table
- "show me details of X" → query the relevant table
- "list all X" → SELECT query
- "pie chart for this" → same topic, new chart_type
- ANY question mentioning a DocType, person, item, customer, supplier → SQL
- "which doctypes use supplier" → query `tabDocField` for Link fields pointing to Supplier
- "which doctypes have customer field" → query `tabDocField`
- "what fields does Sales Invoice have" → query `tabDocField`

SCHEMA INFO:
- You receive DETAILED SCHEMA with full field info for the most relevant DocTypes.
- You also receive ALL AVAILABLE TABLES - a list of every queryable table in the system.
- You can query ANY table from the full list, not just the detailed ones. Use `tab{DocType Name}` as the table name.
- For tables without detailed schema, use common Frappe field names: name, creation, modified, owner, disabled, etc.

SQL RULES:
1. ONLY SELECT statements. Never INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, DROP, ALTER.
2. Table names: backtick-quoted with `tab` prefix: `tabSales Invoice`, `tabCustomer`
3. Column names: backtick-quoted: `grand_total`, `customer_name`
   IMPORTANT: ONLY use standard ASCII quotes in SQL. Use single quotes ' for string values and backticks ` for identifiers. NEVER use unicode curly/smart quotes like \u201c \u201d \u2018 \u2019.
4. NEVER use SELECT *. Always select specific, relevant columns only. Pick the most useful 5-15 columns for the question.
   - For invoice details: `name`, `customer_name`, `posting_date`, `grand_total`, `outstanding_amount`, `status`
   - For items: `item_code`, `item_name`, `qty`, `rate`, `amount`
   - For customers: `name`, `customer_name`, `customer_group`, `territory`
5. docstatus: Check the "note" field in the schema for each table.
   - If note says "Submittable": add WHERE `docstatus` = 1 (for Sales Invoice, Purchase Invoice, etc.)
   - If note says "Not submittable": do NOT filter by docstatus (for Customer, Item, Supplier, Contact, etc.)
   - If unsure (table not in detailed schema): do NOT add docstatus filter
6. MySQL date functions: CURDATE(), DATE_SUB(), MONTH(), YEAR(), DATE_FORMAT()
    7. When querying documents with a status or amount, ALWAYS include `status`, `currency`, and `grand_total`/`outstanding_amount` columns.
    8. When user asks for "details" or "items" of a transaction, ALWAYS JOIN with the child/item table.
       Example (Sales Invoice):
       ```sql
       SELECT si.name, si.posting_date, si.customer_name, si.currency, si.grand_total, si.outstanding_amount, si.status,
              sii.item_code, sii.item_name, sii.qty, sii.rate, sii.amount
       FROM `tabSales Invoice` si
       JOIN `tabSales Invoice Item` sii ON sii.parent = si.name
       WHERE ...
       ```
       Example (Purchase Order):
       ```sql
       SELECT po.name, po.transaction_date, po.supplier_name, po.currency, po.grand_total, po.status,
              poi.item_code, poi.item_name, poi.qty, poi.rate, poi.amount
       FROM `tabPurchase Order` po
       JOIN `tabPurchase Order Item` poi ON poi.parent = po.name
       WHERE ...
       ```

    9. For aggregation:
       - Count: `SELECT count(*) FROM ...`
       - Sum: `SELECT sum(field) FROM ...`
       - Average: `SELECT avg(field) FROM ...`

    10. Date filtering:
       - Today: `posting_date = CURDATE()`
       - This month: `request_date BETWEEN DATE_FORMAT(NOW(), '%Y-%m-01') AND LAST_DAY(NOW())`
       - Last 7 days: `posting_date >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 7 DAY)`

FRAPPE FIELD CONVENTIONS:
- "enabled"/"active" customers = WHERE `disabled` = 0
- "disabled" customers = WHERE `disabled` = 1
- ONLY these tables have a `disabled` field: Customer, Supplier, Item, Employee, Warehouse, Account
- Other tables like Sales Order, Sales Invoice, Purchase Order do NOT have `disabled`. Use `docstatus` instead.
- NEVER add `disabled` to a WHERE clause unless the table is in the list above.

CORRECT SQL PATTERNS:
- Count enabled vs disabled: SUM(`disabled` = 0) AS enabled_count, SUM(`disabled` = 1) AS disabled_count
  (NEVER use COUNT(disabled=0) - COUNT counts non-null which is always true. Use SUM for conditional counting.)
- Conditional count: SUM(CASE WHEN condition THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS alias
- Enabled customers: SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM `tabCustomer` WHERE `disabled` = 0

LIKE SEARCH - Always use percent wildcards for partial matching:
- LIKE '%searchterm%' (with percent signs on both sides)
- Example: WHERE `customer_name` LIKE '%Alison%' (finds "Alison Franklin", "Alison Smith", etc.)
- Example: WHERE `item_name` LIKE '%laptop%' (finds "Gaming Laptop", "Laptop Stand", etc.)

SEARCHING FOR A PERSON OR ENTITY BY NAME:
When user mentions a person name (e.g. "Alison", "Grant Plastics"), search using LIKE with percent wildcards:
- Search customers: SELECT `name`, `customer_name`, `customer_group`, `territory`, `mobile_no`, `email_id` FROM `tabCustomer` WHERE `customer_name` LIKE '%Alison%' ORDER BY `modified` DESC LIMIT 10
- Search contacts: SELECT c.`first_name`, c.`last_name`, c.`email_id`, c.`mobile_no`, c.`phone`, dl.`link_doctype` AS entity_type, dl.`link_name` AS entity_name FROM `tabContact` c LEFT JOIN `tabDynamic Link` dl ON dl.`parent` = c.`name` AND dl.`parenttype` = 'Contact' WHERE c.`first_name` LIKE '%Alison%' OR c.`last_name` LIKE '%Alison%' ORDER BY c.`creation` DESC LIMIT 10
- Search employees: SELECT `name`, `employee_name`, `department`, `designation`, `cell_phone`, `company_email` FROM `tabEmployee` WHERE `employee_name` LIKE '%Alison%' ORDER BY `modified` DESC LIMIT 10
- Search suppliers: SELECT `name`, `supplier_name`, `supplier_group`, `country` FROM `tabSupplier` WHERE `supplier_name` LIKE '%Alison%' ORDER BY `modified` DESC LIMIT 10

FRAPPE RELATIONSHIP PATTERNS:
- Contacts/Addresses link to ANY entity via `tabDynamic Link`:
  SELECT c.`first_name`, c.`last_name`, c.`email_id`, c.`mobile_no`, c.`phone` FROM `tabContact` c JOIN `tabDynamic Link` dl ON dl.`parent` = c.`name` AND dl.`parenttype` = 'Contact' WHERE dl.`link_name` LIKE '%Grant%' ORDER BY c.`creation` DESC LIMIT 10
- Address lookup:
  SELECT a.`address_line1`, a.`city`, a.`state`, a.`country`, a.`pincode` FROM `tabAddress` a JOIN `tabDynamic Link` dl ON dl.`parent` = a.`name` AND dl.`parenttype` = 'Address' WHERE dl.`link_name` LIKE '%Grant%' ORDER BY a.`creation` DESC LIMIT 10
- Child table items link to parent via `parent` field:
  SELECT sii.`item_name`, sii.`qty`, sii.`rate` FROM `tabSales Invoice Item` sii WHERE sii.`parent` = 'SINV-00001' ORDER BY sii.`idx` LIMIT 50

METADATA / SCHEMA QUERIES:
When user asks about DocTypes, fields, relationships, or system structure, query the `tabDocField` and `tabDocType` tables:
- "Which DocTypes use Customer?" → Find all Link fields pointing to Customer:
  SELECT `parent` AS doctype, `fieldname`, `label` FROM `tabDocField` WHERE `fieldtype` = 'Link' AND `options` = 'Customer' ORDER BY `parent` LIMIT 50
- "Which DocTypes use Supplier?" → Same pattern:
  SELECT `parent` AS doctype, `fieldname`, `label` FROM `tabDocField` WHERE `fieldtype` = 'Link' AND `options` = 'Supplier' ORDER BY `parent` LIMIT 50
- "What fields does Sales Invoice have?" → List fields of a DocType:
  SELECT `fieldname`, `label`, `fieldtype`, `options` FROM `tabDocField` WHERE `parent` = 'Sales Invoice' ORDER BY `idx` LIMIT 100
- "List all DocTypes in Selling module":
  SELECT `name`, `module`, `issingle`, `istable` FROM `tabDocType` WHERE `module` = 'Selling' ORDER BY `name` LIMIT 50
- "Which DocTypes are submittable?" →
  SELECT `name`, `module` FROM `tabDocType` WHERE `is_submittable` = 1 ORDER BY `module`, `name` LIMIT 50
- For any question about which DocTypes reference, link to, or use another DocType, query `tabDocField` WHERE `fieldtype` = 'Link' AND `options` = '<DocType Name>'.

FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS:
When user says "this", "them", "it", "those", "for this", "pie chart for this", "show table", "bar chart" - they refer to the PREVIOUS conversation topic. Look at the last assistant message to know what data/DocType was queried.
- "pie chart for this" after customer count → group customers by a dimension (territory, customer_group, customer_type) for the pie chart
- "show table for this" → same SQL but chart_type="table"
- "bar chart" → same data but chart_type="bar"
- NEVER switch to a different DocType on follow-ups unless the user explicitly mentions a new one

PIE/DONUT CHART RULES:
- Pie charts NEED a category dimension and a value. They show proportions.
- If previous query was a simple count (e.g. "how many customers"), and user asks for pie chart:
  → Group by a useful dimension: customer_group, territory, customer_type, etc.
  → Example: SELECT `customer_group`, COUNT(*) as count FROM `tabCustomer` WHERE `disabled` = 0 GROUP BY `customer_group` ORDER BY count DESC LIMIT 10
- NEVER use `disabled` as the pie chart grouping dimension unless user specifically asks "enabled vs disabled"
- For pie charts with too many categories, LIMIT to top 10

RESPONSE FORMAT - Return ONLY valid JSON (no markdown, no code blocks):

For DATA queries:
{"type": "query", "sql": "SELECT ...", "chart_type": "table", "explanation": "Top Customers", "answer": "Here are the top customers by revenue.", "x_field": "customer", "y_field": "revenue"}

For CHAT responses (ONLY for greetings/meta questions):
{"type": "chat", "answer": "I can help you query your business data. Try asking about customers, sales, inventory, etc."}

IMPORTANT: The "answer" field must be a real sentence about the query, NOT a description of the format.
- Do NOT guess or make up specific numbers. You do not know the result yet.
- Describe what the query will show, not what the result is.
- Good: "Here are the active customers.", "Contact details found.", "Sales breakdown by month."
- Bad: "You have 9 active customers." (you don't know it's 9 until the query runs)

CHART TYPE GUIDE:
- number: single aggregate value (COUNT, SUM, AVG with one result)
- table: detailed multi-column data, lists of records
- bar: comparing categories (top customers, items by quantity)
- line: trends over time (monthly sales, daily attendance)
- pie/donut: proportions/distribution (revenue by territory, customers by group)

IMPORTANT: When query returns one row with multiple numeric columns (e.g. enabled_count=9, disabled_count=0), use chart_type="bar" NOT "number". Use "number" ONLY for single-value results."""

	# Task 1.2: inject intent hint into system prompt
	intent_hint = _classify_query_intent(question)
	if intent_hint:
		system_prompt = system_prompt + f"\n\nQUERY INTENT HINT: {intent_hint}"

	# Task 3.8: inject few-shot training examples (approved, semantically similar)
	try:
		from f_genbi.genbi.training_loop import build_few_shot_prompt_section
		few_shot = build_few_shot_prompt_section(question)
		if few_shot:
			system_prompt = system_prompt + few_shot
	except Exception:
		pass

	# Task 4.7: inject company context hint so LLM generates company-filtered SQL
	if company_filter:
		system_prompt = (
			system_prompt
			+ f"\n\nCOMPANY CONTEXT: This is a multi-company ERPNext site. The current user's "
			  f"company is **{company_filter}**. Always add `WHERE \\`company\\` = '{company_filter}'` "
			  f"(or `AND \\`company\\` = '{company_filter}'`) to queries against transaction tables "
			  f"(Sales Invoice, Purchase Invoice, Sales Order, Payment Entry, Journal Entry, etc.) "
			  f"unless the user explicitly asks for all-company data."
		)

	# Build messages array with conversation history as actual chat turns
	messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system_prompt}]

	# Schema now has two parts: detailed fields for relevant tables + lightweight list of ALL tables
	detailed_schema = schema.get("detailed_schema", schema)
	all_doctypes = schema.get("all_doctypes", {})

	schema_parts = []
	schema_parts.append(f"DETAILED SCHEMA (with fields):\n{json.dumps(detailed_schema, indent=2, default=str)}")
	if all_doctypes:
		# Compact grouped format: "Module: DocType1, DocType2, ..."
		# Table name is always `tab` + DocType name (e.g. Customer → `tabCustomer`)
		module_lines = []
		for module, names in all_doctypes.items():
			module_lines.append(f"  {module}: {', '.join(names)}")
		schema_parts.append(
			"ALL AVAILABLE TABLES by module (use `tab` + name as table, e.g. Customer → `tabCustomer`):\n"
			+ "\n".join(module_lines)
		)

	schema_text = "\n\n".join(schema_parts)

	if history:
		# Add previous exchanges as real user/assistant turns
		for h in history[-5:]:
			q = h.get("question", "")
			sql = h.get("sql", "")
			answer = h.get("answer", "")
			if q:
				messages.append({"role": "user", "content": q})
			if sql or answer:
				assistant_response = json.dumps({
					"type": "query" if sql else "chat",
					"sql": sql,
					"answer": answer,
					"explanation": answer,
					"chart_type": h.get("chart_type", "number"),
				})
				messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": assistant_response})

	# Add the current question with schema (+ optional data warnings from Task 1.4)
	warnings_section = f"\n\nDATA WARNINGS: {data_warnings}" if data_warnings else ""
	messages.append({
		"role": "user",
		"content": f"{schema_text}{warnings_section}\n\nQuestion: {question}\n\nReturn ONLY valid JSON."
	})

	response_text = _call_llm(system_prompt, messages, settings)
	_record_usage_log(question, settings, messages, response_text)
	return _parse_llm_response(response_text)


def _record_usage_log(question, settings, messages, response_text):
	"""10.8: Best-effort insert into GenBI Usage Log. Never raises."""
	try:
		provider = getattr(settings, "llm_provider", "unknown") or "unknown"
		model = getattr(settings, "model_name", "") or ""

		# Estimate token counts from character lengths (rough 4 chars/token heuristic)
		tokens_in = sum(len(m.get("content", "")) for m in messages) // 4
		tokens_out = len(response_text) // 4

		# Very rough cost estimate per 1M tokens (provider-agnostic fallback)
		_cost_per_1m = {"openai": 2.0, "anthropic": 3.0, "gemini": 0.35}.get(provider.lower(), 1.0)
		cost_usd = (_cost_per_1m / 1_000_000) * (tokens_in + tokens_out)

		frappe.get_doc({
			"doctype": "GenBI Usage Log",
			"provider": provider,
			"model": model,
			"tokens_in": tokens_in,
			"tokens_out": tokens_out,
			"cost_usd": round(cost_usd, 6),
			"user": frappe.session.user,
			"question_preview": (question or "")[:200],
			"created": frappe.utils.now_datetime(),
		}).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
	except Exception:
		pass  # usage logging must never break the main flow


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_usage_summary():
	"""10.8: Monthly cost + token summary for the GenBI Settings dashboard card."""
	import frappe.utils

	first_of_month = frappe.utils.get_first_day(frappe.utils.today())
	rows = frappe.db.sql(
		"""
		SELECT
			provider,
			SUM(tokens_in)  AS tokens_in,
			SUM(tokens_out) AS tokens_out,
			SUM(cost_usd)   AS cost_usd,
			COUNT(*)        AS calls
		FROM `tabGenBI Usage Log`
		WHERE `created` >= %(from_date)s
		GROUP BY provider
		ORDER BY cost_usd DESC
		""",
		{"from_date": first_of_month},
		as_dict=True,
	)
	total_cost = sum(r.cost_usd or 0 for r in rows)
	total_calls = sum(r.calls or 0 for r in rows)
	return {"rows": rows, "total_cost_usd": round(total_cost, 4), "total_calls": total_calls}


def _normalize_url(url):
	"""Ensure URL has http:// or https:// prefix and strip trailing slash."""
	url = url.strip().rstrip("/")
	if url and not url.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
		url = "http://" + url
	return url


def _get_api_key(settings):
	"""Safely retrieve API key. Returns empty string if not set."""
	# Check if a password value actually exists before calling get_password
	# to avoid frappe.throw adding error to message log
	has_password = frappe.db.sql(
		"SELECT `password` FROM `__Auth` WHERE `doctype`=%s AND `name`=%s AND `fieldname`=%s",
		("GenBI Settings", "GenBI Settings", "api_key"),
	)
	if not has_password:
		return ""
	try:
		return settings.get_password("api_key") or ""
	except Exception:
		return ""


def _call_llm(system_prompt, messages, settings):
	"""Call the configured LLM provider.

	Args:
		system_prompt: System prompt string
		messages: Full messages array [{"role": "system/user/assistant", "content": "..."}]
		settings: GenBI Settings doc
	"""
	provider = settings.llm_provider
	model = settings.model_name or "llama3.2"
	temperature = float(settings.temperature or 0.1)
	max_tokens = int(settings.max_tokens or 4000)

	if provider == "Ollama (Local)":
		# Ollama doesn't need an API key at all
		base_url = _normalize_url(settings.api_base_url or "http://localhost:11434")
		return _call_ollama(model, base_url, messages, temperature, max_tokens)

	# All other providers may need an API key
	api_key = _get_api_key(settings)

	if provider == "OpenAI":
		if not api_key:
			frappe.throw(_("API Key is required for OpenAI"))
		return _call_openai(api_key, model, messages, temperature, max_tokens)
	elif provider == "Anthropic (Claude)":
		if not api_key:
			frappe.throw(_("API Key is required for Anthropic"))
		return _call_anthropic(api_key, model, system_prompt, messages, temperature, max_tokens)
	elif provider == "Google Gemini":
		if not api_key:
			frappe.throw(_("API Key is required for Google Gemini"))
		return _call_gemini(api_key, model, system_prompt, messages, temperature, max_tokens)
	elif provider == "Custom (OpenAI-Compatible)":
		base_url = _normalize_url(settings.api_base_url or "")
		if not base_url:
			frappe.throw(_("API Base URL is required for Custom provider"))
		return _call_custom(api_key, model, base_url, messages, temperature, max_tokens)
	else:
		frappe.throw(_(f"Unknown LLM provider: {provider}"))


def _raise_api_error(response, provider):
	"""Raise a descriptive error from an API response."""
	try:
		error_body = response.json()
		error_msg = error_body.get("error", {})
		if isinstance(error_msg, dict):
			detail = error_msg.get("message", "") or error_msg.get("type", "")
		else:
			detail = str(error_msg)
	except Exception:
		detail = response.text[:300]

	frappe.throw(
		_("{provider} API error ({status}): {detail}").format(
			provider=provider,
			status=response.status_code,
			detail=detail,
		)
	)


def _call_ollama(model, base_url, messages, temperature, max_tokens):
	"""Call Ollama local LLM (OpenAI-compatible API)."""
	import requests

	url = f"{base_url}/v1/chat/completions"

	try:
		response = requests.post(
			url,
			headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
			json={
				"model": model,
				"messages": messages,
				"temperature": temperature,
				"max_tokens": max_tokens,
				"stream": False,
			},
			timeout=120,  # Ollama can be slower on local hardware
		)
	except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:
		frappe.throw(
			_("Cannot connect to Ollama at {url}. Make sure Ollama is running: 'ollama serve'").format(url=base_url)
		)

	if not response.ok:
		_raise_api_error(response, "Ollama")
	data = response.json()
	return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]


def _call_openai(api_key, model, messages, temperature, max_tokens):
	"""Call OpenAI API."""
	import requests

	response = requests.post(
		"https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions",
		headers={
			"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
			"Content-Type": "application/json",
		},
		json={
			"model": model,
			"messages": messages,
			"temperature": temperature,
			"max_tokens": max_tokens,
		},
		timeout=60,
	)
	if not response.ok:
		_raise_api_error(response, "OpenAI")
	data = response.json()
	return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]


def _call_anthropic(api_key, model, system_prompt, messages, temperature, max_tokens):
	"""Call Anthropic Claude API."""
	import requests

	# Anthropic uses separate system param + messages (no system role in messages)
	anthropic_messages = []
	for m in messages:
		if m["role"] != "system":
			anthropic_messages.append({"role": m["role"], "content": m["content"]})

	response = requests.post(
		"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",
		headers={
			"x-api-key": api_key,
			"anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
			"Content-Type": "application/json",
		},
		json={
			"model": model,
			"max_tokens": max_tokens,
			"temperature": temperature,
			"system": system_prompt,
			"messages": anthropic_messages,
		},
		timeout=60,
	)
	if not response.ok:
		_raise_api_error(response, "Anthropic")
	data = response.json()
	return data["content"][0]["text"]


def _call_gemini(api_key, model, system_prompt, messages, temperature, max_tokens):
	"""Call Google Gemini API."""
	import requests

	# Convert messages to Gemini format (user/model turns)
	contents = []
	for m in messages:
		if m["role"] == "system":
			continue  # System prompt handled separately
		role = "model" if m["role"] == "assistant" else "user"
		contents.append({"role": role, "parts": [{"text": m["content"]}]})

	# If no contents, use combined prompt as fallback
	if not contents:
		combined = f"{system_prompt}\n\n---\n\n" + messages[-1]["content"] if messages else ""
		contents = [{"parts": [{"text": combined}]}]
	else:
		# Prepend system prompt to first user message
		if contents[0]["role"] == "user":
			contents[0]["parts"][0]["text"] = system_prompt + "\n\n---\n\n" + contents[0]["parts"][0]["text"]

	url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent?key={api_key}"

	response = requests.post(
		url,
		headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
		json={
			"contents": contents,
			"generationConfig": {
				"temperature": temperature,
				"maxOutputTokens": max_tokens,
			},
		},
		timeout=60,
	)

	if not response.ok:
		_raise_api_error(response, "Gemini")
	data = response.json()
	return data["candidates"][0]["content"]["parts"][0]["text"]


def _call_custom(api_key, model, base_url, messages, temperature, max_tokens):
	"""Call a custom OpenAI-compatible API (works with vLLM, LM Studio, text-generation-webui, etc.)."""
	import requests

	url = base_url.rstrip("/")
	# Append /chat/completions if not already present
	if not url.endswith("/chat/completions"):
		if not url.endswith("/v1"):
			url += "/v1"
		url += "/chat/completions"

	headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
	if api_key:
		headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"

	try:
		response = requests.post(
			url,
			headers=headers,
			json={
				"model": model,
				"messages": messages,
				"temperature": temperature,
				"max_tokens": max_tokens,
			},
			timeout=120,
		)
	except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:
		frappe.throw(
			_("Cannot connect to custom LLM at {url}. Check if the server is running.").format(url=url)
		)

	if not response.ok:
		_raise_api_error(response, "Custom LLM")
	data = response.json()
	return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]


def _parse_llm_response(response_text):
	"""Parse JSON from LLM response, handling markdown code blocks, control characters, and truncation."""
