	text = response_text.strip()

	# Remove markdown code blocks if present
	if text.startswith("```"):
		text = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*\n?", "", text)
		text = re.sub(r"\n?```\s*$", "", text)

	def _clean_json(s):
		"""Remove control characters that break JSON parsing (common with local LLMs)."""
		return re.sub(r'[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]', '', s)

	def _try_parse(s):
		"""Try to parse JSON, return None on failure."""
		try:
			return json.loads(s)
		except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
			return None

	# Direct parse
	result = _try_parse(text)
	if result:
		return result

	# Clean control characters and try again
	cleaned = _clean_json(text)
	result = _try_parse(cleaned)
	if result:
		return result

	# Extract JSON object from text
	match = re.search(r"\{[\s\S]*\}", cleaned)
	if match:
		result = _try_parse(match.group())
		if result:
			return result

	# Handle truncated JSON (LLM hit max_tokens mid-response)
	# Try to find a JSON object and close it properly
	match = re.search(r"\{[\s\S]*", cleaned)
	if match:
		truncated = match.group()
		# Try progressively closing the JSON
		for close in ['"}', '"}', '"}]}', '"}]}']:
			result = _try_parse(truncated + close)
			if result:
				return result
		# More aggressive: find the last complete key-value pair and close
		# Look for last complete "key": "value" or "key": number
		last_complete = re.search(r'(.*"(?:answer|sql|explanation|chart_type|type)":\s*"[^"]*")', truncated, re.DOTALL)
		if last_complete:
			result = _try_parse(last_complete.group(1) + "}")
			if result:
				return result

	# Fix common LLM JSON issues (newlines in strings, trailing commas)
	match = re.search(r"\{[\s\S]*\}", text)
	if match:
		raw_json = match.group()
		fixed = re.sub(r'(?<=": ")(.*?)(?=")', lambda m: m.group(0).replace('\n', '\\n'), raw_json, flags=re.DOTALL)
		fixed = _clean_json(fixed)
		fixed = re.sub(r',\s*}', '}', fixed)
		result = _try_parse(fixed)
		if result:
			return result

	# If nothing works, return a chat response with the raw text
	# This is better than throwing an error
	return {
		"type": "chat",
		"answer": "I understood your question but had trouble formatting the response. Could you try rephrasing it? For example, try specific data questions like 'How many customers do we have?' or 'Show total sales this month'.",
	}


def _summarize_results(question, results, query_plan, settings, child_items=None):
	"""Call LLM to generate a conversational summary of query results."""
	if not results:
		return "No results found for your query."

	# Prepare a compact data preview (limit to avoid huge prompts)
	preview_rows = results[:20]
	columns = list(results[0].keys()) if results else []

	# Build compact text representation of parent data
	data_lines = []
	for row in preview_rows:
		parts = []
		for col in columns:
			val = row.get(col)
			if val is not None and val != "":
				parts.append(f"{col}: {val}")
		data_lines.append(", ".join(parts))
	data_text = "\n".join(data_lines)

	total_rows = len(results)
	if total_rows > 20:
		data_text += f"\n... and {total_rows - 20} more rows"

	# Add child item data if available
	items_text = ""
	if child_items:
		item_columns = list(child_items[0].keys())
		item_lines = []
		for item in child_items[:30]:
			parts = []
			for col in item_columns:
				val = item.get(col)
				if val is not None and val != "":
					parts.append(f"{col}: {val}")
			item_lines.append(", ".join(parts))
		items_text = f"\n\nLINE ITEMS ({len(child_items)} items):\n" + "\n".join(item_lines)
		if len(child_items) > 30:
			items_text += f"\n... and {len(child_items) - 30} more items"

	summary_prompt = f"""You are a helpful business assistant. The user asked: "{question}"

A database query returned {total_rows} row(s) with these columns: {', '.join(columns)}

Here is the data:
{data_text}{items_text}

Write a brief, friendly, conversational summary of this data. Key rules:
- Highlight the most important findings (totals, key names, notable values)
- If there are items/line items, mention each item with its name, quantity, rate, and amount
- Use actual numbers from the data, formatted nicely (e.g. currency with commas)
- Keep it to 2-5 sentences for simple results, or a short bullet list for detailed data
- Do NOT repeat the raw data in tabular form - that will be shown separately
- Do NOT say "based on the query" or "the database returned" - speak naturally
- Do NOT guess or invent data that is not present. Only mention what you can see.
- Respond in plain text only, no markdown formatting"""

	messages = [
		{"role": "system", "content": summary_prompt},
		{"role": "user", "content": question},
	]

	try:
		response = _call_llm(summary_prompt, messages, settings)
		# Clean up the response
		summary = response.strip()
		# Remove markdown formatting if present
		summary = re.sub(r"^```\w*\n?", "", summary)
		summary = re.sub(r"\n?```$", "", summary)
		return summary if summary else query_plan.get("answer", "")
	except Exception:
		# If summarization fails, fall back to the original answer
		return query_plan.get("answer", "") or query_plan.get("explanation", "")


# ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# SQL Validation & Execution
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────


def _sanitize_sql(sql):
	"""Sanitize LLM-generated SQL by fixing common issues like unicode quotes."""
	if not sql:
		return sql
	# Replace unicode smart quotes with standard ASCII quotes
	# \u201c (\u201c) and \u201d (\u201d) → regular single quote '
	# \u2018 (\u2018) and \u2019 (\u2019) → regular single quote '
	# \u00ab (\u00ab) and \u00bb (\u00bb) → regular single quote '
	sql = sql.replace("\u201c", "'").replace("\u201d", "'")
	sql = sql.replace("\u2018", "'").replace("\u2019", "'")
	sql = sql.replace("\u00ab", "'").replace("\u00bb", "'")
	# Replace unicode dashes with regular hyphen-minus
	sql = sql.replace("\u2013", "-").replace("\u2014", "-")
	# Rewrite SELECT * to specific columns using DocType metadata
	sql = _rewrite_select_star(sql)
	return sql


def _rewrite_select_star(sql):
	"""Replace SELECT * with specific useful columns using DocType metadata."""
	if not re.search(r"SELECT\s+\*\s+FROM", sql, re.IGNORECASE):
		return sql

	# Only handle simple single-table queries (not JOINs - those are already good)
	if re.search(r"\bJOIN\b", sql, re.IGNORECASE):
		return sql

	# Extract the table name: `tabSales Invoice` or `tabCustomer`
	match = re.search(r"SELECT\s+\*\s+FROM\s+`(tab[^`]+)`", sql, re.IGNORECASE)
	if not match:
		return sql

	table_name = match.group(1)
	# Remove 'tab' prefix to get the DocType name
	doctype = table_name[3:]  # "tabSales Invoice" → "Sales Invoice"

	try:
		meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
	except Exception:
		return sql

	useful_types = {
		"Currency", "Int", "Float", "Date", "Datetime",
		"Link", "Select", "Data", "Small Text", "Percent", "Check",
	}

	# Always include standard fields
	standard = ["`name`", "`creation`", "`modified`", "`owner`"]
	if meta.is_submittable:
		standard.append("`docstatus`")

	# Score each field by business relevance and pick the top ones
	# Priority: in_list_view (40) > bold (20) > reqd (10) > numeric/date (5) > others (1)
	def _field_score(f):
		if f.fieldtype not in useful_types:
			return -1
		score = 0
		score += 40 if getattr(f, "in_list_view", 0) else 0
		score += 20 if getattr(f, "bold", 0) else 0
		score += 10 if getattr(f, "reqd", 0) else 0
		score += 5 if f.fieldtype in {"Currency", "Float", "Int", "Date", "Datetime", "Percent"} else 0
		score += 1  # base
		return score

	ranked = sorted(
		[f for f in meta.fields if _field_score(f) > 0],
		key=_field_score,
		reverse=True,
	)
	# Cap total at 15 (standard fields + ranked custom fields)
	budget = 15 - len(standard)
	columns = standard + [f"`{f.fieldname}`" for f in ranked[:budget]]

	column_str = ", ".join(columns)

	# Check if table has an alias (e.g. `tabSales Invoice` si)
	alias_match = re.search(
		r"SELECT\s+\*\s+FROM\s+`tab[^`]+`\s+(\w+)",
		sql, re.IGNORECASE,
	)
	if alias_match:
		alias = alias_match.group(1)
		# Don't treat SQL keywords as aliases
		if alias.upper() not in ("WHERE", "ORDER", "GROUP", "HAVING", "LIMIT", "LEFT", "RIGHT", "INNER", "JOIN", "ON", "AND", "OR"):
			column_str = ", ".join(f"{alias}.{c}" for c in columns)

	sql = re.sub(
		r"SELECT\s+\*\s+FROM",
		f"SELECT {column_str} FROM",
		sql, count=1, flags=re.IGNORECASE,
	)
	return sql


# ── Task 1.6: Dynamic child table map ─────────────────────────────────────────
_CHILD_TABLE_MAP_CACHE_KEY = "genbi_child_table_map_v1"


def _get_child_table_map() -> dict:
	"""Return a mapping of parent DocType → child DocType for all Table fields.

	Discovered dynamically from DocField metadata and cached in Redis for 1 hour.
	Falls back to a minimal hard-coded set if the DB query fails.
	"""
	cached = frappe.cache().get_value(_CHILD_TABLE_MAP_CACHE_KEY)
	if cached:
		return cached

	try:
		rows = frappe.db.get_all(
			"DocField",
			filters={"fieldtype": "Table"},
			fields=["parent", "options"],
			ignore_permissions=True,
		)
		child_map = {row["parent"]: row["options"] for row in rows if row.get("options")}
		frappe.cache().set_value(_CHILD_TABLE_MAP_CACHE_KEY, child_map, expires_in_sec=3600)
		return child_map
	except Exception:
		# Minimal fallback so the feature still works if DB query fails
		return {
			"Sales Invoice": "Sales Invoice Item",
			"Purchase Invoice": "Purchase Invoice Item",
			"Sales Order": "Sales Order Item",
			"Purchase Order": "Purchase Order Item",
			"Delivery Note": "Delivery Note Item",
			"Purchase Receipt": "Purchase Receipt Item",
			"Quotation": "Quotation Item",
			"Stock Entry": "Stock Entry Detail",
		}

# Useful columns to fetch from child item tables
_CHILD_ITEM_COLUMNS = [
	"parent", "idx", "item_code", "item_name", "description",
	"qty", "rate", "amount", "uom",
]


def _auto_fetch_child_items(sql, results, question):
	"""Auto-fetch child table items if user asked about them but SQL only queries parent table.

	Returns a list of child item dicts, or None if not applicable.
	"""
	if not results:
		return None

	# Check if question mentions items/details
	item_keywords = r"\bitem|\bproduct|\bline.?item|\bdetail|\bwhat.*(?:contain|include)|\bwhich.*item"
	if not re.search(item_keywords, question.lower()):
		return None

	# If SQL already JOINs a child table, no enrichment needed
	if re.search(r"`tab\w[\w\s]*Item`|`tab\w[\w\s]*Detail`", sql, re.IGNORECASE):
		return None

	# Find which parent table is being queried
	table_match = re.search(r"`tab([^`]+)`", sql)
	if not table_match:
		return None

	parent_doctype = table_match.group(1)
	child_doctype = _get_child_table_map().get(parent_doctype)
	if not child_doctype:
		return None

	# Get parent document names
	parent_names = [r.get("name") for r in results if r.get("name")]
	if not parent_names:
		return None

	try:
		# Build column list — only include columns that actually exist in the child table
		meta = frappe.get_meta(child_doctype)
		existing_fields = {f.fieldname for f in meta.fields}
		existing_fields.add("name")
		existing_fields.add("parent")
		existing_fields.add("idx")

		cols = [c for c in _CHILD_ITEM_COLUMNS if c in existing_fields]
		if not cols:
			cols = ["parent", "name"]
		col_str = ", ".join(f"`{c}`" for c in cols)

		placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(parent_names))
		child_results = frappe.db.sql(
			f"SELECT {col_str} FROM `tab{child_doctype}` "
			f"WHERE `parent` IN ({placeholders}) ORDER BY `parent`, `idx`",
			tuple(parent_names),
			as_dict=True,
		)
		return child_results if child_results else None
	except Exception:
		return None


def _validate_sql(sql):
	"""Validate SQL for safety."""
	if not sql or not sql.strip():
		frappe.throw(_("No SQL query generated"))

	sql_upper = sql.upper().strip()

	# Must start with SELECT
	if not sql_upper.startswith("SELECT"):
		frappe.throw(_("Only SELECT queries are allowed"))

	# Strip string literals before checking keywords so that values like
	# "Grant Plastics Ltd." don't falsely trigger the GRANT keyword blocker
	sql_no_strings = re.sub(r"'[^']*'", "''", sql_upper)
	sql_no_strings = re.sub(r'"[^"]*"', '""', sql_no_strings)

	# Block dangerous keywords (checked against SQL without string values)
	dangerous = ["DROP", "DELETE", "UPDATE", "INSERT", "TRUNCATE", "ALTER", "CREATE", "GRANT", "REVOKE", "EXEC"]
	for keyword in dangerous:
		if re.search(rf"\b{keyword}\b", sql_no_strings):
			frappe.throw(_(f"Forbidden SQL operation: {keyword}"))

	# Block file operations
	if re.search(r"\b(INTO\s+OUTFILE|INTO\s+DUMPFILE|LOAD_FILE)\b", sql_no_strings):
		frappe.throw(_("File operations are not allowed in queries"))

	# Block INFORMATION_SCHEMA and system table access
	if re.search(r"\bINFORMATION_SCHEMA\b", sql_no_strings):
		frappe.throw(_("Access to INFORMATION_SCHEMA is not allowed"))
	if re.search(r"`?mysql`?\s*\.\s*`?\w+`?", sql_no_strings):
		frappe.throw(_("Access to mysql system tables is not allowed"))
	if re.search(r"`?performance_schema`?\s*\.\s*`?\w+`?", sql_no_strings):
		frappe.throw(_("Access to performance_schema is not allowed"))

	# Block multi-statement execution (semicolons not at the very end)
	# Strip trailing semicolon first, then check for any remaining semicolons
	sql_trimmed = sql.rstrip().rstrip(";")
	if ";" in sql_trimmed:
		frappe.throw(_("Multi-statement queries are not allowed"))

	# Block comment-based injection
	if re.search(r"(--|\#|/\*)", sql_no_strings):
		frappe.throw(_("SQL comments are not allowed in queries"))


def _execute_query(sql, max_rows):
	"""Execute query with row limit. Returns (results, from_cache)."""
	# Enforce LIMIT
	if "LIMIT" not in sql.upper():
		sql = sql.rstrip(";") + f" LIMIT {max_rows}"
	else:
		limit_match = re.search(r"LIMIT\s+(\d+)", sql, re.IGNORECASE)
		if limit_match and int(limit_match.group(1)) > max_rows:
			sql = re.sub(r"LIMIT\s+\d+", f"LIMIT {max_rows}", sql, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

	# ── Task 1.8: SQL result cache (user-scoped, 5-min TTL) ───────────────────
	sql_hash = hashlib.md5(sql.encode()).hexdigest()
	cache_key = f"genbi_sql_{sql_hash}_{frappe.session.user}"
	cached = frappe.cache().get_value(cache_key)
	if cached is not None:
		return cached, True

	results = frappe.db.sql(sql, as_dict=True)
	frappe.cache().set_value(cache_key, results, expires_in_sec=300)
	return results, False


# ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# Visualization
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────


def _is_numeric(v):
	"""Check if a value is numeric (handles int, float, Decimal from MySQL)."""
	from decimal import Decimal
	if isinstance(v, (int, float, Decimal)):
		return True
	if v is not None:
		s = str(v).strip()
		if s and s.replace('.', '', 1).replace('-', '', 1).isdigit():
			return True
	return False


def _prepare_visualization(data, query_plan):
	"""Prepare chart configuration from query results."""
	if not data:
		return {"chart_type": "table", "message": "No data found", "columns": [], "rows": []}

	chart_type = query_plan.get("chart_type", "table")
	keys = list(data[0].keys())

	# Single row with multiple numeric columns → bar chart (e.g. enabled=9, disabled=0)
	if len(data) == 1 and len(keys) > 1:
		row = data[0]
		numeric_cols = {k: v for k, v in row.items() if _is_numeric(v)}
		if len(numeric_cols) > 1 and chart_type != "table":
			labels = [str(k).replace("_", " ").title() for k in numeric_cols.keys()]
			values = [float(v) if v is not None else 0 for v in numeric_cols.values()]
			return {
				"chart_type": "bar",
				"title": query_plan.get("explanation", ""),
				"labels": labels,
				"datasets": [{"name": "Value", "values": values}],
				"columns": keys,
				"rows": _serialize_rows(data),
			}

	# Single number result (exactly 1 row, 1 column)
	if chart_type == "number" or (len(data) == 1 and len(keys) == 1):
		from decimal import Decimal
		value = list(data[0].values())[0]
		raw = float(value) if isinstance(value, Decimal) else value
		return {
			"chart_type": "number",
			"value": _format_number(value),
			"raw_value": raw,
			"label": query_plan.get("explanation", keys[0]),
		}

	# Table
	if chart_type == "table":
		return {
			"chart_type": "table",
			"columns": keys,
			"rows": _serialize_rows(data),
		}

	# Charts (bar, line, pie, donut)
	x_field = query_plan.get("x_field") or keys[0]
	y_field = query_plan.get("y_field") or (keys[1] if len(keys) > 1 else keys[0])

	# Ensure fields exist
	if x_field not in keys:
		x_field = keys[0]
	if y_field not in keys:
		y_field = keys[1] if len(keys) > 1 else keys[0]

	labels = []
	values = []
	for row in data:
		label = row.get(x_field, "")
		val = row.get(y_field, 0)
		labels.append(str(label) if label is not None else "")
		values.append(float(val) if val is not None else 0)

	return {
		"chart_type": chart_type,
		"title": query_plan.get("explanation", ""),
		"labels": labels,
		"datasets": [{"name": y_field, "values": values}],
		# Also include table data for drill-down
		"columns": keys,
		"rows": _serialize_rows(data),
	}


def _serialize_rows(data):
	"""Convert row data to JSON-safe format."""
	import datetime
	from decimal import Decimal

	rows = []
	for row in data:
		clean_row = {}
		for k, v in row.items():
			if isinstance(v, (datetime.date, datetime.datetime)):
				clean_row[k] = str(v)
			elif isinstance(v, Decimal):
				clean_row[k] = float(v)
			else:
				clean_row[k] = v
		rows.append(clean_row)
	return rows


def _format_number(value):
	"""Format a number for display."""
	if value is None:
		return "0"
	try:
		num = float(value)
		if num >= 10_000_000:
			return f"{num / 10_000_000:.2f} Cr"
		elif num >= 100_000:
			return f"{num / 100_000:.2f} L"
		elif num >= 1_000:
			return f"{num:,.0f}"
		elif num == int(num):
			return str(int(num))
		else:
			return f"{num:.2f}"
	except (ValueError, TypeError):
		return str(value)


# ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# Logging & Helpers
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────


def _persist_conversation_turn(session_id: str, question: str, result: dict) -> None:
	"""
	Task 1.5: Save a Q&A turn to ConversationState (Redis + GenBI Chat Session DocType).

	Called after every successful process_question() — both LLM and local engine paths.
	Never raises; a persistence failure must not break the chat response.
	"""
	try:
		from f_genbi.genbi.conversation import ConversationState

		# Extract the primary DocType from the SQL for the session base_doctype
		sql = result.get("sql") or ""
		m = re.search(r"FROM\s+`tab([^`]+)`", sql, re.I)
		base_doctype = m.group(1) if m else "GenBI"

		state = ConversationState.load(session_id, base_doctype)
		state.add_turn("user", question)
		state.add_turn("bot", {
			"explanation": result.get("explanation", ""),
			"sql": sql,
			"chart_type": (result.get("chart_config") or {}).get("chart_type", "table"),
			"result_count": result.get("result_count", 0),
		})
		state.save()
	except Exception:
		pass  # Never break the response due to persistence failure


@frappe.whitelist()
def record_query_feedback(question, sql="", rating="up"):
	"""Record 👍/👎 feedback for a query.

	For 👍: extracts the base DocType from the SQL and caches the question's
	key terms → DocType in Redis so EntityResolver picks them up at tier-1
	(highest priority) on the next similar query.
	"""
	try:
		if rating == "up" and sql:
			from f_genbi.genbi.entity_resolver import record_alias_feedback

			m = re.search(r"FROM\s+`tab([^`]+)`", sql, re.I)
			if m:
				doctype = m.group(1)
				# Cache the full (normalised) question as a confirmed mapping
				record_alias_feedback(question.strip().lower(), doctype)
				# Also cache each non-trivial word individually
				_STOP = {
					"show", "give", "get", "find", "fetch", "list", "the", "and",
					"for", "top", "last", "all", "what", "tell", "with", "from",
					"how", "many", "much", "are", "have", "has", "this", "that",
					"some", "our", "their", "your", "which", "when", "where",
				}
				words = [w for w in re.findall(r"\b[a-z]{4,}\b", question.lower()) if w not in _STOP]
				for w in words:
					record_alias_feedback(w, doctype)

			# 3.8: Promote positively-rated query → Training Example
			from f_genbi.genbi.training_loop import promote_feedback_to_training
			promote_feedback_to_training(question, sql)

	except Exception:
		pass
	return {"success": True}


def _log_query(question, sql="", chart_type="", explanation="", status="Pending", error_message="", result_count=0, execution_time=0):
	"""Log a query to GenBI Query Log."""
	try:
		doc = frappe.get_doc({
			"doctype": "GenBI Query Log",
			"question": question,
			"generated_sql": sql,
			"chart_type": chart_type,
			"explanation": explanation,
			"status": status,
			"user": frappe.session.user,
			"error_message": error_message,
			"result_count": result_count,
			"execution_time": execution_time,
		})
		doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
		frappe.db.commit()
		return doc
	except Exception:
		frappe.log_error("GenBI: Failed to log query")
		return None


def _get_error_suggestion(error_msg):
	"""Provide helpful suggestions for common errors."""
	error_lower = error_msg.lower()

	if "doesn't exist" in error_lower or "unknown column" in error_lower:
		return "The AI might have referenced a table or column that doesn't exist. Try rephrasing your question."
	elif "access denied" in error_lower or "permission" in error_lower:
		return "You might not have permission to access this data. Contact your administrator."
	elif "syntax" in error_lower:
		return "The generated SQL had a syntax issue. Try asking the question differently."
	elif "api" in error_lower or "key" in error_lower or "401" in error_lower:
		return "There seems to be an issue with the API key. Check GenBI Settings."
	elif "timeout" in error_lower:
		return "The request took too long. Try a simpler question."
	else:
		return "Try rephrasing your question or ask a simpler one."


def _get_templates():
	"""Pre-built query templates for quick insights."""
	return {
		"Top 10 Customers by Revenue": {
			"query": """
				SELECT `customer_name` as customer, SUM(`grand_total`) as total_revenue
				FROM `tabSales Invoice`
				WHERE `docstatus` = 1
				GROUP BY `customer_name`
				ORDER BY total_revenue DESC
				LIMIT 10
			""",
			"chart": "bar",
			"x_field": "customer",
			"y_field": "total_revenue",
		},
		"Monthly Sales Trend (Last 12 Months)": {
			"query": """
				SELECT DATE_FORMAT(`posting_date`, '%%Y-%%m') as month, SUM(`grand_total`) as revenue
				FROM `tabSales Invoice`
				WHERE `docstatus` = 1
				AND `posting_date` >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 12 MONTH)
				GROUP BY month
				ORDER BY month
			""",
			"chart": "line",
			"x_field": "month",
			"y_field": "revenue",
		},
		"Outstanding Receivables by Customer": {
			"query": """
				SELECT `customer_name` as customer, SUM(`outstanding_amount`) as outstanding
				FROM `tabSales Invoice`
				WHERE `docstatus` = 1 AND `outstanding_amount` > 0
				GROUP BY `customer_name`
				ORDER BY outstanding DESC
				LIMIT 20
			""",
			"chart": "bar",
			"x_field": "customer",
			"y_field": "outstanding",
		},
		"Top 10 Items by Quantity Sold": {
			"query": """
				SELECT sii.`item_name`, SUM(sii.`qty`) as total_qty
				FROM `tabSales Invoice Item` sii
				JOIN `tabSales Invoice` si ON si.`name` = sii.`parent`
				WHERE si.`docstatus` = 1
				GROUP BY sii.`item_name`
				ORDER BY total_qty DESC
				LIMIT 10
			""",
			"chart": "bar",
			"x_field": "item_name",
			"y_field": "total_qty",
		},
		"Total Sales This Month": {
			"query": """
				SELECT SUM(`grand_total`) as total
				FROM `tabSales Invoice`
				WHERE `docstatus` = 1
				AND MONTH(`posting_date`) = MONTH(CURDATE())
				AND YEAR(`posting_date`) = YEAR(CURDATE())
			""",
			"chart": "number",
			"x_field": "total",
			"y_field": "total",
		},
		"Low Stock Items (Below 10)": {
			"query": """
				SELECT b.`item_code`, b.`warehouse`, b.`actual_qty`
				FROM `tabBin` b
				WHERE b.`actual_qty` < 10 AND b.`actual_qty` > 0
				ORDER BY b.`actual_qty` ASC
				LIMIT 20
			""",
			"chart": "table",
		},
		"Purchase vs Sales (Last 6 Months)": {
			"query": """
				SELECT
					DATE_FORMAT(si.`posting_date`, '%%Y-%%m') as month,
					COALESCE(SUM(si.`grand_total`), 0) as sales
				FROM `tabSales Invoice` si
				WHERE si.`docstatus` = 1
				AND si.`posting_date` >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 6 MONTH)
				GROUP BY month
				ORDER BY month
			""",
			"chart": "line",
			"x_field": "month",
			"y_field": "sales",
		},
	}


# ── 10.4: Share API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist()
def create_share(question, sql, rows_json, chart_config_json="{}"):
	"""10.4: Persist a GenBI Shared Result and return its public URL."""
	import secrets
	import frappe.utils

	token = secrets.token_urlsafe(16)
	expires_at = frappe.utils.add_days(frappe.utils.now_datetime(), 7)

	doc = frappe.get_doc({
		"doctype": "GenBI Shared Result",
		"token": token,
		"question": question,
		"generated_sql": sql,
		"rows_json": rows_json,
		"chart_config_json": chart_config_json,
		"created_by": frappe.session.user,
		"expires_at": expires_at,
		"view_count": 0,
	})
	doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
	frappe.db.commit()

	site_url = frappe.utils.get_url()
	return {
		"token": token,
		"url": f"{site_url}/genbi/share/{token}",
		"expires_at": str(expires_at),
	}


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def get_shared_result(token):
	"""10.4: Fetch a shared result by token. Returns 404-style error when expired or not found."""
	import frappe.utils

	results = frappe.get_all(
		"GenBI Shared Result",
		filters={"token": token},
		fields=["name", "question", "generated_sql", "rows_json", "chart_config_json",
		        "created_by", "expires_at", "view_count"],
		limit=1,
	)
	if not results:
		frappe.throw(_("Shared result not found"), frappe.DoesNotExistError)

	row = results[0]
	if row.expires_at and frappe.utils.now_datetime() > row.expires_at:
		frappe.throw(_("This shared link has expired"), frappe.PermissionError)

	# Increment view counter asynchronously (best-effort)
	try:
		frappe.db.set_value("GenBI Shared Result", row.name, "view_count",
		                    (row.view_count or 0) + 1)
		frappe.db.commit()
	except Exception:
		pass

	return {
		"question": row.question,
		"sql": row.generated_sql,
		"rows": json.loads(row.rows_json or "[]"),
		"chart_config": json.loads(row.chart_config_json or "{}"),
		"created_by": row.created_by,
		"expires_at": str(row.expires_at),
	}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/api/schema_builder.py
import re

import frappe


def get_schema_for_doctypes(doctypes, question=None):
	"""Build schema for a pre-resolved list of DocTypes (skips keyword matching).

	Used when entity_resolver has already identified relevant DocTypes with
	high confidence, giving more accurate schema than keyword matching alone.

	Falls back to keyword-based ``get_relevant_schema`` when the resolved set
	is empty or entirely blocked by the allowed_doctypes whitelist.
	"""
	settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
	allowed_csv = (settings.allowed_doctypes_html or "").strip()
	allowed_set = None
	if allowed_csv:
		allowed_set = {d.strip() for d in allowed_csv.split(",") if d.strip()}

	relevant_doctypes = set(doctypes)

	if allowed_set:
		relevant_doctypes = relevant_doctypes & allowed_set
		if not relevant_doctypes:
			# All resolved entities blocked — fall back to keyword matching
			return get_relevant_schema(question or "")

	if not relevant_doctypes:
		return get_relevant_schema(question or "")

	detailed = _build_schema(relevant_doctypes)
	all_doctypes = _get_all_doctypes_list(allowed_set)
	return {"detailed_schema": detailed, "all_doctypes": all_doctypes}


def get_relevant_schema(question):
	"""Get relevant DocType schemas based on question keywords.

	Returns a dict with:
	- "detailed_schema": full field-level schema for keyword-matched DocTypes
	- "all_doctypes": lightweight list of ALL queryable DocTypes (name + table + module)

	The LLM gets full field info for likely-relevant tables, but can still
	query ANY table using the `tab{DocType}` naming convention.
	"""
	keyword_map = {
		r"sales|invoice|revenue|billing|sold|selling": [
			"Sales Invoice",
			"Sales Invoice Item",
			"Sales Order",
			"Sales Order Item",
		],
		r"purchase|supplier|vendor|buying|bought|procurement": [
			"Purchase Invoice",
			"Purchase Invoice Item",
			"Purchase Order",
			"Purchase Order Item",
			"Supplier",
		],
		r"customer|client|party|debtor": [
			"Customer",
			"Customer Group",
		],
		r"contact|phone|email|mobile|address": [
			"Contact",
			"Dynamic Link",
			"Address",
		],
		r"territory|region|area|location|country": [
			"Customer",
			"Territory",
		],
		r"stock|inventory|item|product|warehouse|bin|quantity": [
			"Item",
			"Bin",
			"Warehouse",
			"Stock Entry",
			"Stock Ledger Entry",
		],
		r"employee|hr|payroll|salary|attendance|leave": [
			"Employee",
			"Salary Slip",
			"Attendance",
			"Leave Application",
		],
		r"payment|receipt|outstanding|bank|transaction": [
			"Payment Entry",
			"Journal Entry",
			"GL Entry",
		],
		r"expense|cost|claim": [
			"Expense Claim",
		],
		r"order|quotation|quote": [
			"Quotation",
			"Quotation Item",
			"Sales Order",
			"Purchase Order",
		],
		r"delivery|shipping|dispatch": [
			"Delivery Note",
			"Delivery Note Item",
		],
		r"account|ledger|journal|profit|loss|balance": [
			"Account",
			"GL Entry",
			"Journal Entry",
		],
		r"project|task|timesheet": [
			"Project",
			"Task",
			"Timesheet",
		],
		r"lead|opportunity|crm|pipeline": [
			"Lead",
			"Opportunity",
		],
		r"doctype|field|link|schema|metadata|which.+use|which.+have|which.+contain|uses\s+\w+|linked.+to|relationship|reference": [
			"DocType",
			"DocField",
		],
	}

	# Check allowed DocTypes from settings
	settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
	allowed_csv = (settings.allowed_doctypes_html or "").strip()
	allowed_set = None
	if allowed_csv:
		allowed_set = {d.strip() for d in allowed_csv.split(",") if d.strip()}

	# Keyword-matched DocTypes get full schema
	relevant_doctypes = set()
	question_lower = question.lower()

	for keywords, doctypes in keyword_map.items():
		if re.search(keywords, question_lower):
			relevant_doctypes.update(doctypes)

	# Detect person/entity lookup queries (e.g. "details of Alison", "about Grant Plastics")
	# These need Contact, Customer, Employee, Supplier schemas even without keyword match
	person_patterns = r"detail|about|info|who is|find|search|look up|contact of|address of"
	if re.search(person_patterns, question_lower):
		relevant_doctypes.update(["Customer", "Contact", "Dynamic Link", "Address", "Employee", "Supplier"])

	# Default DocTypes if no keyword match
	if not relevant_doctypes:
		relevant_doctypes = {
			"Sales Invoice",
			"Purchase Invoice",
			"Item",
			"Customer",
			"Supplier",
			"Contact",
			"Dynamic Link",
			"Payment Entry",
			"GL Entry",
		}

	# Filter by allowed DocTypes
	if allowed_set:
		relevant_doctypes = relevant_doctypes & allowed_set
		if not relevant_doctypes:
			relevant_doctypes = allowed_set

	# Build result: detailed schema + lightweight list of ALL DocTypes
	detailed = _build_schema(relevant_doctypes)
	all_doctypes = _get_all_doctypes_list(allowed_set)

	return {
		"detailed_schema": detailed,
		"all_doctypes": all_doctypes,
	}


def _get_all_doctypes_list(allowed_set=None):
	"""Get lightweight list of queryable DocTypes grouped by module.

	Returns a compact dict: {"Selling": ["Customer", "Sales Order", ...], ...}
	This is much smaller than a full list and helps the LLM know what tables exist.
	"""
	# Skip system/internal/UI-only modules
	skip_modules = {
		"Core", "Custom", "Desk", "Integrations", "Email", "Printing",
		"Workflow", "Website", "Automation", "Event Streaming", "Social",
		"Contacts", "Geo", "Data Migration",
	}

	filters = {"istable": 0, "issingle": 0}
	doctypes = frappe.get_all(
		"DocType",
		filters=filters,
		fields=["name", "module"],
		limit=500,
		order_by="module, name",
	)

	by_module = {}
	for d in doctypes:
		if d.module in skip_modules:
			continue
		if allowed_set and d.name not in allowed_set:
			continue
		by_module.setdefault(d.module, []).append(d.name)

	return by_module


def _build_schema(doctypes):
	"""Build structured schema info for LLM consumption."""
	schema = {}

	# Add hardcoded metadata schemas for DocType/DocField (core tables not introspectable via get_meta the same way)
	if "DocType" in doctypes:
		schema["DocType"] = {
			"table_name": "tabDocType",
			"note": "Not submittable: do NOT filter by docstatus. This is the metadata table listing all DocTypes in the system.",
			"fields": [
				{"name": "name", "type": "Data", "label": "DocType Name (ID)"},
				{"name": "module", "type": "Data", "label": "Module"},
				{"name": "issingle", "type": "Check", "label": "Is Single (1=singleton, 0=normal)"},
				{"name": "istable", "type": "Check", "label": "Is Child Table (1=child, 0=standalone)"},
				{"name": "is_submittable", "type": "Check", "label": "Is Submittable"},
				{"name": "creation", "type": "Datetime", "label": "Created On"},
				{"name": "modified", "type": "Datetime", "label": "Modified On"},
			],
		}
		doctypes = doctypes - {"DocType"}

	if "DocField" in doctypes:
		schema["DocField"] = {
			"table_name": "tabDocField",
			"note": "Not submittable: do NOT filter by docstatus. This table stores field definitions for all DocTypes. Use it to find which DocTypes have fields linking to a specific entity (e.g. Customer, Supplier, Item).",
			"fields": [
				{"name": "name", "type": "Data", "label": "Field ID"},
				{"name": "parent", "type": "Data", "label": "Parent DocType name (e.g. 'Sales Invoice')"},
				{"name": "fieldname", "type": "Data", "label": "Field name in database"},
				{"name": "label", "type": "Data", "label": "Field display label"},
				{"name": "fieldtype", "type": "Data", "label": "Field type (Link, Data, Currency, Select, etc.)"},
				{"name": "options", "type": "Data", "label": "For Link fields: the linked DocType name (e.g. 'Customer', 'Supplier'). For Select fields: the options list."},
				{"name": "reqd", "type": "Check", "label": "Is Required (1=yes, 0=no)"},
				{"name": "idx", "type": "Int", "label": "Field order index"},
			],
		}
		doctypes = doctypes - {"DocField"}

	useful_fieldtypes = {
		"Currency",
		"Int",
		"Float",
		"Date",
		"Datetime",
		"Link",
		"Select",
		"Data",
		"Long Int",
		"Percent",
		"Check",
		"Small Text",
	}

	for doctype in doctypes:
		try:
			meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
		except Exception:
			continue

		fields = []
		for f in meta.fields:
			if f.fieldtype not in useful_fieldtypes:
				continue
			field_info = {
				"name": f.fieldname,
				"type": f.fieldtype,
				"label": f.label or f.fieldname,
			}
			if f.fieldtype == "Link" and f.options:
				field_info["linked_to"] = f.options
			if f.fieldtype == "Select" and f.options:
				field_info["options"] = f.options.split("\n")[:10]
			fields.append(field_info)

		is_submittable = bool(meta.is_submittable)

		# Add standard fields
		standard_fields = [
			{"name": "name", "type": "Data", "label": "ID"},
			{"name": "creation", "type": "Datetime", "label": "Created On"},
			{"name": "modified", "type": "Datetime", "label": "Modified On"},
			{"name": "owner", "type": "Data", "label": "Created By"},
		]

		if is_submittable:
			standard_fields.append(
				{"name": "docstatus", "type": "Int", "label": "Document Status (0=Draft, 1=Submitted, 2=Cancelled)"},
			)

		schema[doctype] = {
			"table_name": f"tab{doctype}",
			"note": "Submittable: use WHERE docstatus=1 for submitted records" if is_submittable else "Not submittable: do NOT filter by docstatus",
			"fields": standard_fields + fields,
		}

	return schema

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/doctype/canvas_session/canvas_session.py
# Copyright (c) 2026, Dipesh Patel and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt

import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document
import uuid
import json


class CanvasSession(Document):
	def before_validate(self):
		"""Generate UUID for session_id if not provided - runs before validation"""
		if not self.session_id:
			self.session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]  # Short UUID for user-friendly URLs

	def before_insert(self):
		"""Initialize fields before inserting"""
		if not self.created_at:
			self.created_at = frappe.utils.now()

		if not self.last_activity:
			self.last_activity = frappe.utils.now()

		# Initialize JSON fields if empty
		if not self.canvas_state:
			self.canvas_state = json.dumps({
				"nodes": [],
				"edges": [],
				"positions": {}
			})

		if not self.chat_messages:
			self.chat_messages = json.dumps([])

	def on_update(self):
		"""Update last_activity timestamp on every save"""
		if not self.flags.skip_last_activity_update:
			frappe.db.set_value(
				"Canvas Session",
				self.name,
				"last_activity",
				frappe.utils.now(),
				update_modified=False
			)

	def has_permission(self, ptype, user=None):
		"""Custom permission check"""
		if not user:
			user = frappe.session.user

		# Owner always has full access
		if self.owner == user:
			return True

		# Public sessions: everyone can read/write
		if self.is_public:
			return True

		# Check if user is in collaborators list
		for collab in self.collaborators:
			if collab.user == user:
				if ptype == "read":
					return True
				elif ptype == "write":
					return collab.role == "Editor"

		return False


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_session(title, base_doctype, canvas_state=None):
	"""Create a new canvas session"""
	# Generate a unique session ID
	session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]

	doc = frappe.get_doc({
		"doctype": "Canvas Session",
		"session_id": session_id,
		"title": title,
		"base_doctype": base_doctype,
		"canvas_state": canvas_state or json.dumps({
			"nodes": [],
			"edges": [],
			"positions": {}
		}),
		"is_public": 0,  # Sessions are private by default
		"owner": frappe.session.user
	})
	doc.insert()
	frappe.db.commit()

	return {
		"session_id": doc.session_id,
		"name": doc.name,
		"title": doc.title
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_session(session_id):
	"""Get canvas session by ID"""
	try:
		doc = frappe.get_doc("Canvas Session", session_id)

		# Check permission
		if not doc.has_permission("read"):
			frappe.throw("You do not have permission to access this session")

		return {
			"name": doc.name,
			"session_id": doc.session_id,
			"title": doc.title,
			"base_doctype": doc.base_doctype,
			"canvas_state": json.loads(doc.canvas_state) if doc.canvas_state else {},
			"chat_messages": json.loads(doc.chat_messages) if doc.chat_messages else [],
			"is_public": doc.is_public,
			"collaborators": [
				{
					"user": c.user,
					"role": c.role,
					"joined_at": c.joined_at
				}
				for c in doc.collaborators
			],
			"owner": doc.owner,
			"created_at": doc.created_at,
			"creation": doc.creation,
			"last_activity": doc.last_activity
		}
	except frappe.DoesNotExistError:
		frappe.throw(f"Canvas Session {session_id} not found")


@frappe.whitelist()
def update_canvas_state(session_id, canvas_state=None, chat_messages=None, timestamp=None):
	"""Update canvas session state and broadcast to all users"""
	import time
	from f_genbi.canvas_session_realtime import publish_canvas_update

	doc = frappe.get_doc("Canvas Session", session_id)

	# Check permission
	if not doc.has_permission("write"):
		frappe.throw("You do not have permission to edit this session")

	# Track if canvas_state was updated (for broadcasting)
	canvas_state_updated = False
	parsed_canvas_state = None

	if canvas_state is not None:
		if isinstance(canvas_state, str):
			canvas_state = json.loads(canvas_state)
		doc.canvas_state = json.dumps(canvas_state)
		canvas_state_updated = True
		parsed_canvas_state = canvas_state

	if chat_messages is not None:
		if isinstance(chat_messages, str):
			chat_messages = json.loads(chat_messages)
		doc.chat_messages = json.dumps(chat_messages)

	doc.save()
	frappe.db.commit()

	# Broadcast canvas state update to all users in session
	if canvas_state_updated:
		if not timestamp:
			timestamp = int(time.time() * 1000)  # Milliseconds since epoch

		publish_canvas_update(
			session_id=session_id,
			canvas_state=parsed_canvas_state,
			user=frappe.session.user,
			timestamp=timestamp
		)

	return {"success": True, "timestamp": timestamp}


@frappe.whitelist()
def add_collaborator(session_id, user, role="Editor"):
	"""Add a collaborator to the session"""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("Canvas Session", session_id)

	# Only owner can add collaborators
	if doc.owner != frappe.session.user:
		frappe.throw("Only the session owner can add collaborators")

	# Check if user already exists
	for collab in doc.collaborators:
		if collab.user == user:
			frappe.throw(f"User {user} is already a collaborator")

	# Add new collaborator
	doc.append("collaborators", {
		"user": user,
		"role": role,
		"joined_at": frappe.utils.now()
	})

	doc.save()
	frappe.db.commit()

	return {"success": True}


@frappe.whitelist()
def remove_collaborator(session_id, user):
	"""Remove a collaborator from the session"""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("Canvas Session", session_id)

	# Only owner can remove collaborators
	if doc.owner != frappe.session.user:
		frappe.throw("Only the session owner can remove collaborators")

	# Find and remove collaborator
	for idx, collab in enumerate(doc.collaborators):
		if collab.user == user:
			doc.collaborators.pop(idx)
			break

	doc.save()
	frappe.db.commit()

	return {"success": True}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_my_sessions():
	"""Get all sessions owned by or shared with current user"""
	user = frappe.session.user

	# Sessions owned by user
	owned_sessions = frappe.get_all(
		"Canvas Session",
		filters={"owner": user},
		fields=["name", "session_id", "title", "base_doctype", "created_at", "last_activity", "is_public"],
		order_by="last_activity desc"
	)

	# Sessions shared with user
	shared_session_names = frappe.get_all(
		"Canvas Session Collaborator",
		filters={"user": user},
		fields=["parent"],
		pluck="parent"
	)

	shared_sessions = []
	if shared_session_names:
		shared_sessions = frappe.get_all(
			"Canvas Session",
			filters={"name": ["in", shared_session_names]},
			fields=["name", "session_id", "title", "base_doctype", "created_at", "last_activity", "is_public", "owner"],
			order_by="last_activity desc"
		)

	# Public sessions (exclude owned and already shared)
	public_sessions = frappe.get_all(
		"Canvas Session",
		filters={
			"is_public": 1,
			"owner": ["!=", user],
			"name": ["not in", shared_session_names] if shared_session_names else ["is", "not null"]
		},
		fields=["name", "session_id", "title", "base_doctype", "created_at", "last_activity", "owner"],
		order_by="last_activity desc",
		limit=20
	)

	return {
		"owned": owned_sessions,
		"shared": shared_sessions,
		"public": public_sessions
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_active_sessions(doctype):
	"""Get all active sessions for a specific doctype that user can access"""
	user = frappe.session.user

	# Sessions owned by user for this doctype
	owned_sessions = frappe.get_all(
		"Canvas Session",
		filters={
			"owner": user,
			"base_doctype": doctype
		},
		fields=["name", "session_id", "title", "base_doctype", "owner", "creation", "last_activity", "is_public"],
		order_by="last_activity desc",
		limit=20
	)

	# Sessions shared with user for this doctype
	shared_session_names = frappe.get_all(
		"Canvas Session Collaborator",
		filters={"user": user},
		fields=["parent"],
		pluck="parent"
	)

	shared_sessions = []
	if shared_session_names:
		shared_sessions = frappe.get_all(
			"Canvas Session",
			filters={
				"name": ["in", shared_session_names],
				"base_doctype": doctype
			},
			fields=["name", "session_id", "title", "base_doctype", "owner", "creation", "last_activity", "is_public"],
			order_by="last_activity desc"
		)

	# Public sessions for this doctype
	public_filters = {
		"is_public": 1,
		"base_doctype": doctype,
		"owner": ["!=", user]
	}

	# Exclude sessions already in owned or shared lists
	if shared_session_names:
		public_filters["name"] = ["not in", shared_session_names]

	public_sessions = frappe.get_all(
		"Canvas Session",
		filters=public_filters,
		fields=["name", "session_id", "title", "base_doctype", "owner", "creation", "last_activity"],
		order_by="last_activity desc",
		limit=20
	)

	# Combine all sessions
	all_sessions = owned_sessions + shared_sessions + public_sessions

	return all_sessions


@frappe.whitelist()
def add_chat_message(session_id, message):
	"""Add a chat message to the session"""
	doc = frappe.get_doc("Canvas Session", session_id)

	# Check permission
	if not doc.has_permission("write"):
		frappe.throw("You do not have permission to send messages in this session")

	# Load existing messages
	chat_messages = json.loads(doc.chat_messages) if doc.chat_messages else []

	# Create new message
	new_message = {
		"user": frappe.session.user,
		"full_name": frappe.utils.get_fullname(frappe.session.user),
		"message": message,
		"timestamp": frappe.utils.now()
	}

	# Add to messages
	chat_messages.append(new_message)

	# Save back to doc
	doc.chat_messages = json.dumps(chat_messages)
	doc.flags.skip_last_activity_update = False  # Update last activity
	doc.save()

	# First commit the database changes
	frappe.db.commit()

	# Then broadcast to all users in this session using Frappe's doc room format
	frappe.publish_realtime(
		event='canvas_chat_message',
		message=new_message,
		room=f'doc:Canvas Session/{session_id}'
	)

	return new_message


@frappe.whitelist()
def join_canvas_session(session_id):
	"""Join a canvas session and get online users"""
	try:
		doc = frappe.get_doc("Canvas Session", session_id)
	except frappe.DoesNotExistError:
		frappe.throw(f"Canvas Session {session_id} not found")

	user = frappe.session.user

	# Get or create online users cache key
	cache_key = f"canvas_session_online_users_{session_id}"
	online_users = frappe.cache().get_value(cache_key) or {}

	# Remove any existing entry for this user first
	if user in online_users:
		del online_users[user]

	# Add current user to online users with fresh timestamp
	online_users[user] = {
		"user": user,
		"full_name": frappe.utils.get_fullname(user),
		"joined_at": frappe.utils.now(),
		"last_seen": frappe.utils.now()
	}

	# Clean up stale users (inactive for more than 5 minutes)
	current_time = frappe.utils.now_datetime()
	stale_users = []
	for u, data in list(online_users.items()):
		last_seen = frappe.utils.get_datetime(data.get("last_seen", data["joined_at"]))
		minutes_inactive = (current_time - last_seen).total_seconds() / 60
		if minutes_inactive > 5:
			stale_users.append(u)

	for u in stale_users:
		del online_users[u]

	# Cache for 1 hour
	frappe.cache().set_value(cache_key, online_users, expires_in_sec=3600)

	# Auto-add user as collaborator if not owner and not already a collaborator
	if doc.owner != user:
		existing_collaborator = False
		for collab in doc.collaborators:
			if collab.user == user:
				existing_collaborator = True
				break

		if not existing_collaborator:
			doc.append('collaborators', {
				'user': user,
				'role': 'Editor',
				'joined_at': frappe.utils.now()
			})

	# Update last activity
	doc.last_activity = frappe.utils.now()
	doc.flags.skip_permission_check = True
	doc.save()

	# Broadcast join event
	room_name = f'doc:Canvas Session/{session_id}'
	event_message = {
		"user": user,
		"full_name": frappe.utils.get_fullname(user),
		"session_id": session_id
	}

	frappe.db.commit()

	frappe.publish_realtime(
		event='user_joined',
		message=event_message,
		room=room_name
	)

	return {
		"online_users": list(online_users.values()),
		"session_id": session_id
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def leave_canvas_session(session_id):
	"""Leave a canvas session"""
	user = frappe.session.user

	# Get online users cache
	cache_key = f"canvas_session_online_users_{session_id}"
	online_users = frappe.cache().get_value(cache_key) or {}

	# Remove current user
	if user in online_users:
		del online_users[user]

	# Update cache
	frappe.cache().set_value(cache_key, online_users, expires_in_sec=3600)

	# Broadcast leave event using Frappe's doc room format
	frappe.publish_realtime(
		event='user_left',
		message={
			"user": user,
			"full_name": frappe.utils.get_fullname(user),
			"session_id": session_id
		},
		room=f'doc:Canvas Session/{session_id}'
	)

	return {"status": "left", "session_id": session_id}

import frappe

from frappe.model.document import Document


class ExcelWorkbook(Document):
	def before_insert(self):
		# Always set owner to the creating user — never trust the client.
		self.owner = frappe.session.user

	def before_delete(self):
		# Only the owner or a System Manager may delete a workbook.
		if frappe.session.user != self.owner and not frappe.has_role("System Manager"):
			frappe.throw(_("You can only delete your own saved views."))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/doctype/genbi_query_log/test_genbi_query_log.py
import frappe
from frappe.tests import IntegrationTestCase


class TestGenBIQueryLog(IntegrationTestCase):
	def test_query_log_creation(self):
		"""Should be able to create a query log entry."""
		doc = frappe.get_doc({
			"doctype": "GenBI Query Log",
			"question": "Test question",
			"status": "Pending",
			"user": "Administrator",
		})
		doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

		self.assertTrue(doc.name)
		self.assertEqual(doc.question, "Test question")
		self.assertEqual(doc.status, "Pending")

		doc.delete()

	def test_query_log_fields(self):
		"""GenBI Query Log should have all required fields."""
		meta = frappe.get_meta("GenBI Query Log")
		field_names = [f.fieldname for f in meta.fields]
		self.assertIn("question", field_names)
		self.assertIn("generated_sql", field_names)
		self.assertIn("chart_type", field_names)
		self.assertIn("status", field_names)
		self.assertIn("user", field_names)
		self.assertIn("execution_time", field_names)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/doctype/genbi_settings/genbi_settings.js
// GenBI Settings — form client script
// Adds: Test Connection button, Browse Models button (Ollama), model picker dialog

frappe.ui.form.on("GenBI Settings", {
	refresh(frm) {
		frm.add_custom_button(__("Test Connection"), () => test_connection(frm), __("Actions"));
		frm.add_custom_button(__("Browse Models"), () => browse_models(frm), __("Actions"));
		_update_connection_indicator(frm, null);

		const neural_providers = ["Neural (Local)", "Neural + RPG Boost", "Neural + Lightning"];
		if (neural_providers.includes(frm.doc.llm_provider)) {
			_render_neural_telemetry_card(frm);
		}
	},

	llm_provider(frm) {
		_update_connection_indicator(frm, null);
		const neural_providers = ["Neural (Local)", "Neural + RPG Boost", "Neural + Lightning"];
		if (neural_providers.includes(frm.doc.llm_provider)) {
			_render_neural_telemetry_card(frm);
		} else {
			frm.fields_dict.neural_confidence_threshold?.$wrapper
				?.closest(".form-section")?.find(".genbi-telemetry-card").remove();
		}
	},
});

// ── Test Connection ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function test_connection(frm) {
	const provider = frm.doc.llm_provider;
	if (!provider) {
		frappe.msgprint(__("Select an LLM Provider first."));
		return;
	}

	const btn_label = __("Test Connection");
	frm.disable_save();

	frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Testing connection…"), indicator: "blue" }, 3);

	frappe.call({
		method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.doctype.genbi_settings.genbi_settings.test_connection",
		args: {
			provider,
			api_key:      frm.doc.api_key  || "",
			api_base_url: frm.doc.api_base_url || "",
			model_name:   frm.doc.model_name || "",
		},
		callback(r) {
			frm.enable_save();
			const result = r.message;
			if (result?.success) {
				_update_connection_indicator(frm, "success");
				frappe.show_alert({ message: result.message, indicator: "green" }, 6);
			} else {
				_update_connection_indicator(frm, "error");
				frappe.msgprint({
					title: __("Connection Failed"),
					message: frappe.utils.escape_html(result?.message || __("Unknown error")),
					indicator: "red",
				});
			}
		},
		error() {
			frm.enable_save();
			_update_connection_indicator(frm, "error");
			frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Request failed."), indicator: "red" }, 4);
		},
	});
}

// ── Browse / Pick Model ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function browse_models(frm) {
	const provider = frm.doc.llm_provider;
	if (!provider || provider === "None (Local NLP Engine)") {
		frappe.msgprint(__("No external models for the Local NLP Engine."));
		return;
	}

	frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Fetching model list…"), indicator: "blue" }, 3);

	frappe.call({
		method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.doctype.genbi_settings.genbi_settings.get_available_models",
		args: {
			provider,
			api_base_url: frm.doc.api_base_url || "",
		},
		callback(r) {
			const { models = [], source } = r.message || {};
			if (!models.length) {
				frappe.msgprint(__("No models found. For Ollama, ensure the server is running and at least one model is pulled."));
				return;
			}
			_show_model_picker_dialog(frm, models, source);
		},
		error() {
			frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Could not fetch models."), indicator: "red" }, 4);
		},
	});
}

function _show_model_picker_dialog(frm, models, source) {
	const current = frm.doc.model_name || "";
	const source_label = source === "ollama"
		? __("Models from your local Ollama server")
		: __("Recommended models for {0}", [frm.doc.llm_provider]);

	const d = new frappe.ui.Dialog({
		title: __("Pick a Model"),
		fields: [
			{
				fieldtype: "HTML",
				fieldname: "source_info",
				options: `<p class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px;margin-bottom:10px;">${frappe.utils.escape_html(source_label)}</p>`,
			},
			{
				fieldtype: "Select",
				fieldname: "model",
				label: __("Model"),
				options: models.join("\n"),
				default: current,
				reqd: 1,
			},
		],
		primary_action_label: __("Use This Model"),
		primary_action(values) {
			frm.set_value("model_name", values.model);
			_update_connection_indicator(frm, null);  // reset after model change
			d.hide();
			frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Model set to {0}", [values.model]), indicator: "green" }, 3);
		},
	});
	d.show();
}

// ── Neural Engine Telemetry Card ──────────────────────────────────────────────

function _render_neural_telemetry_card(frm) {
	const $field = frm.fields_dict.neural_confidence_threshold?.$wrapper;
	if (!$field) return;

	$field.find(".genbi-telemetry-card").remove();

	const $card = $(`
		<div class="genbi-telemetry-card" style="margin-top:16px;padding:12px 16px;
			border:1px solid var(--border-color);border-radius:6px;background:var(--fg-color);">
			<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:8px;color:var(--text-color);">
				${__("Neural Engine — Last 7 Days")}
				<span class="telemetry-loading" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;color:var(--text-muted);margin-left:6px;">${__("loading…")}</span>
			</div>
			<div class="telemetry-body"></div>
		</div>
	`).appendTo($field);

	frappe.call({
		method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_neural_telemetry",
		args: { days: 7 },
		callback(r) {
			$card.find(".telemetry-loading").hide();
			const data = r.message || {};
			const layers = data.layers || {};
			const total  = data.total_queries || 0;

			if (!total) {
				$card.find(".telemetry-body").html(
					`<p style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:12px;margin:0;">
						${__("No queries recorded yet.")}
					</p>`
				);
				return;
			}

			const LAYER_LABELS = {
				"neural-memory":  __("Vector Memory"),
				"pattern":        __("Pattern Engine"),
				"schema-compiler":__("Schema Compiler"),
				"neural-slot":    __("Neural Slot"),
				"neural-seq2seq": __("T5-small Seq2Seq"),
				"clarification":  __("Clarification"),
			};

			let rows = "";
			for (const [layer, stats] of Object.entries(layers)) {
				if (!stats.hits && !stats.misses) continue;
				rows += `<tr>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;">${LAYER_LABELS[layer] || layer}</td>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${stats.hits}</td>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${stats.hit_rate}</td>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${stats.avg_ms ? stats.avg_ms + "ms" : "—"}</td>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${stats.avg_confidence}</td>
				</tr>`;
			}

			$card.find(".telemetry-body").html(`
				<table style="width:100%;font-size:12px;border-collapse:collapse;">
					<thead>
						<tr style="color:var(--text-muted);">
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:left;">${__("Layer")}</th>
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${__("Hits")}</th>
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${__("Hit Rate")}</th>
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${__("Avg ms")}</th>
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${__("Confidence")}</th>
						</tr>
					</thead>
					<tbody>${rows}</tbody>
					<tfoot>
						<tr style="border-top:1px solid var(--border-color);color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;">
							<td colspan="5" style="padding:4px 8px;">
								${__("Total queries (last 7 days):")} <strong>${total}</strong>
							</td>
						</tr>
					</tfoot>
				</table>
			`);
		},
	});
}

// ── Connection indicator helper ───────────────────────────────────────────────

function _update_connection_indicator(frm, status) {
	// status: null | "success" | "error"
	const $indicator = frm.fields_dict.sb_llm_config?.$wrapper?.find(".genbi-conn-indicator");

	if (!status) {
		$indicator?.remove();
		return;
	}

	const color  = status === "success" ? "#28a745" : "#dc3545";
	const icon   = status === "success" ? "✓" : "✗";
	const label  = status === "success" ? __("Connected") : __("Failed");

	// Remove old, add new
	frm.fields_dict.sb_llm_config?.$wrapper?.find(".genbi-conn-indicator").remove();
	frm.fields_dict.sb_llm_config?.$wrapper?.prepend(
		`<div class="genbi-conn-indicator" style="
			display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;
			font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:${color};
			padding:4px 10px;border-radius:12px;
			background:${color}18;border:1px solid ${color}40;
			margin-bottom:8px;">
			${icon} ${frappe.utils.escape_html(label)}
		</div>`
	);
}

// ── RPG + Lightning Status Card ───────────────────────────────────────────────

frappe.ui.form.on("GenBI Settings", {
	llm_provider(frm) {
		const rpg_providers = ["Neural + RPG Boost", "Neural + Lightning"];
		if (rpg_providers.includes(frm.doc.llm_provider)) {
			_render_integration_status_card(frm);
		} else {
			frm.fields_dict.neural_confidence_threshold?.$wrapper
				?.find(".genbi-integration-card").remove();
		}
	},
	refresh(frm) {
		const rpg_providers = ["Neural + RPG Boost", "Neural + Lightning"];
		if (rpg_providers.includes(frm.doc.llm_provider)) {
			_render_integration_status_card(frm);
		}
		_render_usage_summary_card(frm);
	},
});

function _render_integration_status_card(frm) {
	const $field = frm.fields_dict.neural_confidence_threshold?.$wrapper;
	if (!$field) return;

	$field.find(".genbi-integration-card").remove();

	const is_rpg = frm.doc.llm_provider === "Neural + RPG Boost";
	const is_lightning = frm.doc.llm_provider === "Neural + Lightning";

	const $card = $(`
		<div class="genbi-integration-card" style="margin-top:16px;padding:12px 16px;
			border:1px solid var(--border-color);border-radius:6px;background:var(--fg-color);">
			<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:8px;color:var(--text-color);">
				${is_rpg ? __("frappe_rpg Integration Status") : __("f_lightning Integration Status")}
				<span class="int-loading" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;color:var(--text-muted);margin-left:6px;">${__("checking…")}</span>
			</div>
			<div class="int-body"></div>
		</div>
	`).appendTo($field);

	frappe.call({
		method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.doctype.genbi_settings.genbi_settings.get_integration_status",
		args: { provider: frm.doc.llm_provider },
		callback(r) {
			$card.find(".int-loading").hide();
			const d = r.message || {};
			const rows = [];

			if (is_rpg) {
				rows.push(_status_row(__("frappe_rpg app"), d.frappe_rpg_installed));
				rows.push(_status_row(__("frappe_rs Rust module"), d.frappe_rs_available));
				rows.push(_status_row(__("Holt-Winters forecasting"), d.holtwinters_available));
				rows.push(_status_row(__("query_plan / query_rewrite"), d.query_plan_available));
				rows.push(_status_row(__("L1 in-process cache"), d.cache_available));
				rows.push(_status_row(__("Polars aggregation"), d.polars_available));
			} else {
				rows.push(_status_row(__("f_lightning app"), d.f_lightning_installed));
				rows.push(_status_row(__("MeiliSearch sidecar"), d.meilisearch_reachable));
				rows.push(_status_row(__("Go NLP service"), d.nlp_service_reachable));
			}

			$card.find(".int-body").html(
				`<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px;">
					${rows.join("")}
				</table>`
			);
		},
	});
}

function _status_row(label, ok) {
	const color = ok ? "#28a745" : "#dc3545";
	const icon  = ok ? "✓" : "✗";
	return `<tr>
		<td style="padding:3px 6px;color:var(--text-muted);">${frappe.utils.escape_html(label)}</td>
		<td style="padding:3px 6px;color:${color};font-weight:600;">${icon} ${ok ? __("OK") : __("Not available")}</td>
	</tr>`;
}

// ── 10.8: Monthly usage summary card ──────────────────────────────────────────

function _render_usage_summary_card(frm) {
	const $anchor = frm.fields_dict.enable_query_logging?.$wrapper;
	if (!$anchor) return;

	$anchor.find(".genbi-usage-card").remove();

	const $card = $(`
		<div class="genbi-usage-card" style="
			margin:12px 0;
			padding:12px 16px;
			background:var(--card-bg,#fff);
			border:1px solid var(--border-color,#d1d8dd);
			border-radius:6px;
		">
			<div style="font-weight:600;margin-bottom:8px;color:var(--heading-color);">
				📊 ${__("This Month's Usage")}
			</div>
			<div class="usage-body" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-muted);">${__("Loading…")}</div>
		</div>
	`);
	$anchor.append($card);

	frappe.call({
		method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_usage_summary",
		callback(r) {
			if (!r.message) return;
			const d = r.message;
			const rows_html = (d.rows || []).map(row => `
				<tr>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;">${frappe.utils.escape_html(row.provider || "")}</td>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${(row.calls || 0).toLocaleString()}</td>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${((row.tokens_in || 0) + (row.tokens_out || 0)).toLocaleString()}</td>
					<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">$${(row.cost_usd || 0).toFixed(4)}</td>
				</tr>
			`).join("");
			$card.find(".usage-body").html(`
				<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;">
					<thead>
						<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color);">
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:left;">${__("Provider")}</th>
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${__("Calls")}</th>
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${__("Tokens")}</th>
							<th style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">${__("Cost")}</th>
						</tr>
					</thead>
					<tbody>${rows_html}</tbody>
					<tfoot>
						<tr style="border-top:1px solid var(--border-color);font-weight:600;">
							<td style="padding:3px 8px;" colspan="2">${__("Total")}: ${(d.total_calls || 0).toLocaleString()} ${__("calls")}</td>
							<td></td>
							<td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;">$${(d.total_cost_usd || 0).toFixed(4)}</td>
						</tr>
					</tfoot>
				</table>
			`);
		},
	});
}

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_available_models(provider, api_base_url=None):
	"""
	Return a list of available models for the given provider.

	- Ollama: queries the local /api/tags endpoint dynamically.
	- Cloud providers: returns a curated static list.
	- Custom / None: returns empty list.
	"""
	if provider in _KNOWN_MODELS:
		return {"models": _KNOWN_MODELS[provider], "source": "static"}

	if provider == "Ollama (Local)":
		base = (api_base_url or "http://localhost:11434").rstrip("/")
		try:
			import requests
			resp = requests.get(f"{base}/api/tags", timeout=5)
			resp.raise_for_status()
			data = resp.json()
			models = [m.get("name", "") for m in data.get("models", []) if m.get("name")]
			return {"models": models, "source": "ollama"}
		except Exception as exc:
			frappe.throw(_("Could not reach Ollama at {0}: {1}").format(base, str(exc)))

	return {"models": [], "source": "none"}


@frappe.whitelist()
def test_connection(provider, api_key=None, api_base_url=None, model_name=None):
	"""
	Send a minimal prompt to the configured LLM and return a pass/fail result.
	Reuses the same HTTP logic as query_engine to test the real code path.
	"""
	if not provider or provider == "None (Local NLP Engine)":
		return {
			"success": True,
			"message": _("Local NLP Engine is active — no external connection needed."),
		}

	# Build a minimal settings-like object
	class _FakeSettings:
		llm_provider   = provider
		api_key        = api_key or ""
		api_base_url   = api_base_url or ""
		model_name     = model_name or ""
		temperature    = 0.0
		max_tokens     = 20

	settings = _FakeSettings()

	probe = "Reply with exactly the word: OK"

	try:
		from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import _call_llm
		messages = [{"role": "user", "content": probe}]
		reply = _call_llm(probe, messages, settings)
		if reply:
			return {
				"success": True,
				"message": _("Connected successfully. Model replied: {0}").format(
					str(reply).strip()[:120]
				),
			}
		return {"success": False, "message": _("Model returned an empty response.")}

	except Exception as exc:
		return {"success": False, "message": str(exc)}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_integration_status(provider: str) -> dict:
	"""Return installation / connectivity status for RPG Boost or Lightning provider."""
	if provider == "Neural + RPG Boost":
		from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.rpg_bridge import get_rpg
		rs = get_rpg()

		frappe_rpg_installed = False
		try:
			import frappe_rpg  # noqa: F401
			frappe_rpg_installed = True
		except ImportError:
			pass

		holtwinters_available = rs is not None and hasattr(rs, "holtwinters_forecast")
		query_plan_available = rs is not None and hasattr(rs, "query_plan")
		cache_available = rs is not None and hasattr(rs, "query_cache_get")
		polars_available = rs is not None and hasattr(rs, "aggregate_sales_data")

		return {
			"frappe_rpg_installed": frappe_rpg_installed,
			"frappe_rs_available": rs is not None,
			"holtwinters_available": holtwinters_available,
			"query_plan_available": query_plan_available,
			"cache_available": cache_available,
			"polars_available": polars_available,
		}

	if provider == "Neural + Lightning":
		f_lightning_installed = False
		try:
			import f_lightning  # noqa: F401
			f_lightning_installed = True
		except ImportError:
			pass

		meilisearch_reachable = False
		nlp_service_reachable = False
		if f_lightning_installed:
			try:
				from f_lightning.api.nlp_client import get_client  # type: ignore[import]
				client = get_client()
				meilisearch_reachable = client.ping_meilisearch()
				nlp_service_reachable = client.ping_nlp()
			except Exception:
				pass

		return {
			"f_lightning_installed": f_lightning_installed,
			"meilisearch_reachable": meilisearch_reachable,
			"nlp_service_reachable": nlp_service_reachable,
		}

	return {}
