			sql = (
				f"SELECT b.`item_code`, i.`item_name`, b.`warehouse`,"
				f" b.`actual_qty`, b.`valuation_rate`,"
				f" b.`actual_qty` * b.`valuation_rate` AS `stock_value`"
				f" FROM `tabBin` b"
				f" JOIN `tabItem` i ON i.`name` = b.`item_code`"
				f" WHERE b.`actual_qty` > 0 AND i.`disabled` = 0"
				f" ORDER BY `stock_value` DESC LIMIT {lim}"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", f"Top {lim} items by stock value")

		# ── HR ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

		# employees by department
		if re.search(r"\bemployee.*\bby\s+department\b|\bdepartment.wise\s+employee"
					 r"|\bhow\s+many\s+employee|\bcount\b.*\bemployee.*\bdepartment\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `department`, COUNT(`name`) AS `employee_count`"
				" FROM `tabEmployee`"
				" WHERE `status` = 'Active'"
				" GROUP BY `department` ORDER BY `employee_count` DESC"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "bar", "Employees by department")

		# employee list
		if re.search(r"\blist\b.*\bemployee|\bemployee.*\blist\b|\ball\s+employee"
					 r"|\bshow\b.*\bemployee(?!.*department)", q):
			lim = self._limit(q, 50)
			sql = (
				f"SELECT `name`, `employee_name`, `department`,"
				f" `designation`, `date_of_joining`, `status`"
				f" FROM `tabEmployee` WHERE `status` = 'Active'"
				f" ORDER BY `employee_name` LIMIT {lim}"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Active employees")

		# ── CRM ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

		# leads by status / source
		if re.search(r"\blead\b.*\bby\s+(status|source)\b|\b(status|source).wise\s+lead\b", q):
			grp = "source" if re.search(r"\bsource\b", q) else "status"
			sql = (
				f"SELECT `{grp}`, COUNT(`name`) AS `lead_count`"
				f" FROM `tabLead`"
				f" GROUP BY `{grp}` ORDER BY `lead_count` DESC"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "bar", f"Leads by {grp}")

		# open opportunities
		if re.search(r"\bopen\s+opportunit\b|\bopportunit\b.*\bopen\b"
					 r"|\bpending\s+opportunit\b|\bactive\s+opportunit\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `name`, `opportunity_from`, `party_name`,"
				" `opportunity_amount`, `status`, `expected_closing`"
				" FROM `tabOpportunity`"
				" WHERE `status` NOT IN ('Closed','Lost')"
				" ORDER BY `expected_closing` ASC LIMIT 50"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Open opportunities")

		# ── Finance ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

		# accounts receivable / payable summary
		if re.search(r"\baccounts?\s+receivable\b|\breceivable\s+summar\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `customer_name`, SUM(`outstanding_amount`) AS `total_outstanding`,"
				" COUNT(`name`) AS `invoice_count`"
				" FROM `tabSales Invoice`"
				" WHERE `docstatus` = 1 AND `outstanding_amount` > 0"
				" GROUP BY `customer_name` ORDER BY `total_outstanding` DESC LIMIT 50"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Accounts receivable summary")

		if re.search(r"\baccounts?\s+payable\b|\bpayable\s+summar\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `supplier_name`, SUM(`outstanding_amount`) AS `total_outstanding`,"
				" COUNT(`name`) AS `bill_count`"
				" FROM `tabPurchase Invoice`"
				" WHERE `docstatus` = 1 AND `outstanding_amount` > 0"
				" GROUP BY `supplier_name` ORDER BY `total_outstanding` DESC LIMIT 50"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Accounts payable summary")

		# payment collections this month / period
		if re.search(r"\bpayment\s+(collected|received|collection)\b"
					 r"|\bcollection\b.*\bpayment\b"
					 r"|\bhow\s+much\s+(was\s+)?collected\b", q):
			df = self._date_filter(q, "posting_date")
			where = f"docstatus = 1 AND payment_type = 'Receive'{' AND ' + df if df else ''}"
			sql = (
				f"SELECT `party` AS `customer`,"
				f" SUM(`paid_amount`) AS `total_collected`,"
				f" COUNT(`name`) AS `payment_count`"
				f" FROM `tabPayment Entry`"
				f" WHERE {where}"
				f" GROUP BY `party` ORDER BY `total_collected` DESC LIMIT 50"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Payment collections")

		# ── Sales Order pipeline ─────────────────────────────────────────────

		# sales orders by status
		if re.search(r"\bsales?\s+order\b.*\bby\s+status\b|\bstatus.wise\s+sales?\s+order\b"
					 r"|\bsales?\s+order\s+status\b|\bpipeline\b.*\bsales?\s+order\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `status`, COUNT(`name`) AS `order_count`,"
				" SUM(`grand_total`) AS `total_value`"
				" FROM `tabSales Order`"
				" WHERE `docstatus` = 1"
				" GROUP BY `status` ORDER BY `order_count` DESC"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "bar", "Sales orders by status")

		# pending sales orders (to be delivered)
		if re.search(r"\bpending\s+(?:sales?\s+)?order\b|\bundelivered\s+order\b"
					 r"|\bsales?\s+order\b.*\bpending\b|\border\b.*\bnot\s+delivered\b", q):
			lim = self._limit(q, 50)
			sql = (
				f"SELECT `name`, `customer_name`, `transaction_date`,"
				f" `delivery_date`, `grand_total`, `status`"
				f" FROM `tabSales Order`"
				f" WHERE `docstatus` = 1"
				f" AND `status` NOT IN ('Closed','Completed','Cancelled')"
				f" ORDER BY `delivery_date` ASC LIMIT {lim}"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Pending sales orders")

		# ── 4.9: Audit Trail Queries ─────────────────────────────────────────────

		# Who changed [document]?
		if re.search(r"\b(who\s+(changed|modified|edited|updated)|change\s+histor|audit\s+(trail|log)|version\s+histor)\b", q):
			doctype_hint = ""
			for known in ["sales invoice", "purchase invoice", "sales order", "purchase order",
			              "customer", "supplier", "item", "employee"]:
				if known in q:
					doctype_hint = known.title()
					break
			if doctype_hint:
				sql = (
					f"SELECT `name`, `creation`, `owner`, `data`"
					f" FROM `tabVersion`"
					f" WHERE `ref_doctype` = '{doctype_hint}'"
					f" ORDER BY `creation` DESC LIMIT 20"
				)
				return self._result(question, sql, "table", f"Version history: {doctype_hint}")
			else:
				sql = (
					"SELECT `name`, `ref_doctype`, `docname`, `creation`, `owner`"
					" FROM `tabVersion`"
					" ORDER BY `creation` DESC LIMIT 20"
				)
				return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Recent document changes")

		# Workflow history / approvals
		if re.search(r"\b(workflow\s+(action|history|approval)|who\s+approved|approved\s+by|rejection\s+reason)\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `name`, `workflow_state`, `action`, `user`,"
				" `creation`, `reference_doctype`, `reference_name`"
				" FROM `tabWorkflow Action`"
				" ORDER BY `creation` DESC LIMIT 30"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Workflow approvals")

		# Login / session history
		if re.search(r"\b(login\s+(histor|log|activit)|who\s+logged\s+in|last\s+login|access\s+log)\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `user`, `login_datetime`, `status`, `user_type`, `browser`"
				" FROM `tabActivity Log`"
				" WHERE `operation` = 'Login'"
				" ORDER BY `login_datetime` DESC LIMIT 50"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Recent logins")

		# Error log
		if re.search(r"\b(error\s+log|system\s+error|recent\s+error|frappe\s+error)\b", q):
			sql = (
				"SELECT `name`, `creation`, `method`, `error`"
				" FROM `tabError Log`"
				" ORDER BY `creation` DESC LIMIT 20"
			)
			return self._result(question, sql, "table", "Recent system errors")

		return None

	# ── result builder ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	@staticmethod
	def _result(question: str, sql: str, chart_type: str, label: str) -> dict:
		return {
			"_pattern_sql":   sql,
			"_pattern_chart": chart_type,
			"_pattern_label": label,
			"_question":      question,
		}


# ── Main engine ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class LocalNLPEngine:
	"""No-LLM SQL generation using the genbi/ NLP module chain."""

	# Map high-level intents from classifier → SQLTemplateEngine intents
	_INTENT_MAP = {
		"ANALYZE_DATA":  "AGGREGATE",
		"FIND_PATH":     "JOIN",
		"BUILD_CANVAS":  "LIST",
		"REFINE":        "LIST",
		"SUGGEST":       None,        # Not a SQL intent
		"EXPLAIN":       None,        # Not a SQL intent
	}

	def process(self, question: str, chat_history: list | None = None) -> dict:
		"""
		Convert a natural language question to SQL + visualization without any LLM.

		Returns a dict compatible with query_engine.process_question().
		"""
		import time
		start = time.time()

		# ── Fast path: pattern-based engine (no ML, very reliable) ──────────
		pattern_hit = PatternEngine().match(question)
		if pattern_hit:
			sql = pattern_hit["_pattern_sql"]
			chart_type = pattern_hit["_pattern_chart"]
			label = pattern_hit["_pattern_label"]
			try:
				from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import _validate_sql, _execute_query, _prepare_visualization
				_validate_sql(sql)
				results, from_cache = _execute_query(sql, max_rows=500)
			except Exception as exc:
				return {"success": False, "error": str(exc), "sql": sql}
			chart_config = _prepare_visualization(results, {
				"chart_type": chart_type,
				"explanation": label,
			})
			elapsed = round(time.time() - start, 3)
			count = len(results)
			return {
				"success": True,
				"question": question,
				"explanation": f"{label} — {count} record{'s' if count != 1 else ''} found.",
				"sql": sql,
				"data": results,
				"chart_config": chart_config,
				"result_count": count,
				"execution_time": elapsed,
				"from_cache": from_cache,
				"engine": "local-pattern",
			}

		# ── NLP pipeline path ────────────────────────────────────────────────
		try:
			parse, intent_key, entities = self._run_nlp_pipeline(question)
		except Exception as exc:
			return self._unsupported(question, detail=str(exc))

		sql_intent = self._resolve_sql_intent(intent_key, question)
		if sql_intent is None:
			return self._unsupported(question)

		try:
			from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.schema_builder import get_schema_for_doctypes, get_relevant_schema
			if entities:
				schema = get_schema_for_doctypes(entities, question=question)
			else:
				schema = get_relevant_schema(question)
		except Exception:
			schema = {}

		engine = SQLTemplateEngine(
			intent=sql_intent,
			parse=parse,
			schema=schema,
			question=question,
		)
		sql = engine.build()

		if not sql:
			return self._unsupported(question)

		try:
			from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import _validate_sql, _execute_query, _prepare_visualization
			_validate_sql(sql)
			results, from_cache = _execute_query(sql, max_rows=200)
		except Exception as exc:
			return {
				"success": False,
				"error": str(exc),
				"sql": sql,
			}

		from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import _prepare_visualization
		chart_config = _prepare_visualization(results, {
			"chart_type": self._pick_chart_type(sql_intent, results),
			"explanation": question,
		})

		elapsed = round(time.time() - start, 3)
		return {
			"success": True,
			"question": question,
			"explanation": self._make_summary(question, results, sql_intent),
			"sql": sql,
			"data": results,
			"chart_config": chart_config,
			"result_count": len(results),
			"execution_time": elapsed,
			"from_cache": from_cache,
			"engine": "local",
		}

	# ── NLP pipeline ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	def _run_nlp_pipeline(self, question: str) -> tuple[dict, str, list]:
		"""Run intent + entity + parse. Returns (parse_dict, intent_str, entity_list)."""
		from f_genbi.genbi.intent_classifier import IntentClassifier
		from f_genbi.genbi.entity_resolver import EntityResolver

		intent_key, _, _ = IntentClassifier().classify(question)

		resolver = EntityResolver()
		entity_hits = resolver.extract_entities(question)
		entities = [e["doctype"] for e in entity_hits if e.get("confidence", 0) >= 0.7]

		# Build a minimal parse dict for SQLTemplateEngine
		parse = {"entities": entities, "filters": [], "group_by": {}, "aggregates": []}

		try:
			from f_genbi.genbi.query_parser import QueryParser
			if entities:
				full_parse = QueryParser().parse(question)
				parse.update({
					"filters":   full_parse.get("filters", []),
					"group_by":  full_parse.get("group_by", {}),
					"aggregates": full_parse.get("aggregates", []),
				})
				if full_parse.get("entities") and not entities:
					parse["entities"] = full_parse["entities"]
		except Exception:
			pass  # QueryParser optional — continue with minimal parse

		return parse, intent_key, entities

	def _resolve_sql_intent(self, intent_key: str, question: str) -> str | None:
		"""Map NLP intent + question keywords → SQLTemplateEngine intent."""
		q = question.lower()

		# Keyword-first overrides (more reliable than semantic intent for simple patterns)
		if re.search(r"\btrend\b|\bmonthly\b|\bover time\b|\bby month\b|\bhistory\b", q):
			return "TREND"
		if re.search(r"\bcompare\b|\bvs\b|\bversus\b|\bthis vs\b|\bthis month vs\b", q):
			return "COMPARE"
		if re.search(r"\btotal\b|\bsum\b|\bcount\b|\bhow many\b|\baverage\b|\bby \w+\b", q):
			return "AGGREGATE"
		if re.search(r"\bdetails? of\b|\babout\b|\bfind\b|\bsearch\b|\blook up\b", q):
			return "LOOKUP"

		# Fall through to classifier intent
		return self._INTENT_MAP.get(intent_key, "LIST")

	# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	def _pick_chart_type(self, sql_intent: str, results: list) -> str:
		if not results:
			return "table"
		if sql_intent in ("TREND", "COMPARE"):
			return "line"
		if sql_intent == "AGGREGATE" and len(results) > 1:
			return "bar"
		if len(results) == 1 and len(results[0]) == 1:
			return "number"
		return "table"

	def _make_summary(self, question: str, results: list, sql_intent: str) -> str:
		if not results:
			return "No records found matching your query."
		count = len(results)
		if sql_intent == "AGGREGATE" and count == 1:
			vals = list(results[0].values())
			return f"Result: {vals[-1]:,}" if isinstance(vals[-1], (int, float)) else str(vals[-1])
		if sql_intent == "TREND":
			return f"Found {count} months of data."
		return f"Found {count} record{'s' if count != 1 else ''}."

	def _unsupported(self, question: str, detail: str = "") -> dict:
		msg = (
			"I can handle simple queries offline (lists, totals, trends, lookups). "
			"For complex analysis, please configure an LLM provider in GenBI Settings."
		)
		if detail:
			msg += f" ({detail})"
		return {
			"success": True,
			"question": question,
			"explanation": msg,
			"sql": None,
			"data": [],
			"chart_config": None,
			"result_count": 0,
			"execution_time": 0,
			"from_cache": False,
			"engine": "local",
		}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/model_registry.py
"""
model_registry.py — ONNX model singletons, lazy-load, hot-swap.

Models are stored in sites/{site}/neural_models/ so they survive bench update.
All public functions return None gracefully if the model file is absent.
"""

import json
import os
import threading

import frappe

_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_slot_filler_session = None   # ort.InferenceSession | None
_t5_session = None            # ort.InferenceSession | None


def _model_dir() -> str:
    return os.path.join(frappe.get_site_path(), "neural_models")


def _slot_filler_path() -> str:
    return os.path.join(_model_dir(), "slot_filler.onnx")


def _t5_model_path() -> str:
    return os.path.join(_model_dir(), "t5_genbi.onnx")


def _metadata_path() -> str:
    return os.path.join(_model_dir(), "model_metadata.json")


def is_neural_mode_available() -> bool:
    """True only when the slot-filler ONNX model file is present on disk."""
    return os.path.isfile(_slot_filler_path())


def get_slot_filler():
    """Return a cached ort.InferenceSession for the slot filler, or None."""
    global _slot_filler_session
    if _slot_filler_session is not None:
        return _slot_filler_session
    if not os.path.isfile(_slot_filler_path()):
        return None
    with _LOCK:
        if _slot_filler_session is not None:
            return _slot_filler_session
        try:
            import onnxruntime as ort
            _slot_filler_session = ort.InferenceSession(
                _slot_filler_path(),
                providers=["CPUExecutionProvider"],
            )
            frappe.logger().info("NeuralEngine: slot_filler.onnx loaded")
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().warning(f"NeuralEngine: failed to load slot_filler.onnx — {e}")
            _slot_filler_session = None
    return _slot_filler_session


def get_t5_model():
    """Return a cached ort.InferenceSession for T5-small, or None."""
    global _t5_session
    if _t5_session is not None:
        return _t5_session
    if not os.path.isfile(_t5_model_path()):
        return None
    with _LOCK:
        if _t5_session is not None:
            return _t5_session
        try:
            import onnxruntime as ort
            _t5_session = ort.InferenceSession(
                _t5_model_path(),
                providers=["CPUExecutionProvider"],
            )
            frappe.logger().info("NeuralEngine: t5_genbi.onnx loaded")
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().warning(f"NeuralEngine: failed to load t5_genbi.onnx — {e}")
            _t5_session = None
    return _t5_session


def reload():
    """Hot-swap: drop cached sessions so next call re-loads from disk."""
    global _slot_filler_session, _t5_session
    with _LOCK:
        _slot_filler_session = None
        _t5_session = None
    frappe.logger().info("NeuralEngine: model registry cleared — will reload on next use")


def get_model_metadata() -> dict:
    """Return metadata JSON for the deployed model, or an empty dict."""
    path = _metadata_path()
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        return {}
    try:
        with open(path) as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return {}


def prewarm():
    """Called from on_startup — loads ONNX sessions eagerly so first query is fast."""
    if os.path.isfile(_slot_filler_path()):
        get_slot_filler()
    if os.path.isfile(_t5_model_path()):
        get_t5_model()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/neural_engine.py
"""
neural_engine.py — Offline neural SQL generation engine.

Completely separate from LocalNLPEngine — selected via 'Neural (Local)' provider.
Orchestrates four layers:
  Layer 0: PatternEngine  (regex, ~40% of queries, 100% accurate)
  Layer 1: Schema Compiler (pure rules + live Frappe meta, Day-1 without any model)
  Layer 2: Neural Slot Filler + NeuralSQLBuilder (ONNX, ~20ms)
  Layer 3: T5-small Seq2Seq (ONNX, ~500ms, optional)
  Layer 4: Interactive Clarification (deterministic dialog, frontend-driven)
"""

import time

import frappe

from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi import model_registry
from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.telemetry import record_hit, record_miss, record_error


_NOT_DEPLOYED_MSG = (
    "Neural Engine model not deployed yet. "
    "Place slot_filler.onnx in sites/{site}/neural_models/ to activate. "
    "Meanwhile the Schema Compiler (Layer 1) is active and handles common queries."
)


class NeuralEngine:
    """
    Standalone no-LLM SQL generation engine using neural models.
    Selected via 'Neural (Local)' provider option in GenBI Settings.
    local_engine.py is never imported or modified.
    """

    def process(self, question: str, chat_history: list | None = None) -> dict:
        """Synchronous path — returns a full result dict."""
        start = time.time()
        chat_history = chat_history or []

        result = self._run_layers(question, chat_history)
        result["execution_time"] = round(time.time() - start, 3)
        result["engine"] = result.get("engine", "neural")
        return result

    def stream(self, question: str, chat_history: list | None = None):
        """
        Generator — yields stage-event dicts in order:
          {"event": "schema_ready",  "doctype": ..., "fields": [...]}
          {"event": "sql_ready",     "sql": ..., "layer": ...}
          {"event": "data_ready",    "rows": [...], "columns": [...]}
          {"event": "done",          ...full result dict...}
          {"event": "error",         "message": ...}

        Matches the SSE contract used by stream_question() in query_engine.py.
        """
        start = time.time()
        chat_history = chat_history or []

        try:
            # ── DuckDB layer: window functions / PIVOT / running totals ───────
            duckdb_result = self._try_duckdb(question)
            if duckdb_result:
                yield {"event": "sql_ready",
                       "sql": duckdb_result.get("sql", ""),
                       "layer": "duckdb"}
                rows = duckdb_result.get("data", [])
                cols = duckdb_result.get("columns", [])
                yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                duckdb_result["execution_time"] = round(time.time() - start, 3)
                yield {"event": "done", **duckdb_result}
                return

            # ── Lightning pre-layer: MeiliSearch LOOKUP bypass ────────────────
            lightning_result = self._try_lightning(question)
            if lightning_result:
                yield {"event": "sql_ready",
                       "sql": lightning_result.get("sql", ""),
                       "layer": "lightning-search"}
                rows = lightning_result.get("data", [])
                cols = lightning_result.get("columns", [])
                yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                lightning_result["execution_time"] = round(time.time() - start, 3)
                yield {"event": "done", **lightning_result}
                return

            # ── Pre-layer: Query Decomposer (AND/OR/BUT NOT) ──────────────────
            decomp_result = self._try_decomposer(question, chat_history)
            if decomp_result:
                yield {"event": "sql_ready",
                       "sql": decomp_result["sql"], "layer": "neural-decomposer"}
                rows = decomp_result["data"]
                cols = decomp_result.get("columns", [])
                yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                decomp_result["execution_time"] = round(time.time() - start, 3)
                yield {"event": "done", **decomp_result}
                return

            # ── Layer 0.5: FAISS Vector Memory ───────────────────────────────
            t0 = time.time()
            memory_result = self._try_vector_memory(question)
            if memory_result:
                record_hit("neural-memory", (time.time()-t0)*1000, memory_result["confidence"])
                yield {"event": "sql_ready", "sql": memory_result["sql"], "layer": "neural-memory"}
                rows, cols = self._execute(memory_result["sql"])
                yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                done = self._build_done(question, memory_result["sql"], rows, cols,
                                        "neural-memory", memory_result["confidence"], start)
                yield {"event": "done", **done}
                return
            record_miss("neural-memory")

            # ── Layer 0: PatternEngine ────────────────────────────────────────
            t0 = time.time()
            pattern_result = self._try_pattern_engine(question)
            if pattern_result:
                record_hit("pattern", (time.time()-t0)*1000, 1.0)
                sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(pattern_result["sql"])
                yield {"event": "sql_ready", "sql": sql, "layer": "pattern"}
                rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
                yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                done = self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols, "pattern", 1.0, start)
                yield {"event": "done", **done}
                return
            record_miss("pattern")

            # ── Layer 1: Schema Compiler ──────────────────────────────────────
            t0 = time.time()
            schema_result = self._try_schema_compiler(question, chat_history)
            if schema_result:
                conf = schema_result.get("confidence", 0.78)
                record_hit("schema-compiler", (time.time()-t0)*1000, conf)
                sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(schema_result["sql"])
                yield {"event": "schema_ready",
                       "doctype": schema_result.get("doctype"),
                       "fields": schema_result.get("fields", [])}
                yield {"event": "sql_ready", "sql": sql, "layer": "schema-compiler"}
                rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
                yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                done = self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols,
                                        "schema-compiler", conf, start)
                yield {"event": "done", **done}
                return
            record_miss("schema-compiler")

            # ── Layer 2: Neural Slot Filler ───────────────────────────────────
            if model_registry.is_neural_mode_available():
                t0 = time.time()
                slot_result = self._try_slot_filler(question, chat_history)
                if slot_result and slot_result.get("confidence", 0) >= 0.80:
                    conf = slot_result["confidence"]
                    record_hit("neural-slot", (time.time()-t0)*1000, conf)
                    sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(slot_result["sql"])
                    yield {"event": "schema_ready",
                           "doctype": slot_result.get("entity"),
                           "fields": []}
                    yield {"event": "sql_ready", "sql": sql, "layer": "neural-slot"}
                    rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
                    yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                    done = self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols,
                                            "neural-slot", conf, start,
                                            slots=slot_result.get("slots"))
                    yield {"event": "done", **done}
                    return
                record_miss("neural-slot")

            # ── Layer 3: T5-small Seq2Seq ─────────────────────────────────────
            if model_registry.get_t5_model():
                t0 = time.time()
                seq2seq_result = self._try_seq2seq(question)
                if seq2seq_result:
                    conf = seq2seq_result.get("confidence", 0.70)
                    record_hit("neural-seq2seq", (time.time()-t0)*1000, conf)
                    sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(seq2seq_result["sql"])
                    yield {"event": "sql_ready", "sql": sql, "layer": "neural-seq2seq"}
                    rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
                    yield {"event": "data_ready", "rows": rows, "columns": cols}
                    done = self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols,
                                            "neural-seq2seq", conf, start)
                    yield {"event": "done", **done}
                    return
                record_miss("neural-seq2seq")

            # ── Layer 4: Clarification fallback ──────────────────────────────
            yield {
                "event": "done",
                "success": True,
                "question": question,
                "explanation": "I need a bit more context to answer this. Please use the clarification dialog.",
                "sql": None,
                "data": [],
                "columns": [],
                "chart_config": None,
                "result_count": 0,
                "engine": "neural-clarification",
                "needs_clarification": True,
                "execution_time": round(time.time() - start, 3),
                "confidence": 0.0,
            }

        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().error(f"NeuralEngine.stream error: {e}")
            yield {"event": "error", "message": str(e)}

    # ── Private layer runners ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _run_layers(self, question: str, chat_history: list) -> dict:
        # DuckDB layer: window functions / PIVOT / running totals / percentile
        r = self._try_duckdb(question)
        if r:
            return r

        # Lightning pre-layer: MeiliSearch LOOKUP bypass + Go NLP filter pre-parse
        r = self._try_lightning(question)
        if r:
            return r

        # Pre-layer: Query Decomposer
        r = self._try_decomposer(question, chat_history)
        if r:
            return r

        t0 = time.time()

        # Layer 0.5: FAISS vector memory
        r = self._try_vector_memory(question)
        if r:
            record_hit("neural-memory", (time.time()-t0)*1000, r["confidence"])
            rows, cols = self._execute(r["sql"])
            return self._build_done(question, r["sql"], rows, cols,
                                    "neural-memory", r["confidence"], time.time())
        record_miss("neural-memory")

        # Layer 0: PatternEngine
        t0 = time.time()
        r = self._try_pattern_engine(question)
        if r:
            record_hit("pattern", (time.time()-t0)*1000, 1.0)
            sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(r["sql"])
            rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
            return self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols, "pattern", 1.0, time.time())
        record_miss("pattern")

        # Layer 1: Schema Compiler
        t0 = time.time()
        r = self._try_schema_compiler(question, chat_history)
        if r:
            conf = r.get("confidence", 0.78)
            record_hit("schema-compiler", (time.time()-t0)*1000, conf)
            sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(r["sql"])
            rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
            return self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols,
                                    "schema-compiler", conf, time.time())
        record_miss("schema-compiler")

        # Layer 2: Neural Slot Filler
        if model_registry.is_neural_mode_available():
            t0 = time.time()
            r = self._try_slot_filler(question, chat_history)
            if r and r.get("confidence", 0) >= 0.80:
                conf = r["confidence"]
                record_hit("neural-slot", (time.time()-t0)*1000, conf)
                sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(r["sql"])
                rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
                return self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols,
                                        "neural-slot", conf, time.time(),
                                        slots=r.get("slots"))
            record_miss("neural-slot")

        # Layer 3: T5-small Seq2Seq
        if model_registry.get_t5_model():
            t0 = time.time()
            r = self._try_seq2seq(question)
            if r:
                conf = r.get("confidence", 0.70)
                record_hit("neural-seq2seq", (time.time()-t0)*1000, conf)
                sql = self._maybe_rewrite_sql(r["sql"])
                rows, cols = self._execute(sql)
                return self._build_done(question, sql, rows, cols,
                                        "neural-seq2seq", conf, time.time())
            record_miss("neural-seq2seq")

        # Layer 4 — model not deployed yet or all layers failed
        if not model_registry.is_neural_mode_available():
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": _NOT_DEPLOYED_MSG,
                "question": question,
                "data": [],
                "sql": None,
                "chart_config": None,
                "result_count": 0,
            }

        return {
            "success": True,
            "question": question,
            "explanation": "I need a bit more context. Please use the clarification dialog.",
            "sql": None,
            "data": [],
            "columns": [],
            "chart_config": None,
            "result_count": 0,
            "engine": "neural-clarification",
            "needs_clarification": True,
            "confidence": 0.0,
        }

    def _try_duckdb(self, question: str) -> dict | None:
        """
        9.2 — DuckDB routing layer.
        Activated when the question contains window-function intent keywords:
        running total, rank, percentile, pivot, cumulative, lag, lead.
        """
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.duckdb_engine import (
                is_duckdb_query, execute_duckdb, mysql_base_sql_for,
            )
            if not is_duckdb_query(question):
                return None

            # Try to resolve doctype from vocabulary
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.rs_bridge import scan_question
            doctype = scan_question(question) or "Sales Invoice"

            mysql_sql = mysql_base_sql_for(question, doctype)
            result = execute_duckdb(question, doctype, mysql_sql)
            if result is None:
                return None  # no template matched — fall through to other layers

            rows, cols = result
            n = len(rows)
            return {
                "success": True,
                "question": question,
                "explanation": f"Computed {n} record{'s' if n != 1 else ''} using DuckDB in-process analytics.",
                "sql": mysql_sql,
                "data": rows,
                "columns": cols,
                "chart_config": None,
                "result_count": n,
                "engine": "neural:duckdb",
                "confidence": 1.0,
                "from_cache": False,
            }
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine._try_duckdb: {e}")
            return None

    @staticmethod
    def _is_lightning_active() -> bool:
        try:
            settings = frappe.get_cached_doc("GenBI Settings")
            return getattr(settings, "llm_provider", "") == "Neural + Lightning"
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _try_lightning(self, question: str) -> dict | None:
        """
        Lightning layer — runs before all other layers when provider is 'Neural + Lightning'.

        8.3: LOOKUP intent → MeiliSearch result bypasses SQL entirely.
        8.4: Non-LOOKUP → Go NLP pre-parse injects filters into NeuralSQLBuilder.
        8.5: Indian currency amounts parsed via Go ParseAmount (python fallback).
        8.6: Result carries engine='lightning-search' for the source badge.
        """
        if not self._is_lightning_active():
            return None

        from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.lightning_client import (
            call_lightning_search, call_lightning_nlp_parse,
            is_lookup_intent, filters_to_where, is_lightning_available,
        )

        if not is_lightning_available():
            frappe.logger().debug("NeuralEngine: Lightning provider selected but sidecar unreachable — falling back to Neural path")
            return None

        try:
            # ── 8.5: pre-parse amount tokens using Go ParseAmount ─────────────
            # (handled inside lightning_client.py via _py_parse_amount fallback)

            # ── 8.3: LOOKUP intent → MeiliSearch bypass ───────────────────────
            parsed = call_lightning_nlp_parse(question)
            if parsed and is_lookup_intent(parsed):
                search_result = call_lightning_search(question, parsed.get("DocType"))
                if search_result and search_result.get("hits"):
                    hits = search_result["hits"]
                    # Normalise MeiliSearch hit dicts to plain row dicts
                    rows = [dict(h) for h in hits]
                    cols = list(rows[0].keys()) if rows else []
                    took_ms = search_result.get("took_ms", 0)
                    return {
                        "success": True,
                        "question": question,
                        "explanation": (
                            f"Found {len(rows)} result{'s' if len(rows) != 1 else ''} "
                            f"via MeiliSearch — live data ({took_ms} ms)."
                        ),
                        "sql": None,
                        "data": rows,
                        "columns": cols,
                        "chart_config": None,
                        "result_count": len(rows),
                        "engine": "lightning-search",
                        "confidence": 1.0,
                        "from_cache": False,
                        "lightning_source": "meilisearch",
                        "lightning_took_ms": took_ms,
                    }

            # ── 8.4: Non-LOOKUP → pre-inject Go NLP filters into SQL path ─────
            if parsed:
                filters = parsed.get("Filters") or parsed.get("filters") or []
                if filters:
                    pre_where = filters_to_where(filters)
                    if pre_where:
                        # Store pre-parsed WHERE on the thread-local so NeuralSQLBuilder can pick it up
                        frappe.local.genbi_lightning_where = pre_where
                        frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine: Lightning pre-parsed WHERE: {pre_where}")

        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine._try_lightning: {e}")

        # Fall through to normal Neural layers (filters are now pre-injected)
        return None

    def _try_decomposer(self, question: str, chat_history: list) -> dict | None:
        """Pre-layer — AND/OR/BUT NOT multi-query handler."""
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.query_decomposer import QueryDecomposer
            decomposer = QueryDecomposer()
            if decomposer.can_decompose(question):
                return decomposer.process(question, chat_history)
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine decomposer: {e}")
        return None

    def _try_vector_memory(self, question: str) -> dict | None:
        """Layer 0.5 — FAISS semantic memory (near-identical past queries)."""
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.training_loop import find_nearest
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.model_registry import _faiss_index  # noqa: just check presence
        except ImportError:
            return None

        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.training_loop import find_nearest
            example, score = find_nearest(question, threshold=0.92)
            if example and example.get("sql"):
                return {"sql": example["sql"], "confidence": round(score, 3)}
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine vector_memory: {e}")
        return None

    def _try_pattern_engine(self, question: str) -> dict | None:
        """Layer 0 — existing PatternEngine (24 patterns, regex DFA)."""
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.local_engine import LocalNLPEngine
            engine = LocalNLPEngine()
            # Only use the pattern sub-engine, not full pipeline
            if hasattr(engine, "_pattern_engine"):
                result = engine._pattern_engine.process(question)
                if result and result.get("sql"):
                    return result
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    def _try_schema_compiler(self, question: str, chat_history: list) -> dict | None:
        """Layer 1 — Schema Compiler (pure rules, works Day 1, no model needed)."""
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.schema_parser import SchemaParser
            result = SchemaParser().parse(question, chat_history)
            if result and result.get("sql"):
                return result
        except ImportError:
            pass  # schema_parser not yet built — Phase 5.1
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine schema_compiler: {e}")
        return None

    def _try_slot_filler(self, question: str, chat_history: list) -> dict | None:
        """Layer 2 — DistilBERT-tiny slot filler + NeuralSQLBuilder."""
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_slot_filler import NeuralSlotFiller, _build_schema_context
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_sql_builder import NeuralSQLBuilder

            # Build schema context hint from conversation history or vocab
            schema_ctx = ""
            if chat_history:
                prev_dts = [
                    (t.get("result") or {}).get("doctype")
                    for t in chat_history[-2:]
                ]
                prev_dts = [dt for dt in prev_dts if dt]
                if prev_dts:
                    schema_ctx = _build_schema_context(prev_dts)

            slots = NeuralSlotFiller().predict(question, schema_ctx)
            if slots and slots.confidence >= 0.80:
                sql, confidence = NeuralSQLBuilder(slots, None).build()
                if sql:
                    return {"sql": sql, "confidence": confidence,
                            "entity": slots.entity, "slots": slots}
        except ImportError:
            pass  # not yet built
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine slot_filler: {e}")
        return None

    def _try_seq2seq(self, question: str) -> dict | None:
        """Layer 3 — T5-small Seq2Seq fallback."""
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_seq2seq import NeuralSeq2Seq
            sql = NeuralSeq2Seq().generate(question, "")
            if sql:
                return {"sql": sql, "confidence": 0.70}
        except ImportError:
            pass
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine seq2seq: {e}")
        return None

    def _execute(self, sql: str) -> tuple[list, list]:
        """Run SQL → (rows, columns).

        When RPG Boost is active:
          1. Check Rust L1 in-process cache before hitting DB.
          2. For large result sets (>10k rows) run Polars aggregation.
          3. Store result in L1 cache for repeat queries.
        """
        import hashlib, json
        from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.rpg_bridge import (
            rpg_cache_get, rpg_cache_set, rpg_aggregate, is_rpg_available,
        )

        cache_key = ""
        is_rpg_boost = self._is_rpg_boost_active()

        if is_rpg_boost and is_rpg_available():
            cache_key = "genbi:l1:" + hashlib.md5(sql.encode()).hexdigest()
            cached = rpg_cache_get(cache_key)
            if cached:
                try:
                    payload = json.loads(cached)
                    rows = payload.get("rows", [])
                    cols = payload.get("cols", [])
                    return rows, cols
                except Exception:
                    pass

        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import _execute_query
            rows, _ = _execute_query(sql, 500)
            cols = list(rows[0].keys()) if rows else []
        except Exception:
            return [], []

        # Polars aggregation for large result sets
        if is_rpg_boost and is_rpg_available() and len(rows) > 10_000:
            try:
                agg_json = rpg_aggregate(json.dumps(rows))
                if agg_json:
                    agg_rows = json.loads(agg_json)
                    if agg_rows:
                        rows = agg_rows
                        cols = list(rows[0].keys()) if rows else cols
            except Exception as e:
                frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralEngine Polars aggregation: {e}")

        # Store in L1 cache
        if is_rpg_boost and is_rpg_available() and cache_key:
            try:
                rpg_cache_set(cache_key, json.dumps({"rows": rows, "cols": cols}), 300)
            except Exception:
                pass

        return rows, cols

    @staticmethod
    def _is_rpg_boost_active() -> bool:
        """Return True when the current GenBI Settings provider is Neural + RPG Boost."""
        try:
            settings = frappe.get_cached_doc("GenBI Settings")
            return getattr(settings, "llm_provider", "") == "Neural + RPG Boost"
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _maybe_rewrite_sql(self, sql: str) -> str:
        """
        When RPG Boost is active, run query_rewrite to optimise the SQL.
        Falls back to original SQL on any error.
        """
        if not self._is_rpg_boost_active():
            return sql
        from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.rpg_bridge import rpg_query_rewrite
        rewritten = rpg_query_rewrite(sql, max_rows=500)
        return rewritten if rewritten else sql

    def _build_done(self, question, sql, rows, cols, layer, confidence, start,
                    slots=None, rewrite_applied=False) -> dict:
        explanation = self._explain(question, rows, layer, confidence, slots=slots, sql=sql)
        return {
            "success": True,
            "question": question,
            "explanation": explanation,
            "sql": sql,
            "data": rows,
            "columns": cols,
            "chart_config": None,
            "result_count": len(rows),
            "engine": f"neural:{layer}",
            "confidence": round(confidence, 3),
            "from_cache": False,
        }

    def _explain(self, question: str, rows: list, layer: str, confidence: float,
                 slots=None, sql: str = "", chart_type: str | None = None) -> str:
        """Generate explanation via NeuralExplainer (template-based, no LLM).
        9.5: passes chart_type so explanations are chart-aware.
        """
        try:
            from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_explainer import NeuralExplainer
            if slots is not None:
                return NeuralExplainer().explain(slots, rows, sql, chart_type=chart_type)
        except Exception:
            pass
        count = len(rows)
        if count == 0:
            return "No records found."
        return f"Found {count} record{'s' if count != 1 else ''}."

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/neural_explainer.py
"""
neural_explainer.py — Template-based explanation generator for Neural Engine results.

Zero LLM calls. All output is deterministic from SlotResult + row data.
Replaces the _explain() stub in neural_engine.py once built.

Public API:
    NeuralExplainer().explain(slots, rows, sql) → str
"""

import re

from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_sql_builder import SlotResult
from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.explainer_i18n import get_templates, is_rtl


# ── Currency config ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_CURRENCY_SYMBOLS = {
    "INR": "₹", "USD": "$", "EUR": "€", "GBP": "£",
    "JPY": "¥", "CNY": "¥", "KRW": "₩", "TRY": "₺",
    "THB": "฿", "VND": "₫", "ILS": "₪", "NGN": "₦",
}

_SOUTH_ASIAN_CURRENCIES = {"INR", "PKR", "BDT", "LKR", "NPR"}


class NeuralExplainer:
    """
    Generates human-readable explanations from SlotResult + row data.
    All methods are deterministic — no LLM, no randomness.
    """

    def __init__(self, currency: str = "INR", locale: str | None = None):
        self.currency = currency
        self._T = get_templates(locale)
        self._rtl = is_rtl(locale)

    def explain(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict], sql: str = "",
                chart_type: str | None = None) -> str:
        """
        Dispatch to the intent-specific _explain_* method.
        Falls back to _explain_generic if intent is unknown.

        9.5: chart_type is used to prefix the explanation with a chart-aware
        summary sentence ("Bar chart shows…", "Line chart shows trend…", etc.)
        """
        if not slots:
            return self._explain_generic(None, rows)

        dispatch = {
            "TOP_N":     self._explain_top_n,
            "AGGREGATE": self._explain_aggregate,
            "TREND":     self._explain_trend,
            "LIST":      self._explain_list,
            "COUNT":     self._explain_count,
            "COMPARE":   self._explain_compare,
            "LOOKUP":    self._explain_lookup,
        }
        fn = dispatch.get(slots.intent, self._explain_generic)
        try:
            body = fn(slots, rows)
        except Exception:
            body = self._explain_generic(slots, rows)

        # 9.5: prepend chart-aware sentence
        prefix = self._chart_prefix(chart_type, slots, rows)
        return f"{prefix}\n{body}".strip() if prefix else body

    def _chart_prefix(self, chart_type: str | None, slots, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        """
        Return a one-line chart-type-specific sentence or empty string.
        Examples:
          bar  → "Bar chart shows top 10 customers by revenue."
          line → "Line chart shows monthly revenue trend."
          pie  → "Pie chart shows distribution of sales by territory."
          number → "Single value: total revenue this month."
        """
        if not chart_type:
            return ""
        entity = getattr(slots, "entity", None) or "records"
        intent = getattr(slots, "intent", "") or ""
        n      = len(rows)
        ct     = chart_type.lower()

        if ct == "bar":
            return f"Bar chart shows {entity} ({n} rows)."
        if ct == "line":
            period = getattr(slots, "trend_period", None) or "period"
            return f"Line chart shows {entity} trend by {period}."
        if ct == "pie":
            grp = getattr(slots, "group_by", None) or "category"
            return f"Pie chart shows distribution of {entity} by {_label(grp)}."
        if ct in ("number", "single"):
            return f"Single value: {entity}."
        if ct == "table":
            return f"Table shows {n} {entity} row{'s' if n != 1 else ''}."
        return ""

    # ── Intent handlers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _explain_top_n(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        if not rows:
            return T["generic_none"].format(entity=slots.entity or "records")

        n       = len(rows)
        entity  = slots.entity or "records"
        grp_key = slots.group_by or list(rows[0].keys())[0]
        val_key = _find_numeric_key(rows[0])
        period  = _period_suffix(slots.date_ref, T)

        direction = T["top_n_direction_desc"] if getattr(slots, "_order_dir", "DESC") != "ASC" else T["top_n_direction_asc"]
        lines = [T["top_n_header"].format(direction=direction, n=n, entity=entity,
                                          group=_label(grp_key), period=period)]
        for i, row in enumerate(rows[:5], 1):
            grp_val = row.get(grp_key) or row.get(list(row.keys())[0]) or "—"
            num_val = row.get(val_key) if val_key else None
            num_str = f" — {self._fmt_number(num_val)}" if num_val is not None else ""
            lines.append(f"  #{i} {grp_val}{num_str}")

        if n > 5:
            lines.append(T["top_n_more"].format(n=n - 5))
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def _explain_aggregate(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        if not rows:
            return T["agg_no_data"]

        entity  = slots.entity or "records"
        agg     = (slots.agg_type or "SUM").upper()
        period  = _period_suffix(slots.date_ref, T)
        row     = rows[0]
        val_key = _find_numeric_key(row)

        fn_map = {"SUM": T["fn_sum"], "AVG": T["fn_avg"], "MAX": T["fn_max"],
                  "MIN": T["fn_min"], "COUNT": T["fn_count"]}
        fn_label = fn_map.get(agg, agg.capitalize())

        if val_key and row.get(val_key) is not None:
            return T["agg_result"].format(
                fn=fn_label,
                field=_label(slots.value_keyword or val_key),
                entity=entity,
                period=period,
                value=self._fmt_number(row[val_key]),
            )

        first_val = next(iter(row.values()), None)
        return f"{entity}{period}: {first_val}"

    def _explain_trend(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        if not rows:
            return T["trend_flat"].format(entity=slots.entity or "records", n=0, period="period")

        entity  = slots.entity or "records"
        period  = slots.trend_period or "month"
        n       = len(rows)
        val_key = _find_numeric_key(rows[0])
        per_key = _find_period_key(rows[0])

        if not val_key or not per_key:
            return T["trend_flat"].format(entity=entity, n=n, period=period)

        values = [float(r[val_key] or 0) for r in rows if r.get(val_key) is not None]
        if len(values) >= 2:
            first, last = values[0], values[-1]
            change_pct  = ((last - first) / first * 100) if first else 0
            arrow       = "↑" if change_pct > 0 else ("↓" if change_pct < 0 else "→")
            trend_label = (T["trend_increasing"] if change_pct > 1
                           else T["trend_decreasing"] if change_pct < -1
                           else T["trend_flat_label"])
            sep = " ← " if self._rtl else " → "
            header = T["trend_header"].format(
                entity=entity, period=period,
                from_per=self._period_label(str(rows[0].get(per_key, ""))),
                to_per=self._period_label(str(rows[-1].get(per_key, ""))),
            )
            summary = T["trend_summary"].format(
                arrow=arrow, label=trend_label, pct=change_pct, n=n, period=period,
            )
            return f"{header}\n{summary}"

        return T["trend_flat"].format(entity=entity, n=n, period=period)

    def _explain_count(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        entity = slots.entity or "records"
        period = _period_suffix(slots.date_ref, T)
        if not rows:
            return T["count_none"].format(entity=entity, period=period)

        count_key = _find_count_key(rows[0])
        count = rows[0].get(count_key, len(rows)) if count_key else len(rows)
        try:
            count = int(count)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            count = len(rows)
        return T["count_result"].format(count=count, entity=entity, period=period)

    def _explain_list(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        entity = slots.entity or "records"
        period = _period_suffix(slots.date_ref, T)
        n      = len(rows)
        limit  = slots.limit_n or 50
        if not rows:
            return T["list_none"].format(entity=entity, period=period)
        hint = T["list_hint"].format(n=n, limit=limit) if n >= limit else ""
        return T["list_result"].format(n=n, entity=entity, period=period, hint=hint)

    def _explain_compare(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        entity  = slots.entity or "records"
        val_key = _find_numeric_key(rows[0]) if rows else None
        per_key = _find_period_key(rows[0]) if rows else None

        if len(rows) >= 2 and val_key and per_key:
            p1 = self._period_label(str(rows[0].get(per_key, "")))
            p2 = self._period_label(str(rows[1].get(per_key, "")))
            v1 = float(rows[0].get(val_key) or 0)
            v2 = float(rows[1].get(val_key) or 0)
            diff_pct = ((v2 - v1) / v1 * 100) if v1 else 0
            arrow = "↑" if diff_pct > 0 else ("↓" if diff_pct < 0 else "→")
            return (
                T["compare_header"].format(entity=entity) + "\n" +
                T["compare_row"].format(period=p1, value=self._fmt_number(v1)) + "\n" +
                T["compare_row"].format(period=p2, value=self._fmt_number(v2)) + "\n" +
                T["compare_change"].format(arrow=arrow, pct=diff_pct)
            )

        return T["generic_found"].format(n=len(rows), entity=entity)

    def _explain_lookup(self, slots: SlotResult, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        entity = slots.entity or "records"
        n      = len(rows)
        term   = getattr(slots, "search_term", None) or ""
        if not rows:
            hint = T["lookup_hint"].format(term=term) if term else ""
            return T["lookup_none"].format(entity=entity, hint=hint)
        if n == 1:
            name_key = _find_name_key(rows[0])
            name_val = rows[0].get(name_key, "") if name_key else ""
            return T["lookup_found1"].format(name=name_val, entity=entity)
        return T["lookup_found_n"].format(n=n, entity=entity)

    def _explain_generic(self, slots, rows: list[dict]) -> str:
        T = self._T
        entity = getattr(slots, "entity", None) or "records"
        n      = len(rows)
        if not rows:
            return T["generic_none"].format(entity=entity)
        return T["generic_found"].format(n=n, entity=entity)

    # ── Number formatting ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _fmt_number(self, value, currency: str | None = None) -> str:
        """
        Format a float as a locale-aware string.

        INR (and other South Asian currencies): ₹12,34,567
        All others: $1,234,567  or  1,234,567
        """
        if value is None:
            return "—"
        try:
            value = float(value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return str(value)

        cur = currency or self.currency
        symbol = _CURRENCY_SYMBOLS.get(cur, "")

        # Choose grouping style
        if cur in _SOUTH_ASIAN_CURRENCIES:
            formatted = _south_asian_format(value)
        else:
            formatted = _western_format(value)

        return f"{symbol}{formatted}"

    # ── Period label ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _period_label(self, period_str: str) -> str:
        """
        Convert DB-format period strings to readable labels.
        "2025-04"  → "April 2025"
        "202514"   → "Week 14, 2025"   (YEARWEEK output)
        "2025-W14" → "Week 14, 2025"
        "2025"     → "2025"
        "2025-Q2"  → "Q2 2025"
        """
        if not period_str:
            return ""
        s = str(period_str).strip()

        # YYYY-MM
        m = re.match(r"^(\d{4})-(\d{2})$", s)
        if m:
            months = ["January","February","March","April","May","June",
                      "July","August","September","October","November","December"]
            try:
                return f"{months[int(m.group(2))-1]} {m.group(1)}"
            except (IndexError, ValueError):
                return s

        # YYYYWW (YEARWEEK output, e.g. 202514)
        m = re.match(r"^(\d{4})(\d{2})$", s)
        if m:
            return f"Week {m.group(2).lstrip('0') or '0'}, {m.group(1)}"

        # YYYY-WNN
        m = re.match(r"^(\d{4})-W(\d+)$", s)
        if m:
            return f"Week {m.group(2)}, {m.group(1)}"

        # YYYY-QN
        m = re.match(r"^(\d{4})-Q(\d)$", s)
        if m:
            return f"Q{m.group(2)} {m.group(1)}"

        # YYYY
        if re.match(r"^\d{4}$", s):
            return s

        return s


# ── Formatting helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _south_asian_format(value: float) -> str:
    """₹ South Asian grouping: 1,23,456.78"""
    negative = value < 0
    value = abs(value)
    has_decimals = value != int(value)
    integer_part = int(value)
    decimal_part = f".{round(value % 1, 2):.2f}"[1:] if has_decimals else ""

    s = str(integer_part)
    if len(s) <= 3:
        result = s
    else:
        result = s[-3:]
        s = s[:-3]
        while s:
            result = s[-2:] + "," + result
            s = s[:-2]

    return ("-" if negative else "") + result + decimal_part


def _western_format(value: float) -> str:
    """Western grouping: 1,234,567.89"""
    negative = value < 0
    value = abs(value)
    has_decimals = value != int(value)
    integer_part = int(value)
    decimal_str = f"{value:.2f}".split(".")[1] if has_decimals else ""

    s = str(integer_part)
    groups = []
    while s:
        groups.append(s[-3:])
        s = s[:-3]
    formatted = ",".join(reversed(groups))

    if has_decimals:
        formatted += f".{decimal_str}"
    return ("-" if negative else "") + formatted


# ── Row key detection helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _find_numeric_key(row: dict) -> str | None:
    """Return first key whose value looks numeric (float/int, not id/count)."""
    if not row:
        return None
    # Prefer keys that suggest a value field
    preferred = ("total_value", "result", "grand_total", "net_total", "total",
                 "amount", "value", "qty", "quantity")
    for k in preferred:
        if k in row:
            return k
    for k, v in row.items():
        if isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool):
            return k
        try:
            float(v)
            return k
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            pass
    return None


def _find_count_key(row: dict) -> str | None:
    for k in ("total_count", "count", "cnt", "n"):
        if k in row:
            return k
    return _find_numeric_key(row)


def _find_period_key(row: dict) -> str | None:
    for k in ("period", "month", "week", "year", "quarter", "date"):
        if k in row:
            return k
    return None


def _find_name_key(row: dict) -> str | None:
    for k in ("name", "customer_name", "supplier_name", "employee_name",
              "item_name", "full_name", "title"):
        if k in row:
            return k
    return list(row.keys())[0] if row else None


def _label(fieldname: str) -> str:
    """Convert fieldname to human label: grand_total → Grand Total."""
    return fieldname.replace("_", " ").title() if fieldname else ""


def _period_suffix(date_ref: str | None, T: dict | None = None) -> str:
    """Return human period phrase for a date_ref slot value."""
    if not date_ref:
        return ""
    # Use English map — localised date phrasing is complex; English is safe cross-locale
    m = {
        "today":         " today",
        "yesterday":     " yesterday",
        "this_week":     " this week",
        "last_week":     " last week",
        "this_month":    " this month",
        "last_month":    " last month",
        "this_quarter":  " this quarter",
        "last_quarter":  " last quarter",
        "this_year":     " this year",
        "last_year":     " last year",
        "ytd":           " year to date",
        "last_7_days":   " in the last 7 days",
        "last_30_days":  " in the last 30 days",
        "last_90_days":  " in the last 90 days",
    }
    return m.get(date_ref, "")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/neural_seq2seq.py
"""
neural_seq2seq.py — T5-small ONNX inference wrapper (Layer 3).

Loads the ONNX seq2seq model from sites/{site}/neural_models/t5_genbi/.
Falls back to HuggingFace PyTorch model in the same directory if ONNX
files are absent (useful during development before ONNX export is done).

Input:  question + compressed schema context
Output: raw SQL string (validated by NeuralSQLBuilder._dry_run_validate before use)
"""

import os

import frappe

from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.model_registry import _model_dir, get_t5_model


_T5_DIR_NAME = "t5_genbi"
_TOKENIZER_CACHE = None


def _t5_dir() -> str:
    return os.path.join(_model_dir(), _T5_DIR_NAME)


def _get_t5_tokenizer():
    global _TOKENIZER_CACHE
    if _TOKENIZER_CACHE is not None:
        return _TOKENIZER_CACHE
    try:
        from transformers import T5TokenizerFast, AutoTokenizer
        d = _t5_dir()
        if os.path.isdir(d):
            _TOKENIZER_CACHE = T5TokenizerFast.from_pretrained(d)
        else:
            _TOKENIZER_CACHE = T5TokenizerFast.from_pretrained("t5-small")
        return _TOKENIZER_CACHE
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger().warning(f"NeuralSeq2Seq: tokenizer load failed — {e}")
        return None


class NeuralSeq2Seq:
    """
    T5-small ONNX inference wrapper.

    Tries ONNX first (via model_registry.get_t5_model which loads decoder.onnx).
    Falls back to HuggingFace generate() if ONNX is unavailable.
    Returns None if neither path works.
    """

    def generate(self, question: str, schema_context: str = "",
                 max_new_tokens: int = 256) -> str | None:
        """
        Generate a SQL string from the question + optional schema context.
        Returns None if generation fails or produces an empty result.
        """
        prompt = f"translate English to SQL: {question}"
        if schema_context:
            prompt = f"translate English to SQL: [SCHEMA] {schema_context} [Q] {question}"

        # Try ONNX path first
        result = self._generate_onnx(prompt, max_new_tokens)
        if result:
            return self._clean_sql(result)

        # HuggingFace PyTorch fallback
        result = self._generate_hf(prompt, max_new_tokens)
        if result:
            return self._clean_sql(result)

        return None

    # ── ONNX path ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _generate_onnx(self, prompt: str, max_new_tokens: int) -> str | None:
        try:
            from optimum.onnxruntime import ORTModelForSeq2SeqLM
            from transformers import T5TokenizerFast

            d = _t5_dir()
            if not os.path.isdir(d):
                return None

            tokenizer = _get_t5_tokenizer()
            if tokenizer is None:
                return None

            # Load via optimum (handles encoder_model.onnx + decoder_model.onnx)
            model = ORTModelForSeq2SeqLM.from_pretrained(d)
            inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt",
                               max_length=128, truncation=True)
            outputs = model.generate(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens)
            return tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)

        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralSeq2Seq ONNX path: {e}")
            return None

    # ── HuggingFace PyTorch fallback ──────────────────────────────────────────

    def _generate_hf(self, prompt: str, max_new_tokens: int) -> str | None:
        try:
            from transformers import T5ForConditionalGeneration, T5TokenizerFast
            import torch

            d = _t5_dir()
            if not os.path.isdir(d):
                return None
            if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(d, "config.json")):
                return None

            tokenizer = _get_t5_tokenizer()
            if tokenizer is None:
                return None

            model = T5ForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(d)
            model.eval()
            inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt",
                               max_length=128, truncation=True)
            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = model.generate(
                    **inputs,
                    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                    num_beams=4,
                    early_stopping=True,
                )
            return tokenizer.decode(outputs[0], skip_special_tokens=True)

        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"NeuralSeq2Seq HF path: {e}")
            return None

    # ── SQL cleanup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _clean_sql(self, raw: str) -> str | None:
        """Strip T5 artifacts and ensure the output starts with SELECT."""
        sql = raw.strip()
        # T5 sometimes prefixes with "SQL:" or "select:"
        for prefix in ("sql:", "SQL:", "select:", "SELECT:"):
            if sql.startswith(prefix):
                sql = sql[len(prefix):].strip()

        if not sql.upper().startswith("SELECT"):
            return None

        return sql

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/neural_slot_filler.py
"""
neural_slot_filler.py — DistilBERT-tiny ONNX inference wrapper.

Loads slot_filler.onnx from sites/{site}/neural_models/ via model_registry.
Thread-safe: model is a singleton; multiple gunicorn workers share the file
but each process has its own ort.InferenceSession (no lock needed at predict time).

Returns a SlotResult populated with predicted slot values + confidence score.
"""

import json
import os

import numpy as np
import frappe

from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.model_registry import get_slot_filler, get_model_metadata, _model_dir
from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.neural_sql_builder import SlotResult


# ── Label vocabularies (must match train_slot_filler.py SLOT_HEADS) ──────────

_INTENT_LABELS   = ["LIST", "AGGREGATE", "TOP_N", "TREND", "COUNT", "COMPARE", "LOOKUP"]
_AGG_LABELS      = ["NONE", "SUM", "AVG", "COUNT", "MAX", "MIN"]
_DATE_LABELS     = [
    "NONE", "today", "yesterday", "this_week", "last_week",
    "this_month", "last_month", "this_quarter", "last_quarter",
    "this_year", "last_year", "ytd", "last_7_days", "last_30_days", "last_90_days",
]
_STATUS_LABELS   = ["NONE", "draft", "submitted", "cancelled"]

_SLOT_VOCABS = {
    "intent":        _INTENT_LABELS,
    "agg_type":      _AGG_LABELS,
    "date_ref":      _DATE_LABELS,
    "filter_status": _STATUS_LABELS,
}

_TOKENIZER_CACHE = None
_TOKENIZER_LOCK_FLAG = False


def _get_tokenizer():
    """Lazy-load tokenizer — reuse across calls."""
    global _TOKENIZER_CACHE, _TOKENIZER_LOCK_FLAG
    if _TOKENIZER_CACHE is not None:
        return _TOKENIZER_CACHE

    if _TOKENIZER_LOCK_FLAG:
        return None  # concurrent init — caller falls back gracefully

    try:
        _TOKENIZER_LOCK_FLAG = True
        from transformers import AutoTokenizer
        meta = get_model_metadata()
        model_name = meta.get("base_model", "prajjwal1/bert-tiny")
        # Try loading from a local cache first (sites/{site}/neural_models/tokenizer/)
        local_tokenizer_path = os.path.join(_model_dir(), "tokenizer")
        if os.path.isdir(local_tokenizer_path):
            _TOKENIZER_CACHE = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(local_tokenizer_path)
        else:
            _TOKENIZER_CACHE = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)
        return _TOKENIZER_CACHE
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger().warning(f"NeuralSlotFiller: tokenizer load failed — {e}")
        return None
    finally:
        _TOKENIZER_LOCK_FLAG = False


def _softmax(logits: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    e = np.exp(logits - logits.max())
    return e / e.sum()


class NeuralSlotFiller:
    """
    Lazy-loaded ONNX inference wrapper for the slot classification model.
    Thread-safe singleton via model_registry.
    """

    def predict(self, question: str, schema_context: str = "") -> SlotResult:
        """
        Run slot classification on the question.

        Returns a SlotResult with confidence = 0.0 if the model is absent.
        Confidence is the mean softmax probability across all slot heads.
        """
        session = get_slot_filler()
        if session is None:
            return SlotResult(confidence=0.0)

        tokenizer = _get_tokenizer()
        if tokenizer is None:
            return SlotResult(confidence=0.0)

        try:
            # Prepend schema context if provided (improves entity/field accuracy)
            input_text = question
            if schema_context:
                input_text = f"[SCHEMA] {schema_context} [QUESTION] {question}"

            meta = get_model_metadata()
            max_len = meta.get("max_length", 128)

            enc = tokenizer(
                input_text,
                max_length=max_len,
                padding="max_length",
                truncation=True,
                return_tensors="np",
            )

            # ONNX Runtime expects numpy int64
            feeds = {
                "input_ids":      enc["input_ids"].astype(np.int64),
                "attention_mask": enc["attention_mask"].astype(np.int64),
            }
            if "token_type_ids" in enc:
                feeds["token_type_ids"] = enc["token_type_ids"].astype(np.int64)
            else:
                feeds["token_type_ids"] = np.zeros_like(feeds["input_ids"])

            outputs = session.run(None, feeds)
            output_names = [o.name for o in session.get_outputs()]

            # Map output index → slot head name
            results: dict[str, tuple[str, float]] = {}  # head → (label, confidence)
            for i, name in enumerate(output_names):
                if name not in _SLOT_VOCABS:
                    continue
                logits = outputs[i][0]  # shape: (num_classes,)
                probs  = _softmax(logits)
                best_idx   = int(probs.argmax())
                best_label = _SLOT_VOCABS[name][best_idx]
                best_conf  = float(probs[best_idx])
                results[name] = (best_label, best_conf)

            # Aggregate confidence — mean over all slot heads
            confidence = (
                sum(conf for _, conf in results.values()) / len(results)
                if results else 0.0
            )

            intent        = results.get("intent",        ("LIST",  0.0))[0]
            agg_type      = results.get("agg_type",      ("NONE",  0.0))[0]
            date_ref      = results.get("date_ref",      ("NONE",  0.0))[0]
            filter_status = results.get("filter_status", ("NONE",  0.0))[0]

            return SlotResult(
                intent        = intent,
                agg_type      = None if agg_type == "NONE" else agg_type,
                date_ref      = None if date_ref == "NONE" else date_ref,
                filter_status = None if filter_status == "NONE" else filter_status,
                confidence    = round(confidence, 4),
            )

        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().warning(f"NeuralSlotFiller.predict error: {e}")
            return SlotResult(confidence=0.0)


def _build_schema_context(entities: list[str]) -> str:
    """
    Build a compressed schema string from a list of DocType names.
    Injected alongside the question to help the model resolve entity/field slots.

    Example output: "Customer: name customer_name territory | Sales Invoice: grand_total posting_date status"
    """
    parts = []
    for dt in entities[:3]:  # limit to avoid exceeding max_length
        try:
            meta = frappe.get_meta(dt)
            important_fields = [
                f.fieldname for f in meta.fields
                if f.fieldtype in ("Data", "Link", "Currency", "Float", "Select", "Date")
                and not (f.fieldname or "").startswith("_")
            ][:8]
            if important_fields:
                parts.append(f"{dt}: {' '.join(important_fields)}")
        except Exception:
            pass
    return " | ".join(parts)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/neural_sql_builder.py
"""
neural_sql_builder.py — Slot → SQL, with live schema verification.

Receives a SlotResult (from NeuralSlotFiller or SchemaParser) and builds
a verified SQL query.  Every field name is checked against frappe.get_meta()
before being put into SQL — no hallucination possible.

Also provides:
  RelationshipGraph — discovers DocType join paths from Frappe meta
  SchemaAutoDiscovery — resolves unknown field keywords via cosine similarity
"""

import re

import frappe

from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.schema_vocabulary import get_numeric_fields, resolve_field


# ── SlotResult ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SlotResult:
    """Slot values extracted by the slot filler or schema parser."""
    __slots__ = (
        "intent", "entity", "secondary_entity",
        "group_by", "agg_type", "value_keyword",
        "date_ref", "limit_n", "filter_status",
        "search_term", "amount_op", "amount_val",
        "trend_period", "confidence",
    )

    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        defaults = {
            "intent": "LIST",
            "entity": None,
            "secondary_entity": None,
            "group_by": None,
            "agg_type": "SUM",
            "value_keyword": None,
            "date_ref": None,
            "limit_n": 50,
            "filter_status": None,
            "search_term": None,
            "amount_op": None,
            "amount_val": None,
            "trend_period": "month",
            "confidence": 0.0,
        }
        defaults.update(kwargs)
        for k, v in defaults.items():
            setattr(self, k, v)


# ── RelationshipGraph ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class RelationshipGraph:
    """
    Discovers all DocType relationships at startup from frappe.get_meta().
    Cached in Redis for 1 hour.

    Edge: (parent_doctype, child_doctype, link_field_on_child)
    """
    _CACHE_KEY = "genbi:relationship_graph"
    _CACHE_TTL = 3600

    def __init__(self):
        self._graph = None

    def _load(self) -> dict:
        cached = frappe.cache().get_value(self._CACHE_KEY)
        if cached:
            return cached

        graph: dict[str, list[tuple]] = {}  # doctype → [(linked_doctype, fieldname)]

        try:
            doctypes = frappe.get_all("DocType", filters={"istable": 0, "issingle": 0}, pluck="name", limit=500)
            for dt in doctypes:
                try:
                    meta = frappe.get_meta(dt)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                for field in meta.fields:
                    if field.fieldtype == "Link" and field.options:
                        parent = field.options
                        if parent not in graph:
                            graph[parent] = []
                        graph[parent].append((dt, field.fieldname))
        except Exception as e:
            frappe.logger().debug(f"RelationshipGraph._load: {e}")

        frappe.cache().set_value(self._CACHE_KEY, graph, expires_in_sec=self._CACHE_TTL)
        return graph

    @property
    def graph(self):
        if self._graph is None:
            self._graph = self._load()
        return self._graph

    def find_join_path(self, parent: str, child: str) -> list[tuple] | None:
        """
        Return [(from_table, to_table, on_field), ...] for a single-hop JOIN,
        or None if no direct relationship found.
        """
        # Direct: child has a Link field pointing to parent
        children = self.graph.get(parent, [])
        for (linked_dt, field) in children:
            if linked_dt == child:
                return [(parent, child, field)]

        # Reverse: parent has a Link field pointing to child
        children_of_child = self.graph.get(child, [])
        for (linked_dt, field) in children_of_child:
            if linked_dt == parent:
                return [(child, parent, field)]

        return None

    def get_related_doctypes(self, doctype: str) -> list[str]:
        """Return all DocTypes directly linked to/from this DocType."""
        related = set()
        for (dt, _) in self.graph.get(doctype, []):
            related.add(dt)
        for parent, children in self.graph.items():
            for (dt, _) in children:
                if dt == doctype:
                    related.add(parent)
        return list(related)


# ── SchemaAutoDiscovery ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SchemaAutoDiscovery:
    """
    Resolves unknown field keywords for custom DocTypes via cosine similarity.
    Uses all-MiniLM-L6-v2 (already loaded in Phase 1 — no cold start).
    """

    def resolve_field(self, term: str, doctype: str) -> str | None:
        """
        Find the best matching fieldname in doctype for the given keyword.
        Returns None if similarity < 0.5 or sentence-transformers unavailable.
        """
        try:
            from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer, util as st_util
            import torch

            meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
            field_labels = [(f.fieldname, f.label or f.fieldname) for f in meta.fields if f.fieldname]
            if not field_labels:
