    """Receive Telegram Bot webhook updates and respond to messages."""
    try:
        body = frappe.local.request.get_data(as_text=True)
        update = json.loads(body) if body else {}
    except Exception:
        frappe.response["http_status_code"] = 400
        return {"ok": False}

    # Only handle message updates
    message = update.get("message") or update.get("edited_message")
    if not message:
        return {"ok": True}

    chat_id = message.get("chat", {}).get("id")
    text = (message.get("text") or "").strip()

    if not chat_id or not text:
        return {"ok": True}

    # Ignore non-command noise
    if text.startswith("/start"):
        _telegram_send(chat_id, (
            "👋 *Welcome to GenBI!*\n\n"
            "Ask me any business question and I'll query your ERPNext data.\n\n"
            "*Examples:*\n"
            "• Show top 10 customers by revenue\n"
            "• What is total sales this month?\n"
            "• List overdue invoices\n"
            "• Forecast sales next 3 months"
        ))
        return {"ok": True}

    if text.startswith("/help"):
        _telegram_send(chat_id, (
            "*GenBI Commands:*\n"
            "Just type any business question in plain English.\n\n"
            "*/start* — Welcome message\n"
            "*/help* — This help message\n"
            "*/stats* — Database summary\n"
        ))
        return {"ok": True}

    if text.startswith("/stats"):
        reply = _get_stats_summary()
        _telegram_send(chat_id, reply)
        return {"ok": True}

    # Run GenBI query
    _telegram_send(chat_id, "⏳ Querying…")

    try:
        result = _run_genbi_query(text)
        reply_text = _format_reply(result)
        _telegram_send(chat_id, reply_text, parse_mode="Markdown")

        # Send chart image if applicable
        if result.get("success") and result.get("data") and _should_send_chart(result):
            img_bytes = _render_chart_image(result)
            if img_bytes:
                _telegram_send_photo(chat_id, img_bytes, caption=result.get("explanation", ""))
    except Exception as e:
        _telegram_send(chat_id, f"❌ Error: {e}")

    return {"ok": True}


def _telegram_send(chat_id, text, parse_mode="Markdown"):
    """Send a Telegram message."""
    import requests
    token = _get_setting("genbi_telegram_bot_token")
    if not token:
        return
    requests.post(
        f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/sendMessage",
        json={"chat_id": chat_id, "text": text[:4096], "parse_mode": parse_mode},
        timeout=15,
    )


def _telegram_send_photo(chat_id, img_bytes, caption=""):
    """Send a photo via Telegram."""
    import requests
    token = _get_setting("genbi_telegram_bot_token")
    if not token:
        return
    requests.post(
        f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/sendPhoto",
        data={"chat_id": chat_id, "caption": caption[:200]},
        files={"photo": ("chart.png", img_bytes, "image/png")},
        timeout=20,
    )


# ── WhatsApp ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def whatsapp_webhook():
    """Handle WhatsApp Cloud API webhook (GET = verification, POST = messages)."""
    method = frappe.local.request.method

    # Webhook verification challenge (GET)
    if method == "GET":
        params = frappe.local.request.args
        verify_token = _get_setting("genbi_whatsapp_verify_token")
        if params.get("hub.verify_token") == verify_token:
            frappe.response["type"] = "text"
            frappe.response["txt"] = params.get("hub.challenge", "")
        else:
            frappe.response["http_status_code"] = 403
        return

    # Incoming message (POST)
    try:
        body = frappe.local.request.get_data(as_text=True)
        data = json.loads(body) if body else {}
    except Exception:
        frappe.response["http_status_code"] = 400
        return

    # Navigate to message
    try:
        entry = data.get("entry", [{}])[0]
        change = entry.get("changes", [{}])[0]
        value = change.get("value", {})
        messages = value.get("messages", [])
        if not messages:
            return

        msg = messages[0]
        from_number = msg.get("from")
        msg_type = msg.get("type")

        if msg_type != "text":
            _whatsapp_send(from_number, "I can only respond to text messages right now. Please type your question.")
            return

        text = msg.get("text", {}).get("body", "").strip()
        if not text:
            return

        # Run query
        result = _run_genbi_query(text)
        reply = _format_reply(result, plain=True)
        _whatsapp_send(from_number, reply)

        # Send chart image if applicable
        if result.get("success") and result.get("data") and _should_send_chart(result):
            img_bytes = _render_chart_image(result)
            if img_bytes:
                _whatsapp_send_image(from_number, img_bytes, caption=result.get("explanation", ""))

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(f"WhatsApp webhook error: {e}", "GenBI Bot")

    return {"status": "ok"}


def _whatsapp_send(to: str, text: str):
    """Send a WhatsApp text message via Meta Cloud API."""
    import requests
    token = _get_setting("genbi_whatsapp_token")
    phone_id = _get_setting("genbi_whatsapp_phone_id")
    if not token or not phone_id:
        return
    requests.post(
        f"https://graph.facebook.com/v18.0/{phone_id}/messages",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "messaging_product": "whatsapp",
            "to": to,
            "type": "text",
            "text": {"body": text[:4096]},
        },
        timeout=15,
    )


def _whatsapp_send_image(to: str, img_bytes: bytes, caption: str = ""):
    """Upload image and send via WhatsApp."""
    import requests
    token = _get_setting("genbi_whatsapp_token")
    phone_id = _get_setting("genbi_whatsapp_phone_id")
    if not token or not phone_id:
        return

    # Upload media first
    upload_resp = requests.post(
        f"https://graph.facebook.com/v18.0/{phone_id}/media",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
        data={"messaging_product": "whatsapp"},
        files={"file": ("chart.png", img_bytes, "image/png")},
        timeout=20,
    )
    if not upload_resp.ok:
        return

    media_id = upload_resp.json().get("id")
    if not media_id:
        return

    requests.post(
        f"https://graph.facebook.com/v18.0/{phone_id}/messages",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        json={
            "messaging_product": "whatsapp",
            "to": to,
            "type": "image",
            "image": {"id": media_id, "caption": caption[:1024]},
        },
        timeout=15,
    )


# ── Shared helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _run_genbi_query(question: str) -> dict:
    """Run a GenBI question in system context."""
    from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import process_question
    return process_question(question)


def _format_reply(result: dict, plain: bool = False) -> str:
    """Format a GenBI result into a readable message string."""
    if not result.get("success"):
        return f"❌ {result.get('error', 'Query failed')}"

    explanation = result.get("explanation") or ""

    # Explain / forecast mode — just text
    if result.get("engine") in ("explain", "forecast"):
        return explanation

    data = result.get("data") or []
    if not data:
        return explanation or "No results found."

    cols = list(data[0].keys())

    if plain:
        # Plain text table for WhatsApp
        lines = [explanation, ""]
        header = " | ".join(cols[:6])
        lines.append(header)
        lines.append("-" * len(header))
        for row in data[:20]:
            lines.append(" | ".join(str(row.get(c, ""))[:20] for c in cols[:6]))
        if len(data) > 20:
            lines.append(f"(+{len(data)-20} more rows)")
    else:
        # Markdown table for Telegram
        lines = [explanation, ""]
        lines.append("| " + " | ".join(cols[:6]) + " |")
        lines.append("| " + " | ".join(["---"] * min(len(cols), 6)) + " |")
        for row in data[:15]:
            lines.append("| " + " | ".join(str(row.get(c, ""))[:20] for c in cols[:6]) + " |")
        if len(data) > 15:
            lines.append(f"\n_(+{len(data)-15} more rows)_")

    sql = result.get("sql")
    if sql and not plain:
        lines.append(f"\n`{sql[:200]}{'…' if len(sql)>200 else ''}`")

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _should_send_chart(result: dict) -> bool:
    """Return True when it makes sense to send a chart image."""
    chart = result.get("chart_config") or {}
    ct = (chart.get("chart_type") or chart.get("type") or "table").lower()
    return ct in ("bar", "line", "pie", "donut") and len(result.get("data") or []) > 1


def _render_chart_image(result: dict) -> bytes | None:
    """Render a matplotlib chart from GenBI result data. Returns PNG bytes or None."""
    try:
        import matplotlib
        matplotlib.use("Agg")  # non-interactive backend
        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
        import io

        data = result.get("data") or []
        if not data or len(data) < 2:
            return None

        chart = result.get("chart_config") or {}
        ct = (chart.get("chart_type") or chart.get("type") or "bar").lower()
        title = chart.get("title") or result.get("explanation", "GenBI Chart")[:60]

        cols = list(data[0].keys())
        if len(cols) < 2:
            return None

        x_col = cols[0]
        y_col = cols[1]
        labels = [str(r.get(x_col, ""))[:15] for r in data[:15]]
        values = []
        for r in data[:15]:
            try:
                values.append(float(r.get(y_col) or 0))
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                values.append(0.0)

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5))
        fig.patch.set_facecolor("#f8f9fd")
        ax.set_facecolor("#f8f9fd")

        if ct == "pie" or ct == "donut":
            wedge_props = {"width": 0.5} if ct == "donut" else {}
            ax.pie(values, labels=labels, autopct="%1.1f%%", wedgeprops=wedge_props)
        elif ct == "line":
            ax.plot(labels, values, marker="o", color="#5e64ff", linewidth=2)
            ax.fill_between(range(len(labels)), values, alpha=0.15, color="#5e64ff")
            ax.set_xticks(range(len(labels)))
            ax.set_xticklabels(labels, rotation=30, ha="right")
        else:  # bar (default)
            bars = ax.bar(labels, values, color="#5e64ff", alpha=0.85)
            ax.bar_label(bars, fmt="%.0f", padding=2, fontsize=8)
            ax.set_xticks(range(len(labels)))
            ax.set_xticklabels(labels, rotation=30, ha="right")

        ax.set_title(title, fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", pad=10)
        ax.set_xlabel(x_col, fontsize=9)
        ax.set_ylabel(y_col, fontsize=9)
        plt.tight_layout()

        buf = io.BytesIO()
        plt.savefig(buf, format="png", dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
        plt.close(fig)
        buf.seek(0)
        return buf.read()

    except Exception:
        return None


def _get_stats_summary() -> str:
    """Return a brief stats overview for /stats command."""
    try:
        lines = ["📊 *ERPNext Quick Stats*", ""]
        for doctype, label in [
            ("Sales Invoice", "Invoices"),
            ("Customer", "Customers"),
            ("Item", "Items"),
            ("Employee", "Employees"),
        ]:
            try:
                count = frappe.db.count(doctype)
                lines.append(f"• {label}: *{count:,}*")
            except Exception:
                pass
        return "\n".join(lines)
    except Exception:
        return "Stats unavailable."


def _get_setting(key: str) -> str:
    """Read a bot config value from frappe.conf or GenBI Settings."""
    # Check frappe.conf first (for non-Frappe deployed bots)
    val = frappe.conf.get(key, "")
    if val:
        return val
    # Try GenBI Settings custom field
    try:
        settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
        return getattr(settings, key, "") or ""
    except Exception:
        return ""

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/mcp_server.py
"""
Task 4.2 — GenBI MCP Server

Implements the Model Context Protocol (MCP) so AI coding assistants
(Claude Desktop, Cursor, Windsurf, Continue.dev, etc.) can query
GenBI / ERPNext data via tool-calls.

Protocol: JSON-RPC 2.0 over HTTP POST  /api/method/f_genbi.f_genbi.mcp_server.handle

Supported MCP tools:
  genbi_query         — Natural-language question → data (rows + explanation)
  genbi_sql           — Run a raw SELECT SQL query
  genbi_schema        — Get DocType fields + row count
  genbi_dictionary    — Search the GenBI Data Dictionary
  genbi_list_doctypes — List available DocTypes by module

Clients configure the server URL + API key in their MCP config file:

  {
    "mcpServers": {
      "genbi": {
        "command": "npx",
        "args": ["-y", "mcp-remote", "https://your-site.com/api/method/f_genbi.f_genbi.mcp_server.handle"],
        "env": {"GENBI_API_KEY": "your-frappe-api-key"}
      }
    }
  }

Or for Claude Desktop (direct HTTP transport):
  {
    "mcpServers": {
      "genbi": {
        "type": "http",
        "url": "https://your-site.com/api/method/f_genbi.f_genbi.mcp_server.handle",
        "headers": {"Authorization": "token api_key:api_secret"}
      }
    }
  }
"""

import json
import frappe
from frappe import _


# ── MCP Tool registry ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_TOOLS = [
    {
        "name": "genbi_query",
        "description": (
            "Ask a natural-language business intelligence question and get data from ERPNext. "
            "Returns rows, an explanation, and the generated SQL."
        ),
        "inputSchema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "question": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The business question in plain English, e.g. 'Show top 10 customers by revenue'",
                },
            },
            "required": ["question"],
        },
    },
    {
        "name": "genbi_sql",
        "description": (
            "Execute a raw SELECT SQL query against ERPNext. "
            "Table names use `tab` prefix: `tabSales Invoice`, `tabCustomer`, etc. "
            "Only SELECT statements are allowed."
        ),
        "inputSchema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "sql": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "A valid SELECT SQL query",
                },
                "limit": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "description": "Maximum number of rows to return (default 100, max 500)",
                    "default": 100,
                },
            },
            "required": ["sql"],
        },
    },
    {
        "name": "genbi_schema",
        "description": (
            "Get the fields, relationships, and row count for a specific ERPNext DocType. "
            "Useful for understanding what data is available before writing SQL."
        ),
        "inputSchema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "doctype": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The ERPNext DocType name, e.g. 'Sales Invoice', 'Customer', 'Item'",
                },
            },
            "required": ["doctype"],
        },
    },
    {
        "name": "genbi_dictionary",
        "description": (
            "Search the GenBI Data Dictionary for field names, labels, and descriptions. "
            "Useful for discovering which DocType/field contains specific business data."
        ),
        "inputSchema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "term": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Search term to find matching DocTypes/fields",
                },
            },
            "required": ["term"],
        },
    },
    {
        "name": "genbi_list_doctypes",
        "description": (
            "List available ERPNext DocTypes, optionally filtered by module. "
            "Returns DocType names grouped by module with row counts."
        ),
        "inputSchema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "module": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional module filter, e.g. 'Selling', 'Buying', 'Accounts', 'Stock'",
                },
            },
            "required": [],
        },
    },
]


# ── MCP HTTP handler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=False)
def handle():
    """Main MCP JSON-RPC endpoint.

    Handles:
      initialize        — return server info + capabilities
      tools/list        — return available tools
      tools/call        — execute a tool
    """
    try:
        body = frappe.local.request.get_data(as_text=True)
        request = json.loads(body) if body else {}
    except Exception:
        return _jsonrpc_error(-32700, "Parse error", id=None)

    method = request.get("method", "")
    params = request.get("params") or {}
    req_id = request.get("id")

    if method == "initialize":
        return _jsonrpc_result({
            "protocolVersion": "2024-11-05",
            "capabilities": {"tools": {}},
            "serverInfo": {
                "name": "GenBI ERPNext",
                "version": "1.0.0",
            },
        }, req_id)

    if method == "notifications/initialized":
        # Fire-and-forget notification — no response needed
        frappe.response["http_status_code"] = 204
        return {}

    if method == "tools/list":
        return _jsonrpc_result({"tools": _TOOLS}, req_id)

    if method == "tools/call":
        tool_name = params.get("name", "")
        arguments = params.get("arguments") or {}
        return _dispatch_tool(tool_name, arguments, req_id)

    return _jsonrpc_error(-32601, f"Method not found: {method}", id=req_id)


# ── Tool dispatcher ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _dispatch_tool(name: str, args: dict, req_id):
    try:
        if name == "genbi_query":
            result = _tool_query(args)
        elif name == "genbi_sql":
            result = _tool_sql(args)
        elif name == "genbi_schema":
            result = _tool_schema(args)
        elif name == "genbi_dictionary":
            result = _tool_dictionary(args)
        elif name == "genbi_list_doctypes":
            result = _tool_list_doctypes(args)
        else:
            return _jsonrpc_error(-32602, f"Unknown tool: {name}", id=req_id)

        return _jsonrpc_result({
            "content": [{"type": "text", "text": result}]
        }, req_id)

    except Exception as e:
        return _jsonrpc_error(-32603, str(e), id=req_id)


# ── Tool implementations ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _tool_query(args: dict) -> str:
    question = (args.get("question") or "").strip()
    if not question:
        return "Error: question is required"

    from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import process_question
    result = process_question(question)

    if not result.get("success"):
        return f"Error: {result.get('error', 'Query failed')}"

    # Explain mode or forecast — just return explanation
    if result.get("engine") in ("explain", "forecast"):
        return result.get("explanation", "No explanation returned.")

    lines = [result.get("explanation", "")]

    data = result.get("data") or []
    if data:
        cols = list(data[0].keys())
        lines.append("\n| " + " | ".join(cols) + " |")
        lines.append("| " + " | ".join(["---"] * len(cols)) + " |")
        for row in data[:50]:
            lines.append("| " + " | ".join(str(row.get(c, "")) for c in cols) + " |")
        if len(data) > 50:
            lines.append(f"\n_(showing 50 of {len(data)} rows)_")

    sql = result.get("sql")
    if sql:
        lines.append(f"\n```sql\n{sql}\n```")

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _tool_sql(args: dict) -> str:
    sql = (args.get("sql") or "").strip()
    limit = min(int(args.get("limit") or 100), 500)

    if not sql:
        return "Error: sql is required"
    if not sql.upper().lstrip().startswith("SELECT"):
        return "Error: only SELECT statements are allowed"

    from f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine import _validate_sql, _execute_query
    try:
        _validate_sql(sql)
        rows, from_cache = _execute_query(sql, limit)
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Error: {e}"

    if not rows:
        return "Query returned 0 rows."

    cols = list(rows[0].keys())
    lines = [f"**{len(rows)} row(s)** returned{'  _(cached)_' if from_cache else ''}"]
    lines.append("\n| " + " | ".join(cols) + " |")
    lines.append("| " + " | ".join(["---"] * len(cols)) + " |")
    for row in rows:
        lines.append("| " + " | ".join(str(row.get(c, "")) for c in cols) + " |")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _tool_schema(args: dict) -> str:
    doctype = (args.get("doctype") or "").strip()
    if not doctype:
        return "Error: doctype is required"

    from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.data_dictionary import get_doctype_docs
    doc = get_doctype_docs(doctype)

    if not doc.get("success"):
        return f"Error: {doc.get('error', 'DocType not found')}"

    lines = [
        f"# {doctype}",
        f"Module: {doc.get('module', '?')}  |  "
        f"Table: `{doc.get('table_name', 'tab' + doctype)}`  |  "
        f"Rows: {doc.get('row_count', 'unknown')}  |  "
        f"Submittable: {'Yes' if doc.get('is_submittable') else 'No'}",
        "",
        "## Fields",
        "| fieldname | label | type | required |",
        "|---|---|---|---|",
    ]
    for f in (doc.get("fields") or [])[:60]:
        lines.append(
            f"| `{f['fieldname']}` | {f.get('label','')} | {f.get('fieldtype','')} | "
            f"{'✓' if f.get('reqd') else ''} |"
        )

    if doc.get("outgoing_links"):
        lines.append("\n## Links to")
        for lk in doc["outgoing_links"][:20]:
            lines.append(f"- `{lk['fieldname']}` → **{lk['links_to']}**")

    if doc.get("incoming_links"):
        lines.append("\n## Referenced by")
        for lk in doc["incoming_links"][:15]:
            lines.append(f"- **{lk['from_doctype']}** via `{lk['fieldname']}`")

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _tool_dictionary(args: dict) -> str:
    term = (args.get("term") or "").strip()
    if not term:
        return "Error: term is required"

    from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.data_dictionary import search_dictionary
    results = search_dictionary(term)

    if not results:
        return f"No fields found matching '{term}'."

    lines = [f"**{len(results)} match(es) for '{term}':**", ""]
    lines.append("| DocType | fieldname | label | type |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
    for r in results[:30]:
        lines.append(
            f"| {r.get('doctype','')} | `{r.get('field','')}` | "
            f"{r.get('label','')} | {r.get('fieldtype','')} |"
        )
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _tool_list_doctypes(args: dict) -> str:
    module = (args.get("module") or "").strip() or None

    from f_genbi.f_genbi.genbi.data_dictionary import get_data_dictionary
    docs = get_data_dictionary(module=module, limit=200)

    if not docs:
        return "No DocTypes found."

    # Group by module
    by_module: dict = {}
    for d in docs:
        m = d.get("module") or "Other"
        by_module.setdefault(m, []).append(d)

    lines = [f"**{len(docs)} DocType(s)**{f' in module {module}' if module else ''}:", ""]
    for mod, dts in sorted(by_module.items()):
        lines.append(f"### {mod}")
        for dt in dts:
            count = dt.get("row_count")
            count_str = f" ({count:,} rows)" if count is not None else ""
            lines.append(f"- **{dt['doctype']}**{count_str} — `{dt['table_name']}`")
        lines.append("")

    return "\n".join(lines)


# ── JSON-RPC helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _jsonrpc_result(result, req_id):
    frappe.response["type"] = "json"
    frappe.response["result"] = {
        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
        "id": req_id,
        "result": result,
    }
    return frappe.response["result"]


def _jsonrpc_error(code: int, message: str, id=None):
    frappe.response["type"] = "json"
    frappe.response["result"] = {
        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
        "id": id,
        "error": {"code": code, "message": message},
    }
    return frappe.response["result"]

frappe.pages["genbi-chat"].on_page_load = function (wrapper) {
	const page = frappe.ui.make_app_page({
		parent: wrapper,
		title: "GenBI Chat",
		single_column: true,
	});

	page.add_inner_button(__("History"), () => {
		window._genbi_chat?.toggle_sidebar();
	});

	page.add_inner_button(__("Settings"), () => {
		frappe.set_route("Form", "GenBI Settings");
	});

	page.add_inner_button(__("Query Log"), () => {
		frappe.set_route("List", "GenBI Query Log");
	});

	page.add_inner_button(__("Query Library"), () => {
		window._genbi_chat?.toggle_library();
	});

	$(page.body).html(get_page_html());

	window._genbi_chat = new GenBIChat(page);
};

					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

	</div>
</div>`;
}

class GenBIChat {
	constructor(page) {
		this.page = page;
		this.$body = $(page.body);
		this.message_counter = 0;
		this.chat_history = [];
		// 1.5 — session tracking (backend persists to GenBI Chat Session)
		this.session_id = null;
		// 2.5 — history sidebar
		this.sidebar_open = false;
		this._sessions_loaded = false;
		// 2.9 — input history navigation
		this.input_history = [];
		this.history_nav_idx = -1;
		this.history_draft = "";
		// 2.7 — last question (for feedback attribution)
		this._last_question = "";
		// 3.5 — query library
		this._library_open = false;
		this._library_loaded = false;
		this.setup_events();
		this.load_suggestions();
		this.load_templates();
		this._setup_voice();
	}

	// ─── Events ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	setup_events() {
		const self = this;
		const $input = this.$body.find("#genbi-input");

		this.$body.find("#genbi-send").on("click", () => self.send_question());

		// 2.9: Enter / Ctrl+Enter to send; ↑↓ to navigate history
		$input.on("keydown", (e) => {
			if (e.key === "Enter" || (e.ctrlKey && e.key === "Enter")) {
				self.send_question();
				return;
			}
			if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
				if (!self.input_history.length) return;
				if (self.history_nav_idx === -1) {
					self.history_draft = $input.val();
				}
				self.history_nav_idx = Math.min(
					self.history_nav_idx + 1,
					self.input_history.length - 1
				);
				$input.val(self.input_history[self.input_history.length - 1 - self.history_nav_idx]);
				e.preventDefault();
			} else if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
				if (self.history_nav_idx <= 0) {
					self.history_nav_idx = -1;
					$input.val(self.history_draft || "");
					return;
				}
				self.history_nav_idx--;
				$input.val(self.input_history[self.input_history.length - 1 - self.history_nav_idx]);
				e.preventDefault();
			}
		});

		// 2.9: Ctrl+K focuses input; Ctrl+H toggles history sidebar
		$(document).on("keydown.genbi_chat", (e) => {
			if (e.ctrlKey && e.key === "k") {
				e.preventDefault();
				$input.focus();
			} else if (e.ctrlKey && e.key === "h") {
				e.preventDefault();
				this.toggle_sidebar();
			}
		});

		// 2.5: History sidebar toggle buttons
		this.$body.find("#genbi-history-toggle").on("click", () => this.toggle_sidebar());
		this.$body.find("#genbi-sidebar-close").on("click", () => this.close_sidebar());
		this.$body.find("#genbi-new-chat").on("click", () => this.new_conversation());

		// 3.5: Library panel
		this.$body.find("#genbi-library-close").on("click", () => this.close_library());
		this.$body.find("#genbi-library-search").on("input", frappe.utils.debounce(() => {
			this._library_loaded = false;
			this.load_library();
		}, 350));
		this.$body.find("#genbi-library-mine").on("change", () => {
			this._library_loaded = false;
			this.load_library();
		});

		// Cleanup global listener when Frappe navigates away
		frappe.router.on("change", () => {
			$(document).off("keydown.genbi_chat");
		});
	}

	// ─── Suggestions & Templates ─────────────────────────────────────────────

	load_suggestions() {
		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_suggestions",
			callback: (r) => {
				if (r.message) this.render_suggestions(r.message);
			},
		});
	}

	render_suggestions(suggestions) {
		const container = this.$body.find("#genbi-suggestions");
		container.empty();
		suggestions.forEach((text) => {
			const chip = $(`<button class="genbi-chip">${frappe.utils.escape_html(text)}</button>`);
			chip.on("click", () => {
				this.$body.find("#genbi-input").val(text);
				this.send_question();
			});
			container.append(chip);
		});
	}

	load_templates() {
		const container = this.$body.find("#genbi-templates");
		const templates = [
			"Top 10 Customers by Revenue",
			"Monthly Sales Trend (Last 12 Months)",
			"Outstanding Receivables by Customer",
			"Top 10 Items by Quantity Sold",
			"Total Sales This Month",
			"Low Stock Items (Below 10)",
		];
		templates.forEach((key) => {
			const btn = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-template-btn">${frappe.utils.escape_html(key)}</button>`);
			btn.on("click", () => this.run_template(key));
			container.append(btn);
		});
	}

	run_template(template_key) {
		this._last_question = template_key;
		this.$body.find(".genbi-welcome").hide();
		this.$body.find("#genbi-suggestions").hide();
		this.add_message(template_key, "user");
		const thinking_id = this.add_thinking();

		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_quick_insight",
			args: { template_key },
			callback: (r) => {
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				if (r.message && r.message.success) {
					this.display_results(r.message);
				} else {
					this.add_message(`Error: ${r.message?.error || "Unknown error"}`, "error");
				}
			},
			error: () => {
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				this.add_message("Failed to execute template query.", "error");
			},
		});
	}

	// ─── Question Processing ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

	send_question() {
		const $input = this.$body.find("#genbi-input");
		const question = $input.val().trim();
		if (!question) return;

		$input.val("");

		// 2.9: push to input history, reset navigation
		this.input_history.push(question);
		if (this.input_history.length > 50) this.input_history.shift();
		this.history_nav_idx = -1;
		this.history_draft = "";

		this.$body.find(".genbi-welcome").hide();
		this.$body.find("#genbi-suggestions").hide();
		this.add_message(question, "user");
		const thinking_id = this.add_thinking();

		// 2.1: prefer streaming when Socket.IO is connected
		const use_stream = !!(frappe.realtime && frappe.realtime.socket && frappe.realtime.socket.connected);
		if (use_stream) {
			this._stream_question(question, thinking_id);
		} else {
			this._fetch_question(question, thinking_id);
		}
	}

	// 2.1: Streaming via Frappe Realtime (Socket.IO publish_realtime)
	_stream_question(question, thinking_id) {
		const stream_id = Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
		const event_name = `genbi_stream_${stream_id}`;
		let done = false;

		const cleanup = () => {
			if (!done) {
				done = true;
				frappe.realtime.off(event_name, handler);
			}
		};

		const handler = (data) => {
			if (data.event === "schema_ready") {
				const names = (data.doctypes || []).join(", ");
				if (names) this._update_thinking(thinking_id, __("Tables: {0}", [names]));

			} else if (data.event === "sql_ready") {
				this._update_thinking(thinking_id, __("Querying database\u2026"));

			} else if (data.event === "data_ready") {
				this._update_thinking(thinking_id, __("Summarizing {0} records\u2026", [data.result_count || 0]));

			} else if (data.event === "done") {
				cleanup();
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				if (data.success) {
					if (data.session_id && !this.session_id) this.session_id = data.session_id;
					this._sessions_loaded = false;
					this.chat_history.push({
						question,
						answer: data.explanation || "",
						sql: data.sql || "",
						chart_type: data.chart_config?.chart_type || "table",
					});
					if (this.chat_history.length > 10) this.chat_history = this.chat_history.slice(-10);
					this.display_results(data);
				} else {
					this.add_message(data.error || __("Something went wrong"), "error");
				}

			} else if (data.event === "error") {
				cleanup();
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				this.add_message(data.error || __("Something went wrong"), "error");
				if (data.suggestion) this.add_message(data.suggestion, "suggestion");
			}
		};

		frappe.realtime.on(event_name, handler);

		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.stream_question",
			args: {
				question,
				chat_history: JSON.stringify(this.chat_history),
				session_id: this.session_id || undefined,
				stream_id,
			},
			error: () => {
				cleanup();
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				this.add_message(__("Request failed. Please try again."), "error");
			},
		});

		// Safety: if no `done` event within 3 minutes, give up
		setTimeout(() => {
			if (!done) {
				cleanup();
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				this.add_message(__("Request timed out. Please try again."), "error");
			}
		}, 180_000);
	}

	// Fallback: plain frappe.call when Socket.IO is unavailable
	_fetch_question(question, thinking_id) {
		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.process_question",
			args: {
				question,
				chat_history: JSON.stringify(this.chat_history),
				session_id: this.session_id || undefined,
			},
			callback: (r) => {
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				if (r.message && r.message.success) {
					if (r.message.session_id && !this.session_id) this.session_id = r.message.session_id;
					this._sessions_loaded = false;
					// Neural Engine clarification fallback
					if (r.message.needs_clarification && frappe.genbi?.clarification?.show) {
						frappe.genbi.clarification.show(
							question,
							r.message.doctype || null,
							(clarified_result, clarified_q) => {
								this.chat_history.push({
									question: clarified_q,
									answer: clarified_result.explanation || "",
									sql: clarified_result.sql || "",
									chart_type: clarified_result.chart_config?.chart_type || "table",
								});
								if (this.chat_history.length > 10) this.chat_history = this.chat_history.slice(-10);
								this.display_results(clarified_result);
							}
						);
						return;
					}
					this.chat_history.push({
						question,
						answer: r.message.explanation || "",
						sql: r.message.sql || "",
						chart_type: r.message.chart_config?.chart_type || "table",
					});
					if (this.chat_history.length > 10) this.chat_history = this.chat_history.slice(-10);
					this.display_results(r.message);
				} else {
					this.add_message(r.message?.error || __("Something went wrong"), "error");
					if (r.message?.suggestion) this.add_message(r.message.suggestion, "suggestion");
				}
			},
			error: () => {
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				this.add_message(__("Request failed. Please try again."), "error");
			},
		});
	}

	// ─── Display Results ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	display_results(result) {
		const answer_text = result.explanation || result.answer || "";
		this.add_message(answer_text, "assistant");

		const config = result.chart_config;
		if (!config) {
			this.scroll_to_bottom();
			return;
		}

		const messages = this.$body.find("#genbi-messages");
		const cid = `chart-${++this.message_counter}`;

		if (config.chart_type === "number") {
			messages.append(this.render_number_card(config));
		} else {
			// ── Content area (chart or table) ─────────────────────────────────
			const content_area = $(`<div class="genbi-result-content" id="${cid}-content"></div>`);

			if (config.chart_type === "table") {
				content_area.html(GenBIChat.build_table_html(config.columns, config.rows));
			} else {
				content_area.html(`<div id="${cid}" class="genbi-chart-area"></div>`);
				this.render_frappe_chart(cid, config);

				// Collapsible raw-data table under the chart
				if (config.rows) {
					const toggle = $(`<div class="genbi-toggle-table">
						<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-show-table-btn">${__("Show Table")}</button>
					</div>`);
					const tbl = $(`<div id="${cid}-table" class="genbi-hidden-table" style="display:none;"></div>`);
					toggle.find(".genbi-show-table-btn").on("click", function () {
						if (tbl.is(":visible")) {
							tbl.hide(); $(this).text(__("Show Table"));
						} else {
							if (!tbl.children().length) {
								tbl.html(GenBIChat.build_table_html(config.columns, config.rows));
							}
							tbl.show(); $(this).text(__("Hide Table"));
						}
					});
					content_area.append(toggle).append(tbl);
				}
			}

			// ── 2.3: Chart type chips + 2.8: Copy/Download ───────────────────
			const actions_bar = this._build_actions_bar(config, cid, result);

			const block = $(`<div class="genbi-result-block"></div>`)
				.append(content_area)
				.append(actions_bar);
			messages.append(block);
		}

		// ── 2.2: SQL toggle (already existed, kept) ────────────────────────
		if (result.sql) {
			const sql_wrapper = $(`
				<div class="genbi-sql-wrapper">
					<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-show-sql-btn">${__("Show SQL")}</button>
					<pre class="genbi-sql-code" style="display:none;"><code>${frappe.utils.escape_html(result.sql)}</code></pre>
				</div>
			`);
			sql_wrapper.find(".genbi-show-sql-btn").on("click", function () {
				const code = sql_wrapper.find(".genbi-sql-code");
				if (code.is(":visible")) {
					code.hide(); $(this).text(__("Show SQL"));
				} else {
					code.show(); $(this).text(__("Hide SQL"));
				}
			});
			messages.append(sql_wrapper);
		}

		// ── Neural confidence panel ───────────────────────────────────────
		if (result.engine && result.engine.startsWith("neural:") && result.confidence) {
			messages.append(this._build_neural_confidence_panel(result));
		}

		// ── 8.6: Lightning source badge ───────────────────────────────────
		if (result.engine === "lightning-search") {
			const took = result.lightning_took_ms != null ? ` · ${result.lightning_took_ms} ms` : "";
			messages.append($(`
				<div style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;
					font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#fff;
					padding:2px 9px;border-radius:10px;
					background:linear-gradient(90deg,#5e64ff,#a855f7);
					margin:4px 0 6px 0;width:fit-content;">
					⚡ ${__("via MeiliSearch — live data")}${frappe.utils.escape_html(took)}
				</div>
			`));
		}

		// ── Meta line ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
		const meta_parts = [];
		if (result.result_count !== undefined) {
			meta_parts.push(`${result.result_count} record${result.result_count !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`);
		}
		if (result.execution_time !== undefined) {
			meta_parts.push(`${result.execution_time}s`);
		}
		if (result.engine && result.engine !== "lightning-search") {
			meta_parts.push(result.engine === "local-pattern" ? "local" : result.engine);
		}
		if (meta_parts.length) {
			this.add_message(meta_parts.join(" · "), "meta");
		}

		// ── 2.7: Feedback buttons ────────────────────────────────────────
		if (result.sql) {
			messages.append(this._build_feedback_bar(result.sql));
		}

		// ── 2.4: Follow-up suggestions ────────────────────────────────────
		const followups = this._follow_up_suggestions(result);
		if (followups.length) {
			const fu_block = $(`<div class="genbi-followup-block"></div>`);
			followups.forEach((q) => {
				const chip = $(`<button class="genbi-chip genbi-followup-chip">${frappe.utils.escape_html(q)}</button>`);
				chip.on("click", () => {
					this.$body.find("#genbi-input").val(q);
					this.send_question();
				});
				fu_block.append(chip);
			});
			messages.append(fu_block);
		}

		this.scroll_to_bottom();
	}

	// ── 2.3 + 2.8: Actions bar (chart type chips + copy/download) ─────────────

	_build_actions_bar(config, cid, result) {
		const bar = $(`<div class="genbi-actions-bar"></div>`);

		const has_multi_chart = config.labels && config.labels.length > 0
			&& config.datasets && config.datasets.length > 0;
		const has_table_data = config.columns && config.rows && config.rows.length > 0;

		// 2.3 — chart type chips
		if (has_multi_chart && has_table_data) {
			const chip_types = [
				{ type: "table", label: "Table" },
				{ type: "bar",   label: "Bar"   },
				{ type: "line",  label: "Line"  },
				{ type: "pie",   label: "Pie"   },
			];
			const chips_wrap = $(`<div class="genbi-chart-type-chips"></div>`);
			chip_types.forEach(({ type, label }) => {
				const is_active = config.chart_type === type
					|| (config.chart_type === "table" && type === "table");
				const chip = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs genbi-type-chip${is_active ? " active" : ""}">${label}</button>`);
				chip.on("click", () => {
					chips_wrap.find(".genbi-type-chip").removeClass("active");
					chip.addClass("active");
					const content = this.$body.find(`#${cid}-content`);
					// Remove chart + subtable, keep actions bar outside
					content.empty();
					if (type === "table") {
						content.html(GenBIChat.build_table_html(config.columns, config.rows));
					} else {
						const new_id = `${cid}-${type}`;
						content.html(`<div id="${new_id}" class="genbi-chart-area"></div>`);
						this.render_frappe_chart(new_id, { ...config, chart_type: type });
					}
				});
				chips_wrap.append(chip);
			});
			bar.append(chips_wrap);
		}

		// 2.8 — copy CSV / download CSV
		if (has_table_data) {
			const export_wrap = $(`<div class="genbi-export-btns"></div>`);

			const copy_btn = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-copy-btn">${__("Copy CSV")}</button>`);
			copy_btn.on("click", () => {
				const csv = GenBIChat.build_csv(config.columns, config.rows);
				const _done = () => {
					copy_btn.text(__("Copied!"));
					setTimeout(() => copy_btn.text(__("Copy CSV")), 2000);
				};
				if (navigator.clipboard) {
					navigator.clipboard.writeText(csv).then(_done).catch(_done);
				} else {
					_done();
				}
			});

			const dl_btn = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-dl-btn">${__("↓ CSV")}</button>`);
			dl_btn.on("click", () => {
				GenBIChat.download_csv(config.columns, config.rows, result.explanation || "genbi_data");
			});

			// 3.5 — save to library
			const save_btn = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-save-btn" title="${__("Save to Query Library")}">&#x2605; ${__("Save")}</button>`);
			save_btn.on("click", () => {
				const d = new frappe.ui.Dialog({
					title: __("Save to Query Library"),
					fields: [
						{ fieldtype: "Data", fieldname: "title", label: __("Title"), reqd: 1,
						  default: (result.explanation || this._last_question || "").slice(0, 60) },
						{ fieldtype: "Small Text", fieldname: "description", label: __("Notes") },
						{ fieldtype: "Data", fieldname: "tags", label: __("Tags (comma-separated)") },
						{ fieldtype: "Check", fieldname: "is_public", label: __("Share with all users") },
					],
					primary_action_label: __("Save"),
					primary_action: (values) => {
						d.hide();
						frappe.call({
							method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.save_query",
							args: {
								question: result.question || this._last_question || "",
								sql: result.sql || "",
								chart_type: config.chart_type || "table",
								title: values.title,
								description: values.description || "",
								tags: values.tags || "",
								is_public: values.is_public ? 1 : 0,
							},
							callback: (r) => {
								if (r.message?.success) {
									save_btn.text(__("Saved ✓")).prop("disabled", true);
									frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Saved to Query Library"), indicator: "green" }, 3);
								}
							},
						});
					},
				});
				d.show();
			});

			// 3.1 — add to workspace
			const ws_btn = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-ws-btn" title="${__("Add to Workspace")}">&#x1f4cc; ${__("Workspace")}</button>`);
			ws_btn.on("click", () => {
				frappe.genbi?.add_to_workspace_dialog?.(result);
			});

			// 4.4 — save as Frappe Query Report
			const report_btn = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-report-btn" title="${__("Save as Frappe Report")}">&#x1f4ca; ${__("Report")}</button>`);
			report_btn.on("click", () => {
				if (!result.sql) {
					frappe.show_alert({ message: __("No SQL to save as a report"), indicator: "orange" }, 3);
					return;
				}
				const d = new frappe.ui.Dialog({
					title: __("Save as Frappe Report"),
					fields: [
						{
							fieldtype: "Data",
							fieldname: "title",
							label: __("Report Name"),
							reqd: 1,
							default: (result.explanation || this._last_question || "").slice(0, 80),
						},
					],
					primary_action_label: __("Create Report"),
					primary_action: (values) => {
						d.hide();
						frappe.call({
							method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.create_query_report",
							args: {
								title: values.title,
								question: result.question || this._last_question || "",
								sql: result.sql,
							},
							callback: (r) => {
								const res = r && r.message;
								if (res && res.success) {
									report_btn.text(__("Saved ✓")).prop("disabled", true);
									frappe.show_alert({
										message: `${__("Report created:")} <a href="${res.url}" target="_blank">${res.report_name}</a>`,
										indicator: "green",
									}, 5);
								} else {
									frappe.show_alert({ message: res && res.error || __("Failed to create report"), indicator: "red" }, 4);
								}
							},
						});
					},
				});
				d.show();
			});

			// 10.5 — Share button
			const share_btn = $(`<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-share-btn">${__("Share")}</button>`);
			share_btn.on("click", () => {
				share_btn.prop("disabled", true).text(__("Sharing…"));
				frappe.call({
					method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.create_share",
					args: {
						question: result.question || "",
						sql: result.sql || "",
						rows_json: JSON.stringify(result.data || []),
						chart_config_json: JSON.stringify(result.chart_config || {}),
					},
					callback(r) {
						share_btn.prop("disabled", false).text(__("Share"));
						if (r.message && r.message.url) {
							frappe.utils.copy_to_clipboard(r.message.url);
							frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Share link copied to clipboard!"), indicator: "green" }, 4);
						}
					},
					error() {
						share_btn.prop("disabled", false).text(__("Share"));
						frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Could not create share link"), indicator: "red" }, 3);
					},
				});
			});

			export_wrap.append(copy_btn).append(dl_btn).append(save_btn).append(ws_btn).append(report_btn).append(share_btn);
			bar.append(export_wrap);
		}

		return bar;
	}

	// ── 2.4: Template-based follow-up suggestions ─────────────────────────────

	_follow_up_suggestions(result) {
		const sql = (result.sql || "").toLowerCase();

		if (sql.includes("sales invoice") && sql.includes("group by") && sql.includes("customer")) {
			return [
				__("Show monthly revenue trend"),
				__("Which items contributed most to sales?"),
				__("Show outstanding receivables"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("sales invoice") && sql.includes("date_format")) {
			return [
				__("Top 10 customers by revenue"),
				__("Compare this month vs last month"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("tabcustomer") && !sql.includes("group by")) {
			return [
				__("Show this customer's invoices"),
				__("Show outstanding receivables"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("purchase invoice") && sql.includes("group by") && sql.includes("supplier")) {
			return [
				__("Show monthly purchase trend"),
				__("Show pending purchase invoices"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("purchase invoice") && sql.includes("date_format")) {
			return [
				__("Top 10 suppliers by purchase"),
				__("Show pending purchase invoices"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("sales invoice item") || (sql.includes("item_code") && sql.includes("sum"))) {
			return [
				__("Show low stock items"),
				__("Show stock value by item"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("tabbin") || sql.includes("reorder_level")) {
			return [
				__("Top 10 items by stock value"),
				__("Show total inventory value"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("tabemployee")) {
			return [
				__("Show employees by department"),
				__("How many active employees?"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("tablead") || sql.includes("tabopportunity")) {
			return [
				__("Show open opportunities"),
				__("Leads by status"),
			];
		}
		if (sql.includes("outstanding_amount")) {
			return [
				__("Show total outstanding receivables"),
				__("Payments collected this month"),
			];
		}
		return [];
	}

	// ── 3.9: Voice Input (Web Speech API) ────────────────────────────────────

	_setup_voice() {
		const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
		if (!SR) return; // browser doesn't support — mic button stays hidden

		const $mic = this.$body.find("#genbi-mic").show();
		const $input = this.$body.find("#genbi-input");
		let recognizing = false;
		const rec = new SR();
		rec.lang = "en-US";
		rec.interimResults = false;
		rec.maxAlternatives = 1;

		rec.onstart = () => {
			recognizing = true;
			$mic.addClass("listening").attr("title", __("Listening\u2026"));
		};
		rec.onend = () => {
			recognizing = false;
			$mic.removeClass("listening").attr("title", __("Voice input"));
		};
		rec.onerror = (e) => {
			recognizing = false;
			$mic.removeClass("listening");
			if (e.error !== "no-speech") {
				frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Voice error: {0}", [e.error]), indicator: "red" }, 3);
			}
		};
		rec.onresult = (e) => {
			const transcript = e.results[0][0].transcript;
			$input.val(transcript);
			// Auto-send if confidence is high enough
			if (e.results[0][0].confidence > 0.7) {
				this.send_question();
			} else {
				$input.focus();
			}
		};

		$mic.on("click", () => {
			if (recognizing) {
				rec.stop();
			} else {
				rec.start();
			}
		});
	}

	// ── 2.7: Feedback bar ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	_build_feedback_bar(sql) {
		const bar = $(`<div class="genbi-feedback-bar"></div>`);
		const question = this._last_question;

		const up_btn = $(`<button class="genbi-feedback-btn" title="${__("Good answer")}" data-rating="up">&#x1f44d;</button>`);
		const down_btn = $(`<button class="genbi-feedback-btn" title="${__("Bad answer")}" data-rating="down">&#x1f44e;</button>`);
		const label = $(`<span class="genbi-feedback-label">${__("Was this helpful?")}</span>`);

		const _send = (rating, $btn) => {
			frappe.call({
				method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.record_query_feedback",
				args: { question, sql, rating },
			});
			bar.find(".genbi-feedback-btn").prop("disabled", true);
			$btn.addClass("active");
			label.text(rating === "up" ? __("Thanks for the feedback!") : __("Got it, we'll improve."));
		};

		up_btn.on("click", () => _send("up", up_btn));
		down_btn.on("click", () => _send("down", down_btn));

		bar.append(label).append(up_btn).append(down_btn);
		return bar;
	}

	// ── 2.5: History sidebar ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

	toggle_sidebar() {
		if (this.sidebar_open) {
			this.close_sidebar();
		} else {
			this.open_sidebar();
		}
	}

	open_sidebar() {
		this.$body.find("#genbi-body").addClass("genbi-sidebar-open");
		this.$body.find("#genbi-history-toggle").addClass("active");
		this.sidebar_open = true;
		if (!this._sessions_loaded) {
			this.load_session_list();
		}
	}

	close_sidebar() {
		this.$body.find("#genbi-body").removeClass("genbi-sidebar-open");
		this.$body.find("#genbi-history-toggle").removeClass("active");
		this.sidebar_open = false;
	}

	load_session_list() {
		const $list = this.$body.find("#genbi-sessions-list");
		$list.html(`<div class="genbi-sessions-loading text-muted">${__("Loading...")}</div>`);

		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_chat_sessions",
			args: { limit: 30 },
			callback: (r) => {
				this._sessions_loaded = true;
				const sessions = r.message || [];
				if (!sessions.length) {
					$list.html(`<div class="genbi-sessions-empty text-muted">${__("No past conversations yet.")}</div>`);
					return;
				}
				$list.empty();
				sessions.forEach((s) => this._render_session_row(s, $list));
			},
			error: () => {
				$list.html(`<div class="text-danger text-muted">${__("Failed to load history.")}</div>`);
			},
		});
	}

	_render_session_row(session, $container) {
		const title = frappe.utils.escape_html(session.session_title || session.name);
		const when = frappe.datetime.prettyDate(session.last_activity);
		const count = session.message_count || 0;
		const pinned = session.is_pinned ? "📌 " : "";
		const is_active = this.session_id && (
			this.session_id === session.name || this.session_id.startsWith(session.name)
		);

		const $row = $(`
			<div class="genbi-session-row${is_active ? " active" : ""}"
				 data-name="${frappe.utils.escape_html(session.name)}"
				 title="${title}">
				<div class="genbi-session-title">${pinned}${title}</div>
				<div class="genbi-session-meta">${when} · ${count} msgs</div>
			</div>
		`);

		$row.on("click", () => this.load_session(session.name, $row));
		$container.append($row);
	}

	load_session(session_name, $row) {
		// Highlight selected row
		this.$body.find(".genbi-session-row").removeClass("active");
		$row?.addClass("active");

		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_chat_session_history",
			args: { session_name },
			callback: (r) => {
				const turns = r.message || [];
				this._replay_session(session_name, turns);
				this.close_sidebar();
			},
		});
	}

	_replay_session(session_name, turns) {
		const $messages = this.$body.find("#genbi-messages");
		$messages.empty();

		if (!turns.length) {
			$messages.html(`<div class="text-muted text-center" style="padding:40px">${__("This conversation is empty.")}</div>`);
			return;
		}

		// Restore session_id so subsequent questions continue this session
		this.session_id = session_name;
		this.chat_history = [];

		turns.forEach((turn) => {
			if (turn.role === "user") {
				const q = typeof turn.message === "string" ? turn.message : JSON.stringify(turn.message);
				this.add_message(q, "user");
				this.chat_history.push({ question: q, answer: "", sql: "", chart_type: "table" });
			} else if (turn.role === "bot") {
				const msg = turn.message;
				const explanation = typeof msg === "string" ? msg : (msg?.explanation || "");
				const sql = typeof msg === "object" ? (msg?.sql || "") : "";
				const chart_type = typeof msg === "object" ? (msg?.chart_type || "table") : "table";
				const result_count = typeof msg === "object" ? (msg?.result_count ?? "") : "";

				this.add_message(explanation, "assistant");

				// Show stored SQL (collapsed by default)
				if (sql) {
					const sql_wrapper = $(`
						<div class="genbi-sql-wrapper">
							<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-show-sql-btn">${__("Show SQL")}</button>
							<pre class="genbi-sql-code" style="display:none;"><code>${frappe.utils.escape_html(sql)}</code></pre>
						</div>
					`);
					sql_wrapper.find(".genbi-show-sql-btn").on("click", function () {
						const code = sql_wrapper.find(".genbi-sql-code");
						if (code.is(":visible")) { code.hide(); $(this).text(__("Show SQL")); }
						else { code.show(); $(this).text(__("Hide SQL")); }
					});
					$messages.append(sql_wrapper);
				}

				// Re-run button to fetch fresh data for this query
				if (sql) {
					const $rerun = $(`<div class="genbi-rerun-wrap">
						<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-rerun-btn">
							&#x21bb; ${__("Re-run")}
						</button>
					</div>`);
					$rerun.find(".genbi-rerun-btn").on("click", () => {
						const q = this.chat_history[this.chat_history.length - 1]?.question || "";
						if (q) { this.$body.find("#genbi-input").val(q); this.send_question(); }
					});
					$messages.append($rerun);
				}

				// Update last chat_history entry
				if (this.chat_history.length) {
					const last = this.chat_history[this.chat_history.length - 1];
					last.answer = explanation;
					last.sql = sql;
					last.chart_type = chart_type;
				}

				if (result_count !== "") {
					this.add_message(`${result_count} records · ${chart_type} · ${__("historical")}`, "meta");
				}
			}
		});

		this.$body.find(".genbi-welcome").hide();
		this.$body.find("#genbi-suggestions").hide();
		// Keep chat_history trimmed
		if (this.chat_history.length > 10) this.chat_history = this.chat_history.slice(-10);
		this.scroll_to_bottom();
	}

	new_conversation() {
		// Clear messages, reset session so next question starts a fresh session
		this.session_id = null;
		this.chat_history = [];
		this.message_counter = 0;
		const $messages = this.$body.find("#genbi-messages");
		$messages.empty();
		$messages.html(`
			<div class="genbi-welcome">
				<div class="genbi-welcome-icon"><img class="genbi-welcome-logo" src="/assets/f_genbi/images/genbi_logo.svg" alt="GenBI logo" /></div>
				<h3>${__("New Conversation")}</h3>
				<p>${__("Ask a new question to start fresh.")}</p>
			</div>
		`);
		this.$body.find("#genbi-input").val("").focus();
		// Force session list to reload next time sidebar opens
		this._sessions_loaded = false;
		this.close_sidebar();
	}

	// ── 3.5: Query Library ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	toggle_library() {
		if (this._library_open) this.close_library();
		else this.open_library();
	}

	open_library() {
		// Close history sidebar if open
		if (this.sidebar_open) this.close_sidebar();
		this.$body.find("#genbi-body").addClass("genbi-library-open");
		this._library_open = true;
		if (!this._library_loaded) this.load_library();
	}

	close_library() {
		this.$body.find("#genbi-body").removeClass("genbi-library-open");
		this._library_open = false;
	}

	load_library() {
		const $list = this.$body.find("#genbi-library-list");
		$list.html(`<div class="genbi-sessions-loading text-muted">${__("Loading\u2026")}</div>`);

		const search = this.$body.find("#genbi-library-search").val() || "";
		const only_mine = this.$body.find("#genbi-library-mine").is(":checked") ? 1 : 0;

		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.get_saved_queries",
			args: { search, only_mine, limit: 50 },
			callback: (r) => {
				this._library_loaded = true;
				const rows = r.message || [];
				if (!rows.length) {
					$list.html(`<div class="genbi-sessions-empty text-muted">${__("No saved queries yet. Save a result using the \u2605 button below any answer.")}</div>`);
					return;
				}
				$list.empty();
				rows.forEach((row) => this._render_library_row(row, $list));
			},
			error: () => {
				$list.html(`<div class="text-danger text-muted">${__("Failed to load library.")}</div>`);
			},
		});
	}

	_render_library_row(row, $container) {
		const title = frappe.utils.escape_html(row.title || row.question);
		const tags = row.tags ? row.tags.split(",").map(t => `<span class="genbi-tag">${frappe.utils.escape_html(t.trim())}</span>`).join("") : "";
		const pub = row.is_public ? `<span class="genbi-tag genbi-tag-public">${__("public")}</span>` : "";
		const pin = row.is_pinned ? "&#x1f4cc; " : "";

		const $row = $(`
			<div class="genbi-library-row" data-name="${frappe.utils.escape_html(row.name)}" title="${frappe.utils.escape_html(row.question)}">
				<div class="genbi-session-title">${pin}${title}</div>
				<div class="genbi-library-row-meta">${pub}${tags}</div>
				<div class="genbi-library-row-actions">
					<button class="btn btn-xs btn-primary genbi-lib-run-btn">${__("Run")}</button>
					<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-lib-fill-btn">${__("Edit \u2192 Chat")}</button>
					<button class="btn btn-xs btn-danger genbi-lib-del-btn">&#x1f5d1;</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		`);

		$row.find(".genbi-lib-run-btn").on("click", () => {
			this.close_library();
			this._run_saved_query(row.name, row.title);
		});
		$row.find(".genbi-lib-fill-btn").on("click", () => {
			this.close_library();
			this.$body.find("#genbi-input").val(row.question).focus();
		});
		$row.find(".genbi-lib-del-btn").on("click", () => {
			frappe.confirm(__("Delete saved query '{0}'?", [row.title]), () => {
				frappe.call({
					method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.delete_saved_query",
					args: { name: row.name },
					callback: () => {
						$row.remove();
						frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Deleted"), indicator: "green" }, 2);
					},
				});
			});
		});

		$container.append($row);
	}

	_run_saved_query(name, title) {
		this.add_message(__("Running saved query: {0}", [title]), "user");
		const thinking_id = this.add_thinking();
		frappe.call({
			method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.run_saved_query",
			args: { name },
			callback: (r) => {
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				if (r.message?.success) {
					this._sessions_loaded = false;
					this.display_results(r.message);
				} else {
					this.add_message(r.message?.error || __("Error running query"), "error");
				}
			},
			error: () => {
				this.remove_thinking(thinking_id);
				this.add_message(__("Failed to run saved query."), "error");
			},
		});
	}

	// ── CSV helpers (2.8) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	static build_csv(columns, rows) {
		const esc = (v) => {
			const s = String(v ?? "");
			return s.includes(",") || s.includes('"') || s.includes("\n")
				? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
		};
		const header = columns.map(esc).join(",");
		const body = rows.map((row) => columns.map((c) => esc(row[c] ?? "")).join(",")).join("\n");
		return header + "\n" + body;
	}

	static download_csv(columns, rows, label) {
		const csv = GenBIChat.build_csv(columns, rows);
		const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });
		const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
		const a = document.createElement("a");
		a.href = url;
		a.download = label.replace(/[^\w\s-]/g, "").trim().slice(0, 50) + ".csv";
		document.body.appendChild(a);
		a.click();
		document.body.removeChild(a);
		URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
	}

	// ─── Neural Confidence Panel ──────────────────────────────────────────────

	_build_neural_confidence_panel(result) {
		const conf_pct  = Math.round((result.confidence || 0) * 100);
		const layer     = (result.engine || "").replace("neural:", "");
		const layer_labels = {
			"memory":         __("Vector Memory"),
			"pattern":        __("Pattern Engine"),
			"schema-compiler":__("Schema Compiler"),
			"slot":           __("Neural Slot Filler"),
			"seq2seq":        __("T5-small Seq2Seq"),
			"decomposer":     __("Query Decomposer"),
		};
		const layer_label = layer_labels[layer] || layer;

		const bar_color = conf_pct >= 90 ? "#28a745"
		                : conf_pct >= 75 ? "#fd7e14"
		                : "#dc3545";

		const $panel = $(`
			<div class="genbi-neural-confidence collapsed" style="
				margin:4px 0 2px;font-size:12px;
				border:1px solid var(--border-color);border-radius:6px;
				overflow:hidden;">
				<div class="genbi-conf-header" style="
					display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;
					padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;
					background:var(--fg-color);">
					<span style="font-weight:600;color:var(--text-muted);">🧠 ${__("Neural Engine")}</span>
					<span style="flex:1;height:6px;background:var(--border-color);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden;">
						<span style="display:block;height:100%;width:${conf_pct}%;background:${bar_color};transition:width .4s;"></span>
					</span>
					<span style="color:${bar_color};font-weight:700;">${conf_pct}%</span>
					<span class="conf-chevron" style="color:var(--text-muted);">▾</span>
				</div>
				<div class="genbi-conf-body" style="display:none;padding:8px 12px;background:var(--subtle-fg);border-top:1px solid var(--border-color);">
					<table style="width:100%;font-size:12px;border-collapse:collapse;">
						<tr>
							<td style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:2px 6px 2px 0;">${__("Layer")}</td>
							<td style="font-weight:500;">${frappe.utils.escape_html(layer_label)}</td>
						</tr>
						<tr>
							<td style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:2px 6px 2px 0;">${__("Confidence")}</td>
							<td style="font-weight:500;">${conf_pct}%</td>
						</tr>
						${result.slots ? _render_slot_rows(result.slots) : ""}
					</table>
					<div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:6px;">
						<button class="btn btn-xs btn-default genbi-conf-correct-btn">
							✏️ ${__("Correct this")}
						</button>
						<button class="btn btn-xs btn-primary genbi-conf-looks-right-btn">
							✅ ${__("Looks right")}
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		`);

		// Toggle expand/collapse
		$panel.find(".genbi-conf-header").on("click", () => {
			const $body = $panel.find(".genbi-conf-body");
			const $chev = $panel.find(".conf-chevron");
			if ($body.is(":visible")) {
				$body.slideUp(150);
				$chev.text("▾");
			} else {
				$body.slideDown(150);
				$chev.text("▴");
			}
		});

		// "Looks right" → thumbs-up feedback
		$panel.find(".genbi-conf-looks-right-btn").on("click", () => {
			if (result.sql) {
				frappe.call({
					method: "f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.record_query_feedback",
					args: { sql: result.sql, question: result.question || "", rating: "good" },
				});
			}
			frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Thanks! This improves the Neural Engine."), indicator: "green" }, 3);
			$panel.find(".genbi-conf-body").slideUp(150);
		});

		// "Correct this" → open clarification dialog
		$panel.find(".genbi-conf-correct-btn").on("click", () => {
			if (frappe.genbi?.clarification?.show) {
				frappe.genbi.clarification.show(
					result.question || "",
					(result.slots?.entity) || null,
					(clarified_result, clarified_q) => {
						this.display_results(clarified_result);
					}
				);
			}
		});

		return $panel;
	}

	// ─── Chart Rendering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	render_frappe_chart(container_id, config) {
		setTimeout(() => {
			try {
				const el = document.getElementById(container_id);
				if (!el) return;
				const chart = new frappe.Chart(el, {
					title: config.title || "",
					data: {
						labels: config.labels || [],
						datasets: config.datasets || [],
					},
					type: config.chart_type,
					height: 300,
					colors: ["#5e64ff","#ff5858","#ffa00a","#28a745","#17a2b8","#6f42c1","#fd7e14"],
					animate: 1,
					truncateLegends: 1,
				});

				// 3.2: Chart drilldown — clicking a bar/pie segment fires a follow-up query
				this._attach_chart_drilldown(el, chart, config);

			} catch (e) {
				console.error("Chart render error:", e);
				const el = document.getElementById(container_id);
				if (el) {
					el.innerHTML = '<p class="text-muted">Could not render chart.</p>';
					if (config.rows) {
						$(el).append(GenBIChat.build_table_html(config.columns, config.rows));
					}
				}
			}
		}, 100);
	}

	// 3.2: Chart Drilldown ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	_attach_chart_drilldown(el, chart, config) {
		// frappe.Chart fires a custom 'data_select' event on the SVG element.
		// We listen at the container level via DOM event delegation.
		$(el).on("data_select", (e) => {
			const label = e.originalEvent?.detail?.label || e.detail?.label;
			if (!label) return;
			this._drilldown_on_label(label, config);
		});

		// frappe.Chart also exposes chart.parent — attach click to SVG data points
		// as a fallback for versions that don't fire data_select
		if (chart && chart.parent) {
			$(chart.parent).on("click", ".data-point, .bar, path[data-point-index]", (e) => {
				// Try to read the label from the nearest point index
				const idx = parseInt(
					$(e.currentTarget).attr("data-point-index") ||
					$(e.currentTarget).closest("[data-point-index]").attr("data-point-index"),
					10
				);
				if (isNaN(idx)) return;
				const label = (config.labels || [])[idx];
				if (label) this._drilldown_on_label(label, config);
			});
		}
	}

	_drilldown_on_label(label, config) {
		// Build a natural-language drilldown question from the chart context
		const title = (config.title || "").toLowerCase();
		let question;
		if (title.includes("customer") || (config.x_field || "").includes("customer")) {
			question = `Show details for customer "${label}"`;
		} else if (title.includes("item") || (config.x_field || "").includes("item")) {
			question = `Show sales details for item "${label}"`;
		} else if (title.includes("supplier") || (config.x_field || "").includes("supplier")) {
			question = `Show details for supplier "${label}"`;
		} else {
			question = `Show details for "${label}"`;
		}
		this.$body.find("#genbi-input").val(question);
		frappe.show_alert({ message: __("Drilling down into: {0}", [label]), indicator: "blue" }, 2);
		this.send_question();
	}

	render_number_card(config) {
		return $(`
			<div class="genbi-number-card">
				<div class="genbi-number-value">${frappe.utils.escape_html(String(config.value))}</div>
				<div class="genbi-number-label">${frappe.utils.escape_html(String(config.label))}</div>
			</div>
		`);
	}

	render_table(config, container_id) {
		const wrapper = $(`<div class="genbi-table-wrapper" id="${container_id}"></div>`);
		wrapper.html(GenBIChat.build_table_html(config.columns, config.rows));
		return wrapper;
	}

	static build_table_html(columns, rows) {
		if (!columns || !rows || !rows.length) {
			return '<p class="text-muted">No data to display</p>';
		}
		let html = '<div class="genbi-table-scroll"><table class="table table-bordered table-sm genbi-data-table">';
		html += "<thead><tr>";
		columns.forEach((col) => { html += `<th>${frappe.utils.escape_html(col)}</th>`; });
		html += "</tr></thead><tbody>";
		const max_rows = Math.min(rows.length, 100);
		for (let i = 0; i < max_rows; i++) {
			html += "<tr>";
			columns.forEach((col) => {
				let val = rows[i][col];
				if (val === null || val === undefined) val = "";
				if (typeof val === "number") val = val.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
				html += `<td>${frappe.utils.escape_html(String(val))}</td>`;
			});
			html += "</tr>";
		}
		html += "</tbody></table></div>";
		if (rows.length > 100) {
			html += `<p class="text-muted text-center">Showing first 100 of ${rows.length} rows</p>`;
		}
		return html;
	}

	// ─── Message Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	add_message(content, type) {
		const messages = this.$body.find("#genbi-messages");
		const css_class_map = {
			user: "genbi-msg-user",
			assistant: "genbi-msg-assistant",
			error: "genbi-msg-error",
			suggestion: "genbi-msg-suggestion",
			meta: "genbi-msg-meta",
		};
		let text = content;
		if (text === null || text === undefined) text = "";
		else if (typeof text === "object") text = JSON.stringify(text);
		else text = String(text);
		if (!text.trim()) return;
		const css_class = css_class_map[type] || "genbi-msg-assistant";
		const escaped = frappe.utils.escape_html(text).replace(/\n/g, "<br>");
		messages.append($(`<div class="genbi-msg ${css_class}">${escaped}</div>`));
		this.scroll_to_bottom();
	}

	add_thinking() {
		const id = `thinking-${Date.now()}`;
		this.$body.find("#genbi-messages").append(`
			<div id="${id}" class="genbi-msg genbi-msg-thinking">
				<span class="genbi-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></span>
				<span class="genbi-thinking-text">${__("Analyzing your question\u2026")}</span>
			</div>
		`);
		this.scroll_to_bottom();
		return id;
	}

	remove_thinking(id) {
		this.$body.find(`#${id}`).remove();
	}

	// 2.1: update text inside an existing thinking bubble
	_update_thinking(id, text) {
		const $el = this.$body.find(`#${id}`);
		if ($el.length) {
			$el.find(".genbi-thinking-text").text(text);
		}
	}

	scroll_to_bottom() {
		const container = this.$body.find(".genbi-messages-container")[0];
		if (container) container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
	}
}

// ── Neural slot row renderer (outside class) ──────────────────────────────────

function _render_slot_rows(slots) {
	if (!slots || typeof slots !== "object") return "";
	const SLOT_LABELS = {
		intent:        __("Intent"),
		entity:        __("Table"),
		group_by:      __("Group by"),
		agg_type:      __("Aggregation"),
		date_ref:      __("Period"),
		limit_n:       __("Limit"),
		filter_status: __("Status"),
		value_keyword: __("Value field"),
	};
	return Object.entries(SLOT_LABELS)
		.filter(([k]) => slots[k] && slots[k] !== "NONE")
		.map(([k, label]) =>
			`<tr>
				<td style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:2px 6px 2px 0;">${label}</td>
				<td style="font-weight:500;">${frappe.utils.escape_html(String(slots[k]))}</td>
			</tr>`
		).join("");
}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/f_genbi/report/customer_sales_summary/customer_sales_summary.py
import frappe


def execute(filters=None):
	columns = get_columns()
	data = get_data(filters)
	return columns, data


def get_columns():
	return [
		{
			"label": "Customer",
			"fieldname": "customer_name",
			"fieldtype": "Link",
			"options": "Customer",
			"width": 220,
		},
		{
			"label": "No. of Orders",
			"fieldname": "order_count",
			"fieldtype": "Int",
			"width": 130,
		},
		{
			"label": "Total Amount",
			"fieldname": "total_amount",
			"fieldtype": "Currency",
			"width": 160,
		},
	]


def get_data(filters=None):
	conditions = ""
	if filters:
		if filters.get("customer"):
			conditions += " AND so.customer = %(customer)s"
		if filters.get("from_date"):
			conditions += " AND so.transaction_date >= %(from_date)s"
		if filters.get("to_date"):
			conditions += " AND so.transaction_date <= %(to_date)s"

	return frappe.db.sql(
		f"""
		SELECT
			so.customer_name,
			COUNT(so.name) AS order_count,
			SUM(so.grand_total) AS total_amount
		FROM
			`tabSales Order` so
		WHERE
			so.docstatus = 0
			{conditions}
		GROUP BY
			so.customer_name
		ORDER BY
			total_amount DESC
		""",
		filters or {},
		as_dict=True,
	)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/__init__.py
"""
GenBI (Generative BI) - AI-powered conversational canvas builder.

Components:

- EntityResolver: Extracts and disambiguates DocType entities from queries
- ConversationState: Manages multi-turn conversation context
- RelationshipExplainer: Generates human-readable relationship explanations
- QueryParser: Parses complex queries into canvas configurations

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/anomaly_detector.py
"""
Task 3.7 — Anomaly Detection + Proactive Alerts

Runs as a daily scheduled task. For each active GenBI Widget / pinned Saved
Query it compares today's metric value to the 30-day rolling average. If the
deviation exceeds 25% (default threshold), a Frappe Notification is sent to
the widget owner.

Metric queries tracked:
  - total_sales_today      → SUM(grand_total) from Sales Invoice WHERE docstatus=1 AND posting_date=CURDATE()
  - total_purchases_today  → SUM(grand_total) from Purchase Invoice
  - open_leads_count       → COUNT(*) from Lead WHERE status NOT IN ('Converted','Lost')
  - low_stock_count        → COUNT(*) from Bin WHERE actual_qty < reorder_level AND reorder_level > 0
  - overdue_receivables    → SUM(outstanding_amount) from Sales Invoice WHERE docstatus=1 AND due_date < CURDATE()

Plus any GenBI Widget whose saved_query has a single numeric result (auto-detected).
"""

import frappe
from frappe import _

_DEVIATION_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT = 0.25  # 25% — overridden by GenBI Settings.anomaly_deviation_threshold
_HISTORY_DAYS = 30

# ── Built-in metric definitions ───────────────────────────────────────────────

_BUILTIN_METRICS = [
	{
		"name": "Daily Sales Total",
		"sql": "SELECT SUM(`grand_total`) AS value FROM `tabSales Invoice` WHERE `docstatus`=1 AND `posting_date`=CURDATE()",
		"history_sql": (
			"SELECT `posting_date` AS dt, SUM(`grand_total`) AS value "
			"FROM `tabSales Invoice` WHERE `docstatus`=1 "
			"AND `posting_date` >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL {days} DAY) "
			"AND `posting_date` < CURDATE() "
			"GROUP BY `posting_date`"
		),
		"unit": "currency",
	},
	{
		"name": "Daily Purchases Total",
		"sql": "SELECT SUM(`grand_total`) AS value FROM `tabPurchase Invoice` WHERE `docstatus`=1 AND `posting_date`=CURDATE()",
		"history_sql": (
			"SELECT `posting_date` AS dt, SUM(`grand_total`) AS value "
			"FROM `tabPurchase Invoice` WHERE `docstatus`=1 "
			"AND `posting_date` >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL {days} DAY) "
			"AND `posting_date` < CURDATE() "
			"GROUP BY `posting_date`"
		),
		"unit": "currency",
	},
	{
		"name": "Open Leads Count",
		"sql": "SELECT COUNT(*) AS value FROM `tabLead` WHERE `status` NOT IN ('Converted','Lost')",
		"history_sql": None,  # point-in-time — no history comparison
		"unit": "count",
	},
	{
		"name": "Overdue Receivables",
		"sql": (
			"SELECT SUM(`outstanding_amount`) AS value FROM `tabSales Invoice` "
			"WHERE `docstatus`=1 AND `outstanding_amount`>0 AND `due_date`<CURDATE()"
		),
		"history_sql": None,
		"unit": "currency",
	},
]


def run_anomaly_detection():
	"""Entry point for the daily scheduler hook."""
	if not frappe.db.exists("DocType", "GenBI Settings"):
		return
	try:
		settings = frappe.get_single("GenBI Settings")
		if not settings.enabled:
			return
		if not settings.get("anomaly_enabled", 1):
			return
	except Exception:
		return

	threshold_pct = frappe.utils.cint(settings.get("anomaly_deviation_threshold") or 25)
	threshold = threshold_pct / 100.0

	alerts = []
	alerts.extend(_check_builtin_metrics(threshold))
	alerts.extend(_check_widget_metrics(threshold))

	for alert in alerts:
		_send_alert(alert)


def _check_builtin_metrics(threshold=_DEVIATION_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT):
	alerts = []
	for metric in _BUILTIN_METRICS:
		if not metric.get("history_sql"):
			continue
		try:
			today_row = frappe.db.sql(metric["sql"], as_dict=True)
			today_val = float(today_row[0].get("value") or 0) if today_row else 0.0

			hist_sql = metric["history_sql"].format(days=_HISTORY_DAYS)
			hist_rows = frappe.db.sql(hist_sql, as_dict=True)
			if not hist_rows:
				continue

			daily_vals = [float(r.get("value") or 0) for r in hist_rows if r.get("value") is not None]
			if not daily_vals:
				continue
			avg = sum(daily_vals) / len(daily_vals)
			if avg == 0:
				continue

			deviation = (today_val - avg) / avg  # positive = above avg

			if abs(deviation) >= threshold:
				direction = "up" if deviation > 0 else "down"
				pct = round(abs(deviation) * 100)
				alerts.append({
					"metric": metric["name"],
					"today": today_val,
					"avg": avg,
					"deviation_pct": pct,
					"direction": direction,
					"unit": metric["unit"],
					"recipient": "Administrator",
				})
		except Exception:
			pass
	return alerts


def _check_widget_metrics(threshold=_DEVIATION_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT):
	"""Check GenBI Widgets with a single numeric saved_query result."""
	alerts = []
	try:
		widgets = frappe.get_all(
			"GenBI Widget",
			filters={"saved_query": ["is", "set"]},
			fields=["name", "title", "saved_query", "owner_user"],
		)
	except Exception:
		return alerts

	for w in widgets:
		try:
			sq = frappe.get_doc("GenBI Saved Query", w.saved_query)
			if not sq.generated_sql:
				continue
