        order_by="modified desc",
        limit=int(limit),
        ignore_permissions=True,
    )

    buf = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.DictWriter(buf, fieldnames=_COLUMNS)
    writer.writeheader()
    for row in rows:
        q   = (row.question or "").strip()
        sql = (row.generated_sql or "").strip()
        if not q or not sql or not sql.upper().startswith("SELECT"):
            continue
        slots = generate_labels_from_sql(sql)
        slots["question"] = q
        writer.writerow({c: slots.get(c, "") for c in _COLUMNS})

    return buf.getvalue()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/genbi/training_loop.py
"""
Task 3.8 — GenBI Training Loop

Two responsibilities:
  1. promote_feedback_to_training()  — called by record_query_feedback (👍 path)
     Saves a GenBI Training Example from a positively-rated query.

  2. get_few_shot_examples(question, n=3)  — called by _convert_to_sql()
     Returns the n most semantically similar approved training examples
     to inject as few-shot pairs into the LLM system prompt.
"""

import re
import frappe

_FEW_SHOT_CACHE_KEY = "genbi_few_shot_examples_v1"
_FEW_SHOT_CACHE_TTL = 3600  # 1 hour


def promote_feedback_to_training(question: str, sql: str) -> None:
	"""Create a GenBI Training Example from a 👍-rated query.

	Silently no-ops if:
	  - SQL is empty or not a SELECT
	  - An identical question already exists
	  - The DocType doesn't exist yet (pre-migration)
	"""
	if not sql or not question:
		return
	if not sql.strip().upper().startswith("SELECT"):
		return
	try:
		if not frappe.db.exists("DocType", "GenBI Training Example"):
			return
		# Avoid duplicates
		if frappe.db.exists("GenBI Training Example", {"question": question}):
			return
		doc = frappe.get_doc({
			"doctype": "GenBI Training Example",
			"question": question,
			"generated_sql": sql,
			"quality": "Good",
			"is_approved": 1,
			"source_type": "Feedback (👍)",
			"contributed_by": frappe.session.user,
		})
		doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
		frappe.db.commit()
		# Bust the cache so next request picks up the new example
		frappe.cache().delete_value(_FEW_SHOT_CACHE_KEY)
	except Exception:
		pass


def get_few_shot_examples(question: str, n: int = 3) -> list[dict]:
	"""Return up to n approved training examples most relevant to the question.

	Uses fast token-overlap scoring (no ML inference) so this is always cheap.
	Falls back to returning the n most-recent approved examples if scoring fails.

	Returns:
	    [{"question": str, "sql": str}, ...]
	"""
	try:
		if not frappe.db.exists("DocType", "GenBI Training Example"):
			return []

		examples = _get_cached_examples()
		if not examples:
			return []

		question_tokens = set(re.findall(r"\b[a-z]{3,}\b", question.lower()))
		if not question_tokens:
			return examples[:n]

		scored = []
		for ex in examples:
			ex_tokens = set(re.findall(r"\b[a-z]{3,}\b", ex["question"].lower()))
			overlap = len(question_tokens & ex_tokens)
			if overlap > 0:
				scored.append((overlap, ex))

		scored.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])
		return [ex for _, ex in scored[:n]]
	except Exception:
		return []


def _get_cached_examples() -> list[dict]:
	"""Load approved training examples, cached for 1 hour."""
	import json
	cached = frappe.cache().get_value(_FEW_SHOT_CACHE_KEY)
	if cached:
		try:
			return json.loads(cached)
		except Exception:
			pass

	try:
		rows = frappe.get_all(
			"GenBI Training Example",
			filters={"is_approved": 1, "quality": ["!=", "Rejected"]},
			fields=["question", "generated_sql"],
			order_by="modified desc",
			limit=200,
		)
		examples = [{"question": r["question"], "sql": r["generated_sql"]} for r in rows]
		frappe.cache().set_value(_FEW_SHOT_CACHE_KEY, json.dumps(examples), expires_in_sec=_FEW_SHOT_CACHE_TTL)
		return examples
	except Exception:
		return []


def build_few_shot_prompt_section(question: str, n: int = 3) -> str:
	"""Build the few-shot examples string to inject into the LLM system prompt.

	Returns an empty string if no examples are available.
	"""
	examples = get_few_shot_examples(question, n)
	if not examples:
		return ""

	lines = ["\nFEW-SHOT EXAMPLES (question → correct SQL from this system):"]
	for i, ex in enumerate(examples, 1):
		lines.append(f"\nExample {i}:")
		lines.append(f"  Q: {ex['question']}")
		lines.append(f"  SQL: {ex['sql'].strip()}")
	lines.append("\nApply the same patterns and table/column names as these examples.\n")
	return "\n".join(lines)


# ── Task 4.10: Fine-Tuning Dataset Export ────────────────────────────���────────

def export_training_dataset(fmt: str = "alpaca", limit: int = 2000) -> dict:
	"""Task 4.10: Export approved training examples as a JSONL fine-tuning dataset.

	Formats supported:
	  alpaca  — {"instruction": "...", "input": "", "output": "SELECT ..."}
	  openai  — {"messages": [{"role": "user", ...}, {"role": "assistant", ...}]}
	  raw     — {"question": "...", "sql": "..."}

	Returns {"success": True, "format": fmt, "count": N, "jsonl": "<lines>"}
	"""
	import json as _json

	fmt = (fmt or "alpaca").lower()
	if fmt not in ("alpaca", "openai", "raw"):
		return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown format '{fmt}'. Use: alpaca, openai, raw"}

	try:
		if not frappe.db.exists("DocType", "GenBI Training Example"):
			return {"success": False, "error": "GenBI Training Example DocType not installed."}

		rows = frappe.get_all(
			"GenBI Training Example",
			filters={"is_approved": 1, "quality": ["in", ["Good", "Excellent"]]},
			fields=["question", "generated_sql", "quality", "notes"],
			order_by="modified desc",
			limit=int(limit),
			ignore_permissions=True,
		)

		if not rows:
			return {"success": True, "format": fmt, "count": 0, "jsonl": ""}

		lines = []
		for r in rows:
			q = (r.question or "").strip()
			sql = (r.generated_sql or "").strip()
			if not q or not sql:
				continue

			if fmt == "alpaca":
				obj = {
					"instruction": (
						"You are an ERPNext SQL expert. Convert the following business question "
						"into a valid SQL SELECT query for a Frappe/ERPNext database."
					),
					"input": q,
					"output": sql,
				}
			elif fmt == "openai":
				obj = {
					"messages": [
						{
							"role": "system",
							"content": (
								"You are GenBI, an ERPNext SQL assistant. "
								"Convert business questions to SQL SELECT queries."
							),
						},
						{"role": "user", "content": q},
						{"role": "assistant", "content": sql},
					]
				}
			else:  # raw
				obj = {"question": q, "sql": sql}

			lines.append(_json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False))

		return {
			"success": True,
			"format": fmt,
			"count": len(lines),
			"jsonl": "\n".join(lines),
		}

	except Exception as e:
		return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}


# ── Phase 5.4a: FAISS Vector Memory ─────────────────────────────────────────

_FAISS_CACHE_KEY = "genbi_faiss_index_built"
_faiss_index = None        # faiss.IndexFlatIP | None
_faiss_examples: list = [] # parallel list of {"question", "sql"} dicts


def embed_all_examples() -> bool:
    """
    Build a FAISS flat-IP index over all approved training examples.
    Uses sentence-transformers all-MiniLM-L6-v2 (already loaded in Phase 1).
    Safe to call at startup — returns False silently if faiss/ST not installed.
    """
    global _faiss_index, _faiss_examples
    try:
        import faiss
        import numpy as np
        from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

        if not frappe.db.exists("DocType", "GenBI Training Example"):
            return False

        rows = frappe.get_all(
            "GenBI Training Example",
            filters={"is_approved": 1, "quality": ["in", ["Good", "Excellent"]]},
            fields=["question", "generated_sql"],
            order_by="modified desc",
            limit=2000,
            ignore_permissions=True,
        )
        if not rows:
            return False

        examples = [{"question": r.question, "sql": r.generated_sql} for r in rows]
        questions = [e["question"] for e in examples]

        model = SentenceTransformer("all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
        embeddings = model.encode(questions, convert_to_numpy=True, normalize_embeddings=True)
        embeddings = embeddings.astype("float32")

        index = faiss.IndexFlatIP(embeddings.shape[1])
        index.add(embeddings)

        _faiss_index = index
        _faiss_examples = examples
        frappe.cache().set_value(_FAISS_CACHE_KEY, 1, expires_in_sec=3600)
        return True

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger().debug(f"FAISS embed_all_examples: {e}")
        return False


def find_nearest(question: str, threshold: float = 0.92) -> tuple[dict | None, float]:
    """
    Return the closest training example to the question if similarity >= threshold.

    Returns (example_dict, score) or (None, 0.0).
    """
    global _faiss_index, _faiss_examples
    if _faiss_index is None:
        return None, 0.0

    try:
        import numpy as np
        from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

        model = SentenceTransformer("all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
        vec = model.encode([question], convert_to_numpy=True, normalize_embeddings=True)
        vec = vec.astype("float32")

        scores, indices = _faiss_index.search(vec, 1)
        score = float(scores[0][0])
        idx   = int(indices[0][0])

        if score >= threshold and 0 <= idx < len(_faiss_examples):
            return _faiss_examples[idx], score
        return None, score

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger().debug(f"FAISS find_nearest: {e}")
        return None, 0.0


def get_training_dataset_stats() -> dict:
	"""Return summary statistics about the training dataset."""
	try:
		if not frappe.db.exists("DocType", "GenBI Training Example"):
			return {"total": 0, "approved": 0, "by_quality": {}, "by_source": {}}

		total = frappe.db.count("GenBI Training Example")
		approved = frappe.db.count("GenBI Training Example", {"is_approved": 1})

		quality_rows = frappe.db.sql("""
			SELECT quality, COUNT(*) AS cnt
			FROM `tabGenBI Training Example`
			WHERE is_approved = 1
			GROUP BY quality
		""", as_dict=True)

		source_rows = frappe.db.sql("""
			SELECT source_type, COUNT(*) AS cnt
			FROM `tabGenBI Training Example`
			GROUP BY source_type
		""", as_dict=True)

		return {
			"total": total,
			"approved": approved,
			"by_quality": {r.quality: r.cnt for r in quality_rows},
			"by_source": {r.source_type: r.cnt for r in source_rows},
		}
	except Exception:
		return {"total": 0, "approved": 0, "by_quality": {}, "by_source": {}}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/patches/v1_0/install_neural_dependencies.py
# Copyright (c) 2026, Thunder BluePhoenix and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt

import importlib.util
import subprocess
import sys

import frappe

# (module_to_check, pip_install_spec, required)
# required=False → log warning on failure instead of raising
NEURAL_PACKAGES = [
	("torch",          "torch>=2.0",          True),
	("transformers",   "transformers>=4.30",  True),
	("onnxruntime",    "onnxruntime>=1.16",   True),
	("faiss",          "faiss-cpu>=1.7",      True),
	("sklearn",        "scikit-learn>=1.3",   True),
	("numpy",          "numpy>=1.24",         True),
	("pandas",         "pandas>=2.0",         True),
	("scipy",          "scipy>=1.11",         True),
	("sentence_transformers", "sentence-transformers>=2.2", True),
	("spacy",          "spacy>=3.7",          True),
	("optimum",        "optimum>=1.14",       False),
	("rapidfuzz",      "RapidFuzz>=3.0",      True),
	("pdfplumber",     "pdfplumber>=0.10",    False),
	("networkx",       "networkx>=3.0",       True),
	("mlxtend",        "mlxtend>=0.23",       False),
	("polars",         "polars>=0.20",        True),
]


def execute():
	"""Check and pip-install all Neural Engine Python dependencies before migration."""
	missing = [
		(mod, spec, req)
		for mod, spec, req in NEURAL_PACKAGES
		if importlib.util.find_spec(mod) is None
	]

	if not missing:
		frappe.logger().info("f_genbi: all neural dependencies already installed")
		return

	frappe.logger().info(
		f"f_genbi: installing missing neural dependencies: "
		f"{[spec for _, spec, _ in missing]}"
	)

	failed_required = []

	for mod, spec, required in missing:
		try:
			frappe.logger().info(f"f_genbi: pip install {spec}")
			subprocess.check_call(
				[sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", spec],
				stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
				stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
			)
			frappe.logger().info(f"f_genbi: installed {spec} ✓")
		except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
			if required:
				frappe.logger().error(f"f_genbi: FAILED to install {spec}: {e}")
				failed_required.append(spec)
			else:
				frappe.logger().warning(f"f_genbi: optional package {spec} failed — skipping")

	if failed_required:
		frappe.throw(
			"f_genbi migration: failed to install required neural dependencies:\n"
			+ "\n".join(f"  • {p}" for p in failed_required)
			+ "\n\nRun manually:  pip install "
			+ " ".join(f'"{p}"' for p in failed_required)
		)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/patches/v1_0/install_npm_dependencies.py
# Copyright (c) 2026, Thunder BluePhoenix and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt

import os
import shutil
import subprocess

import frappe

REQUIRED_PACKAGES = [
	"handsontable",
	"hyperformula",
	"papaparse",
	"@simonwep/pickr",
	"numfmt",
]


def execute():
	"""Ensure all required npm packages for f_genbi are installed before migration."""
	app_path = frappe.get_app_path("f_genbi")
	app_root = os.path.dirname(app_path)  # .../apps/f_genbi
	node_modules = os.path.join(app_root, "node_modules")

	missing = _get_missing_packages(node_modules)

	if not missing:
		frappe.logger().info("f_genbi: all npm packages already installed, skipping yarn install")
		return

	frappe.logger().info(f"f_genbi: missing npm packages: {missing} — running yarn install")

	yarn_bin = shutil.which("yarn") or shutil.which("npm")
	if not yarn_bin:
		frappe.logger().warning(
			"f_genbi: neither yarn nor npm found in PATH — skipping npm install. "
			"Run `yarn install` manually inside apps/f_genbi/ before building assets."
		)
		return

	cmd = [yarn_bin, "install", "--non-interactive"] if "yarn" in yarn_bin else [yarn_bin, "install"]

	result = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=app_root, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)

	if result.returncode != 0:
		frappe.logger().error(
			f"f_genbi: npm install failed (exit {result.returncode}):\n{result.stderr}"
		)
		frappe.throw(
			f"Failed to install f_genbi npm dependencies. "
			f"Run `yarn install` manually inside apps/f_genbi/ and retry.\n\n{result.stderr}"
		)

	frappe.logger().info(f"f_genbi: npm packages installed successfully.\n{result.stdout}")


def _get_missing_packages(node_modules: str) -> list[str]:
	missing = []
	for pkg in REQUIRED_PACKAGES:
		# scoped packages like @simonwep/pickr live at node_modules/@simonwep/pickr
		pkg_path = os.path.join(node_modules, *pkg.split("/"))
		if not os.path.isdir(pkg_path):
			missing.append(pkg)
	return missing

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/f_genbi/www/canvas/index.py
import frappe

def get_context(context):
	"""Handle /app/canvas-session/{session_id} route"""
	# Get session_id from URL path
	path_parts = frappe.request.path.strip('/').split('/')
	session_id = path_parts[-1] if len(path_parts) > 0 else None

	if not session_id or session_id == 'canvas-session':
		frappe.throw("Session ID required")

	# Validate session exists and user has access
	try:
		doc = frappe.get_doc("Canvas Session", session_id)

		# Check permission
		if not doc.has_permission("read"):
			frappe.throw("You do not have permission to access this session")

		context.session_id = doc.session_id
		context.session_name = doc.name
		context.session_title = doc.title
		context.base_doctype = doc.base_doctype
		context.no_cache = 1

	except frappe.DoesNotExistError:
		frappe.throw(f"Canvas Session {session_id} not found")

	return context

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/scripts/train_slot_filler.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/train_slot_filler.py — Offline slot-filler training script.

Fine-tunes prajjwal1/bert-tiny on the slot-labeled CSV exported from
GenBI Training Examples, then exports to ONNX + INT8 quantises.

Usage
-----
# 1. Export labeled CSV from a running site:
bench execute f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.export_slot_training_data \
  > sites/default/neural_models/slot_training.csv

# 2. Train (on developer machine — not needed on server):
python scripts/train_slot_filler.py \
  --data   sites/default/neural_models/slot_training.csv \
  --output sites/default/neural_models/slot_filler.onnx \
  --epochs 10

# 3. The resulting slot_filler.onnx is ready for NeuralSlotFiller inference.
#    Place it at sites/{site}/neural_models/slot_filler.onnx on the target server.

Requirements (offline dev machine only — NOT needed on production server):
  pip install transformers datasets scikit-learn optimum[onnxruntime] torch
"""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import os
import sys

# ── Slot label schemas ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

INTENT_LABELS   = ["LIST", "AGGREGATE", "TOP_N", "TREND", "COUNT", "COMPARE", "LOOKUP"]
AGG_LABELS      = ["NONE", "SUM", "AVG", "COUNT", "MAX", "MIN"]
DATE_LABELS     = [
    "NONE", "today", "yesterday", "this_week", "last_week",
    "this_month", "last_month", "this_quarter", "last_quarter",
    "this_year", "last_year", "ytd", "last_7_days", "last_30_days", "last_90_days",
]

SLOT_HEADS = {
    "intent":        INTENT_LABELS,
    "agg_type":      AGG_LABELS,
    "date_ref":      DATE_LABELS,
    "filter_status": ["NONE", "draft", "submitted", "cancelled"],
}


# ── Data loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def load_csv(path: str) -> list[dict]:
    rows = []
    with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for row in csv.DictReader(f):
            if row.get("question"):
                rows.append(row)
    print(f"Loaded {len(rows)} rows from {path}")
    return rows


def encode_labels(rows: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Convert string labels to integer indices for each slot head."""
    encoded = {head: [] for head in SLOT_HEADS}
    for row in rows:
        for head, vocab in SLOT_HEADS.items():
            val = (row.get(head) or "NONE").strip()
            if val not in vocab:
                val = "NONE"
            encoded[head].append(vocab.index(val))
    return encoded


# ── Training ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def train(data_path: str, output_path: str, model_name: str, epochs: int, batch_size: int):
    try:
        import torch
        import torch.nn as nn
        from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
        from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModel
        from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
    except ImportError as e:
        print(f"ERROR: missing dependency — {e}")
        print("Install: pip install transformers torch scikit-learn optimum[onnxruntime]")
        sys.exit(1)

    rows = load_csv(data_path)
    if len(rows) < 10:
        print("ERROR: need at least 10 labeled rows. Run export_slot_training_data first.")
        sys.exit(1)

    labels = encode_labels(rows)
    questions = [r["question"] for r in rows]

    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)

    class SlotDataset(Dataset):
        def __init__(self, questions, labels, indices):
            self.questions = [questions[i] for i in indices]
            self.labels = {head: [vals[i] for i in indices] for head, vals in labels.items()}

        def __len__(self):
            return len(self.questions)

        def __getitem__(self, idx):
            enc = tokenizer(
                self.questions[idx],
                max_length=128,
                padding="max_length",
                truncation=True,
                return_tensors="pt",
            )
            item = {k: v.squeeze(0) for k, v in enc.items()}
            for head in SLOT_HEADS:
                item[head] = torch.tensor(self.labels[head][idx], dtype=torch.long)
            return item

    class SlotFillerModel(nn.Module):
        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__()
            self.encoder = AutoModel.from_pretrained(model_name)
            hidden = self.encoder.config.hidden_size
            self.heads = nn.ModuleDict({
                head: nn.Linear(hidden, len(vocab))
                for head, vocab in SLOT_HEADS.items()
            })

        def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask, token_type_ids=None):
            outputs = self.encoder(
                input_ids=input_ids,
                attention_mask=attention_mask,
                token_type_ids=token_type_ids,
            )
            cls = outputs.last_hidden_state[:, 0, :]  # [CLS] token
            return {head: self.heads[head](cls) for head in SLOT_HEADS}

    # Train / val split
    indices = list(range(len(rows)))
    train_idx, val_idx = train_test_split(indices, test_size=0.15, random_state=42)
    train_ds = SlotDataset(questions, labels, train_idx)
    val_ds   = SlotDataset(questions, labels, val_idx)

    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)
    val_loader   = DataLoader(val_ds,   batch_size=batch_size)

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Training on {device}, model={model_name}, epochs={epochs}, batch={batch_size}")

    model = SlotFillerModel().to(device)
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-5, weight_decay=0.01)
    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

    best_val_acc = 0.0
    best_state = None

    for epoch in range(epochs):
        # ── train ──
        model.train()
        total_loss = 0.0
        for batch in train_loader:
            input_ids      = batch["input_ids"].to(device)
            attention_mask = batch["attention_mask"].to(device)
            token_type_ids = batch.get("token_type_ids")
            if token_type_ids is not None:
                token_type_ids = token_type_ids.to(device)

            logits = model(input_ids, attention_mask, token_type_ids)
            loss = sum(
                criterion(logits[head], batch[head].to(device))
                for head in SLOT_HEADS
            )
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
            optimizer.step()
            total_loss += loss.item()

        # ── validate ──
        model.eval()
        correct = {head: 0 for head in SLOT_HEADS}
        total   = 0
        with torch.no_grad():
            for batch in val_loader:
                input_ids      = batch["input_ids"].to(device)
                attention_mask = batch["attention_mask"].to(device)
                token_type_ids = batch.get("token_type_ids")
                if token_type_ids is not None:
                    token_type_ids = token_type_ids.to(device)
                logits = model(input_ids, attention_mask, token_type_ids)
                bs = input_ids.shape[0]
                total += bs
                for head in SLOT_HEADS:
                    preds = logits[head].argmax(dim=-1)
                    correct[head] += (preds == batch[head].to(device)).sum().item()

        accs = {head: correct[head] / total for head in SLOT_HEADS}
        avg_acc = sum(accs.values()) / len(accs)
        print(
            f"Epoch {epoch+1}/{epochs}  loss={total_loss/len(train_loader):.4f}  "
            + "  ".join(f"{h}={v:.2%}" for h, v in accs.items())
            + f"  avg={avg_acc:.2%}"
        )

        if avg_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = avg_acc
            best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}

    print(f"\nBest avg val accuracy: {best_val_acc:.2%}")
    if best_state:
        model.load_state_dict(best_state)

    # ── ONNX export ──
    _export_onnx(model, tokenizer, output_path, device)
    _save_metadata(output_path, model_name, len(rows), best_val_acc)
    print(f"\nDone — model saved to {output_path}")


def _export_onnx(model, tokenizer, output_path: str, device):
    import torch

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(output_path)), exist_ok=True)
    model.eval()

    dummy = tokenizer(
        "show total sales this month",
        max_length=128, padding="max_length",
        truncation=True, return_tensors="pt",
    )
    input_ids      = dummy["input_ids"].to(device)
    attention_mask = dummy["attention_mask"].to(device)
    token_type_ids = dummy.get("token_type_ids")
    if token_type_ids is not None:
        token_type_ids = token_type_ids.to(device)
    else:
        token_type_ids = torch.zeros_like(input_ids)

    tmp_path = output_path.replace(".onnx", "_fp32.onnx")

    torch.onnx.export(
        model,
        (input_ids, attention_mask, token_type_ids),
        tmp_path,
        input_names=["input_ids", "attention_mask", "token_type_ids"],
        output_names=list(SLOT_HEADS.keys()),
        dynamic_axes={
            "input_ids":      {0: "batch"},
            "attention_mask": {0: "batch"},
            "token_type_ids": {0: "batch"},
        },
        opset_version=14,
    )
    print(f"FP32 ONNX exported → {tmp_path}")

    # INT8 quantisation (reduces size ~4x, minimal accuracy loss)
    try:
        from onnxruntime.quantization import quantize_dynamic, QuantType
        quantize_dynamic(tmp_path, output_path, weight_type=QuantType.QInt8)
        os.remove(tmp_path)
        print(f"INT8 quantised → {output_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Quantisation failed ({e}) — using FP32 model")
        os.rename(tmp_path, output_path)


def _save_metadata(onnx_path: str, model_name: str, n_examples: int, accuracy: float):
    meta = {
        "base_model":  model_name,
        "n_examples":  n_examples,
        "accuracy":    round(accuracy, 4),
        "slot_heads":  {head: vocab for head, vocab in SLOT_HEADS.items()},
        "max_length":  128,
    }
    meta_path = onnx_path.replace(".onnx", "_metadata.json")
    with open(meta_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(meta, f, indent=2)
    print(f"Metadata → {meta_path}")


# ── CLI ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Train GenBI slot filler (bert-tiny → ONNX)")
    parser.add_argument("--data",       required=True,  help="Path to slot_training.csv")
    parser.add_argument("--output",     required=True,  help="Output path for slot_filler.onnx")
    parser.add_argument("--model",      default="prajjwal1/bert-tiny", help="HuggingFace model name")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs",     type=int, default=10)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    train(
        data_path=args.data,
        output_path=args.output,
        model_name=args.model,
        epochs=args.epochs,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/f_genbi/scripts/train_t5_genbi.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/train_t5_genbi.py — Offline T5-small seq2seq training.

Fine-tunes t5-small on (question → SQL) pairs from GenBI Training Examples,
then exports to ONNX (encoder + decoder separately, as required by seq2seq ONNX).

Usage
-----
# 1. Export training JSONL from a running site:
bench execute f_genbi.f_genbi.api.query_engine.export_training_dataset \
  --args '["raw"]' > sites/default/neural_models/t5_training.jsonl

# 2. Train (developer machine):
python scripts/train_t5_genbi.py \
  --data   sites/default/neural_models/t5_training.jsonl \
  --output sites/default/neural_models/t5_genbi \
  --epochs 5

# 3. Copy the t5_genbi/ directory to sites/{site}/neural_models/t5_genbi/ on the server.

Requirements (dev machine only):
  pip install transformers datasets torch optimum[onnxruntime]
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys


def load_jsonl(path: str) -> list[dict]:
    rows = []
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.strip()
            if line:
                try:
                    rows.append(json.loads(line))
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    pass
    print(f"Loaded {len(rows)} examples from {path}")
    return rows


def train(data_path: str, output_dir: str, model_name: str, epochs: int,
          batch_size: int, max_source: int, max_target: int):
    try:
        import torch
        from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
        from transformers import T5ForConditionalGeneration, T5TokenizerFast
        from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
    except ImportError as e:
        print(f"ERROR: missing dependency — {e}")
        print("Install: pip install transformers torch scikit-learn optimum[onnxruntime]")
        sys.exit(1)

    rows = load_jsonl(data_path)
    if len(rows) < 10:
        print("ERROR: need at least 10 examples. Run export_training_dataset first.")
        sys.exit(1)

    pairs = [(r["question"], r["sql"]) for r in rows
             if r.get("question") and r.get("sql")]
    if not pairs:
        print("ERROR: no valid (question, sql) pairs found in JSONL.")
        sys.exit(1)

    tokenizer = T5TokenizerFast.from_pretrained(model_name)

    class Seq2SeqDataset(Dataset):
        def __init__(self, pairs):
            self.pairs = pairs

        def __len__(self):
            return len(self.pairs)

        def __getitem__(self, idx):
            q, sql = self.pairs[idx]
            src = tokenizer(
                f"translate English to SQL: {q}",
                max_length=max_source,
                padding="max_length",
                truncation=True,
                return_tensors="pt",
            )
            tgt = tokenizer(
                sql,
                max_length=max_target,
                padding="max_length",
                truncation=True,
                return_tensors="pt",
            )
            labels = tgt["input_ids"].squeeze()
            # T5 uses -100 for padding tokens in loss
            labels[labels == tokenizer.pad_token_id] = -100
            return {
                "input_ids":      src["input_ids"].squeeze(),
                "attention_mask": src["attention_mask"].squeeze(),
                "labels":         labels,
            }

    train_pairs, val_pairs = train_test_split(pairs, test_size=0.10, random_state=42)
    train_ds = Seq2SeqDataset(train_pairs)
    val_ds   = Seq2SeqDataset(val_pairs)
    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)
    val_loader   = DataLoader(val_ds,   batch_size=batch_size)

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Training on {device}, model={model_name}, epochs={epochs}, n={len(pairs)}")

    model = T5ForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(model_name).to(device)
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=0.01)

    best_val_loss = float("inf")
    best_state = None

    for epoch in range(epochs):
        model.train()
        total_loss = 0.0
        for batch in train_loader:
            input_ids      = batch["input_ids"].to(device)
            attention_mask = batch["attention_mask"].to(device)
            labels         = batch["labels"].to(device)
            out = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask, labels=labels)
            loss = out.loss
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            import torch.nn as nn
            nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
            optimizer.step()
            total_loss += loss.item()

        model.eval()
        val_loss = 0.0
        with torch.no_grad():
            for batch in val_loader:
                out = model(
                    input_ids=batch["input_ids"].to(device),
                    attention_mask=batch["attention_mask"].to(device),
                    labels=batch["labels"].to(device),
                )
                val_loss += out.loss.item()
        val_loss /= max(len(val_loader), 1)

        print(f"Epoch {epoch+1}/{epochs}  train_loss={total_loss/len(train_loader):.4f}  val_loss={val_loss:.4f}")

        if val_loss < best_val_loss:
            best_val_loss = val_loss
            best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}

    if best_state:
        model.load_state_dict(best_state)
    print(f"\nBest val loss: {best_val_loss:.4f}")

    # Save HuggingFace model + tokenizer (for ONNX export and local inference)
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    model.cpu().save_pretrained(output_dir)
    tokenizer.save_pretrained(output_dir)
    print(f"HF model saved → {output_dir}")

    # ONNX export via optimum
    _export_onnx(output_dir, max_source, max_target)
    _save_metadata(output_dir, model_name, len(pairs), best_val_loss)
    print(f"\nDone — T5 model saved to {output_dir}/")


def _export_onnx(model_dir: str, max_source: int, max_target: int):
    try:
        from optimum.onnxruntime import ORTModelForSeq2SeqLM
        print("Exporting to ONNX via optimum…")
        ort_model = ORTModelForSeq2SeqLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, export=True)
        ort_model.save_pretrained(model_dir)
        print(f"ONNX model saved → {model_dir}/")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"ONNX export failed ({e}) — HF PyTorch model saved, ONNX skipped.")
        print("Install: pip install optimum[onnxruntime]")


def _save_metadata(model_dir: str, model_name: str, n_examples: int, val_loss: float):
    import json as _json
    meta = {
        "base_model":  model_name,
        "n_examples":  n_examples,
        "val_loss":    round(val_loss, 4),
        "type":        "seq2seq",
    }
    with open(os.path.join(model_dir, "genbi_metadata.json"), "w") as f:
        _json.dump(meta, f, indent=2)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Train GenBI T5-small seq2seq (→ ONNX)")
    parser.add_argument("--data",        required=True, help="Path to t5_training.jsonl")
    parser.add_argument("--output",      required=True, help="Output directory for model files")
    parser.add_argument("--model",       default="t5-small", help="HuggingFace model name")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs",      type=int, default=5)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size",  type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--max-source",  type=int, default=128)
    parser.add_argument("--max-target",  type=int, default=256)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    train(
        data_path=args.data,
        output_dir=args.output,
        model_name=args.model,
        epochs=args.epochs,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        max_source=args.max_source,
        max_target=args.max_target,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
